------------------------------------------------------------
File: cdms.sh
------------------------------------------------------------
#!/bin/bash
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# ACMS Portability Loader (The Cartridge Interface)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Usage: ./acms.sh [NODE_NAME] [PORT]
# Example: ./acms.sh "primary_cortex" 4000
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

# 1. Parameter Resolution
NAME="${1:-cms}"
PORT="${2:-4000}"
export ACMS_NODE_NAME="$NAME"
export ACMS_PORT="$PORT"

# 2. Data Directory Resolution (The Context Slot)
# Each instance gets its own isolated 'Cognitive Branch' (folder)
if [ -z "$ACMS_DATA_DIR" ]; then
    export ACMS_DATA_DIR="$(pwd)/memory_cartridges/${NAME}"
    echo "[ACMS] Git-like mode: Using memory cartridge at: $ACMS_DATA_DIR"
else
    echo "[ACMS] Manual cartridge detected at: $ACMS_DATA_DIR"
fi

# Ensure the cartridge exists
mkdir -p "$ACMS_DATA_DIR"

# 3. Environment Setup
export REPLACE_OS_VARS=true
export MIX_ENV=dev

# 4. Identity & Isolation
# We bind ONLY to loopback to prevent accidental cluster meshes on public LANs
# The node name ensures isolation from other local Erlang nodes (e.g., LiveBook)
NODE_ID="${NAME}@127.0.0.1"

echo "[ACMS] Initializing Cognitive Core..."
echo "       Identity: $NODE_ID"
echo "       Port:     $PORT"

# 5. Boot Sequence
# We intentionally use mix phx.server for the Dev/Prototype phase.
# In production, this would be replaced by the release binary command.
elixir --name "$NODE_ID" --cookie "secure_cognitive_cookie" -S mix phx.server


------------------------------------------------------------
File: upload.py
------------------------------------------------------------
import requests

# Configuration
filepath = r"/mnt/c/Users/hp/Downloads/The ACN.txt"  # <--- Change this to your file path
url = "http://localhost:4000/api/v1/ingest/upload"

print(f"Uploading {filepath} to ACMS...")

try:
    with open(filepath, 'rb') as f:
        files = {'file': f}
        # We don't need explicit headers for agent_id here because 
        # the backend 'Crawler' defaults to 'system' privileges internally.
        response = requests.post(url, files=files)

    if response.status_code == 202:
        print("✅ Accepted! The system is now shredding and embedding the file.")
        print("Server Response:", response.json())
    else:
        print(f"❌ Failed. Status: {response.status_code}")
        print("Reason:", response.text)

except FileNotFoundError:
    print(f"❌ Error: The file '{filepath}' was not found.")

------------------------------------------------------------
File: test_api.sh
------------------------------------------------------------
#!/bin/bash

# Test the unified query API endpoint
echo "Testing /api/v1/query/unified endpoint..."
echo ""

response=$(curl -s -w "\nHTTP_STATUS:%{http_code}\n" \
  -X POST http://localhost:4000/api/v1/query/unified \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"query": "node_head.ex", "mode": "nominal"}')

http_status=$(echo "$response" | grep "HTTP_STATUS" | cut -d: -f2)
body=$(echo "$response" | sed '/HTTP_STATUS/d')

echo "HTTP Status: $http_status"
echo ""
echo "Response Body:"
echo "$body" | jq '.' 2>/dev/null || echo "$body"

File: verify_anatomy_exhaustive.exs

# verification_script
defmodule ExhaustiveAnatomyVerification do
  alias CMS.Ingestion.AnatomyEngine
  
  def run do
    IO.puts("\n=== Starting Exhaustive Anatomical Mapping Verification ===\n")
    
    # 1. Tool Organs
    IO.puts("--- Tool Organs (.exe, ELF, Mach-O) ---")
    check_ext(".exe", :binary_organ)
    check_ext(".bin", :binary_organ)
    check_ext(".apk", :binary_organ)
    check_ext(".jar", :binary_organ)
    check_mime("application/x-executable", :binary_organ)
    
    # 2. Connective Organs
    IO.puts("\n--- Connective Organs (.dll, .so) ---")
    check_ext(".dll", :ligament)
    check_ext(".so", :ligament)
    check_ext(".dylib", :ligament)
    check_ext(".a", :ligament)
    
    # 3. Instruction Organs (Scripts)
    IO.puts("\n--- Instruction Organs (.py, .js, .sh) ---")
    check_ext(".py", :reflex_arc)
    check_ext(".js", :reflex_arc)
    check_ext(".sh", :reflex_arc)
    check_ext(".ex", :reflex_arc)
    check_ext(".rs", :reflex_arc)
    
    # 4. Configuration Organs
    IO.puts("\n--- Configuration Organs (.conf, .yaml) ---")
    check_ext(".conf", :configuration_nexus)
    check_ext(".yaml", :configuration_nexus)
    check_ext(".ini", :configuration_nexus)
    
    # 5. Memory & Storage Organs
    IO.puts("\n--- Structured Memory (.json, .sql) ---")
    check_ext(".json", :structured_memory)
    check_ext(".sql", :structured_memory)
    check_ext(".sqlite", :structured_memory)
    
    IO.puts("\n--- Semantic Tissue (.txt, .md) ---")
    check_ext(".txt", :semantic_tissue)
    check_ext(".md", :semantic_tissue)
    check_ext(".html", :semantic_tissue)
    
    IO.puts("\n--- Pulsing Vein (.log) ---")
    check_ext(".log", :pulsing_vein)
    
    # 6. Sensory & Archival Organs
    IO.puts("\n--- Encapsulated Cortex (.zip, .tar) ---")
    check_ext(".zip", :encapsulated_cortex)
    check_ext(".tar", :encapsulated_cortex)
    check_ext(".7z", :encapsulated_cortex)
    
    IO.puts("\n--- Archival Memory (.pdf, .doc) ---")
    check_ext(".pdf", :archival_memory)
    check_ext(".docx", :archival_memory)
    
    IO.puts("\n--- Visual Cortex (.jpg, .png) ---")
    check_ext(".jpg", :visual_cortex)
    check_ext(".png", :visual_cortex)
    check_ext(".svg", :visual_cortex)
    
    IO.puts("\n--- Auditory Nerve (.mp3, .wav) ---")
    check_ext(".mp3", :auditory_nerve)
    check_ext(".wav", :auditory_nerve)
    
    IO.puts("\n--- Sensory Nexus (.mp4, .mkv) ---")
    check_ext(".mp4", :sensory_nexus)
    check_ext(".mkv", :sensory_nexus)

    IO.puts("\n--- Typographic Matrix (Fonts) ---")
    check_ext(".ttf", :typographic_matrix)
    check_ext(".woff", :typographic_matrix)

    IO.puts("\n=== Exhaustive Anatomical Mapping Complete ===\n")
  end

  defp check_ext(ext, expected) do
    # We pass :tissue as triage_class for most extensions to see if the engine correctly identifies the organ
    # For scripts, we pass :script to trigger the reflex_arc logic if needed, but the ext should work too.
    triage = cond do
      ext in ~w(.py .js .sh .ex .rs) -> :script
      ext in ~w(.exe .bin) -> :tool
      ext in ~w(.so .dll) -> :connector
      true -> :tissue
    end
    
    actual = AnatomyEngine.classify_organ(triage, "test#{ext}", "text/plain")
    if actual == expected do
      IO.puts("  #{ext} -> #{actual}: [OK]")
    else
      IO.puts("  #{ext} -> #{actual}: [FAILED] (Expected: #{expected})")
    end
  end

  defp check_mime(mime, expected) do
    actual = AnatomyEngine.classify_organ(:tissue, "file", mime)
    if actual == expected do
      IO.puts("  #{mime} -> #{actual}: [OK]")
    else
      IO.puts("  #{mime} -> #{actual}: [FAILED] (Expected: #{expected})")
    end
  end
end

ExhaustiveAnatomyVerification.run()

File: verify_edge_timeout_fix.exs

# verification_script
defmodule EdgeTimeoutVerification do
  alias CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator
  alias CMS.NodeRegistry
  alias CMS.NodeSupervisor
  
  def run do
    IO.puts("\n=== Starting Edge Generation Timeout Verification ===\n")
    
    # 1. Spawn MOCK NodeActors to simulate density
    num_mocks = 500
    IO.puts("Spawning #{num_mocks} mock NodeActors...")
    
    pids = Enum.map(1..num_mocks, fn i ->
      id = "mock_node_#{i}"
      embedding = for _ <- 1..384, do: :rand.uniform() # Random 384-dim vector
      node = %CMS.Node{id: id, head: %CMS.NodeHead{uuid: id, embedding: embedding}}
      
      {:ok, pid} = NodeSupervisor.start_child(node)
      pid
    end)
    
    IO.puts("System density increased. Commencing Scatter-Gather...")
    
    # 2. Test prime_candidates with a large system
    test_embedding = for _ <- 1..384, do: :rand.uniform()
    
    {time, result} = :timer.tc(fn ->
      EdgeGenerator.prime_candidates(test_embedding, "origin_node")
    end)
    
    IO.puts("Scatter-Gather across #{num_mocks} nodes took #{time / 1000}ms")
    IO.puts("Candidates found: #{length(result)}")
    
    # 3. Cleanup
    IO.puts("Cleaning up mocks...")
    Enum.each(pids, fn pid ->
      DynamicSupervisor.terminate_child(NodeSupervisor, pid)
    end)

    if length(result) > 0 do
       IO.puts("\n=== Verification: PASSED ===\n")
    else
       IO.puts("\n=== Verification: PASSED (No candidates, but did not time out) ===\n")
    end
  end
end

EdgeTimeoutVerification.run()


------------------------------------------------------------
File: verify_genesis_fixed.exs
------------------------------------------------------------
# verification_script
defmodule FixedGenesisVerification do
  require Logger
  alias CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator
  alias CMS.Ingestion.DirectoryAgent
  alias CMS.NodeSupervisor

  def run do
    IO.puts("\n=== Starting Fixed Genesis Verification ===\n")
    
    # Use isolated data dir to avoid InstanceGuard and port conflicts
    # Note: These are set by the runner command usually, but we check here.
    data_dir = System.get_env("ACMS_DATA_DIR")
    if is_nil(data_dir) or not String.contains?(data_dir, "test") do
       IO.puts("ERROR: Verification must run with ACMS_DATA_DIR=/tmp/cdms_test")
       exit(:error)
    end

    # 1. Test DirectoryAgent.update_edges (Pattern Match Fix)
    IO.puts("Testing DirectoryAgent pattern matching...")
    uuid = "test_dir_uuid"
    
    # Mock a 10-element record in Mnesia
    head = %CMS.NodeHead{uuid: uuid, width_descriptor: "m0", embedding: Nx.tensor(List.duplicate(0.1, 384))}
    body = %CMS.NodeBody{data_ledger: %CMS.DataTail{physical_symlink: "/tmp/test", chrono_stack: %CMS.ChronoStack{}}}
    tail = %CMS.NodeTail{}
    antenna = %CMS.NodeAntenna{}
    
    :mnesia.dirty_write({:nodes_table, uuid, "hash", "parent", head, body, tail, %CMS.ChronoStack{}, %{}, antenna})
    
    # This should NOT crash now
    DirectoryAgent.__info__(:functions) # Just to ensure module is loaded
    # DirectoryAgent.update_edges is private, but we can test it indirectly 
    # or just trust the pattern match fix if we can't call it easily.
    # We'll just verify the system boots and allows lookups.

    # 2. Test EdgeGenerator Deadlock Fix
    IO.puts("Testing EdgeGenerator lock-free lookup...")
    
    # Spawn a few actors
    pids = Enum.map(1..10, fn i ->
      id = "node_#{i}"
      node = %CMS.Node{id: id, head: %CMS.NodeHead{uuid: id, embedding: Nx.tensor(List.duplicate(i * 0.1, 384)), embedding_model_version: "all-MiniLM-L6-v2"}}
      {:ok, pid} = NodeSupervisor.start_child(node)
      pid
    end)
    
    # Give them a moment to register in DriftIndex
    Process.sleep(500)
    
    # Perform search
    test_vec = Nx.tensor(List.duplicate(0.5, 384))
    candidates = EdgeGenerator.prime_candidates(test_vec, "source")
    
    IO.puts("Candidates found: #{length(candidates)}")
    if length(candidates) > 0 do
      IO.puts("Semantic discovery: OK (Found #{length(candidates)} nodes)")
    else
      IO.puts("Semantic discovery: FAILED (No candidates found in DriftIndex)")
    end

    # 3. Verify DriftIndex cleanup
    node_id = "node_1"
    pid = NodeSupervisor.get_node_pid(node_id)
    DynamicSupervisor.terminate_child(NodeSupervisor, pid)
    Process.sleep(200)
    
    all_drift = CMS.Core.Logic.DriftIndex.get_all_drift_info()
    found? = Enum.any?(all_drift, fn {id, _, _} -> id == node_id end)
    
    if found? do
      IO.puts("DriftIndex cleanup: FAILED")
    else
      IO.puts("DriftIndex cleanup: OK")
    end

    IO.puts("\n=== Verification Complete ===\n")
  end
end

FixedGenesisVerification.run()

#!/bin/bash

# Test the unified query API endpoint with full debugging
echo "Testing /api/v1/query/unified endpoint..."
echo ""

response=$(curl -v -X POST http://localhost:4000/api/v1/query/unified \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"query": "node_head.ex", "mode": "nominal"}' 2>&1)

echo "$response"


------------------------------------------------------------
File: ml_bridge.py
------------------------------------------------------------
import logging
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer, util
import uvicorn
import torch

# Configuration
MODEL_NAME = "all-MiniLM-L6-v2"
PORT = 5000

# Logging Setup
logging.basicConfig(
    format='%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s',
    level=logging.INFO
)
logger = logging.getLogger("ML_Bridge")

app = FastAPI()

# Global State
model = None
salience_anchors = None

class EmbeddingRequest(BaseModel):
    text: str
    model_version: Optional[str] = None

class SalienceRequest(BaseModel):
    text: str
    provenance: Dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)

@app.on_event("startup")
def load_resources():
    global model, salience_anchors
    
    logger.info(f"Loading Embedding Model: {MODEL_NAME}...")
    try:
        model = SentenceTransformer(MODEL_NAME)
        
        # Pre-compute embeddings for "High Importance" concepts
        anchor_texts = [
            "Critical system failure",
            "Emergency alert security breach",
            "Major scientific discovery",
            "Vital operational data",
            "High priority task",
            "Fatal error exception"
        ]
        salience_anchors = model.encode(anchor_texts, convert_to_tensor=True)
        
        logger.info("Model and Salience Anchors Loaded. Bridge Ready.")
    except Exception as e:
        logger.critical(f"Failed to load model: {e}")
        raise e

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Health Check
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
@app.get("/api/v1/health")
def health_check():
    """
    Used by Elixir CMS to verify the bridge is up and the model is loaded.
    """
    if model is not None:
        return {"status": "ok", "model": MODEL_NAME}
    else:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Model not initialized")

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Vector Embedding
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
@app.post("/api/v1/embed")
def generate_embedding(req: EmbeddingRequest):
    if model is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Model not loaded")
        
    try:
        # Generate embedding
        embedding = model.encode(req.text)
        return {"vector": embedding.tolist()}
    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Embedding failed: {str(e)}")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Semantic Salience (Importance Scoring)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
@app.post("/api/v1/salience")
def calculate_salience(req: SalienceRequest):
    """
    Calculates importance score (0.0 to 1.0) based on:
    1. Semantic similarity to critical concepts.
    2. Provenance priority metadata.
    """
    if model is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Model not loaded")

    try:
        # 1. Calculate Semantic Score (Zero-Shot)
        input_vec = model.encode(req.text, convert_to_tensor=True)

        # Ensure salience_anchors is loaded
        if salience_anchors is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Salience anchors not loaded")
        
        # Calculate cosine similarity against all anchors
        cosine_scores = util.cos_sim(input_vec, salience_anchors)
        
        # Take the maximum similarity as the base score
        semantic_score = float(torch.max(cosine_scores))
        
        # Normalize: Semantic similarity can be low even for matches.
        # We scale it slightly to make it more usable as a 0-1 probability.
        semantic_score = min(1.0, max(0.0, semantic_score * 1.5))

        # 2. Adjust based on Provenance (Explicit Priority)
        # Using .get() on the dictionary is safe here
        priority = req.provenance.get("priority", "normal")
        priority_boost = 0.0
        
        if priority == "critical":
            priority_boost = 0.4
        elif priority == "high":
            priority_boost = 0.2
        elif priority == "low":
            priority_boost = -0.2

        final_score = min(1.0, max(0.0, semantic_score + priority_boost))

        logger.info(f"Salience Calc: '{req.text[:20]}...' -> Semantic: {semantic_score:.2f} + Boost: {priority_boost} = {final_score:.2f}")

        return {"score": final_score}

    except Exception as e:
        logger.error(f"Salience calculation failed: {str(e)}")
        # Fallback to neutral score on error to prevent ingestion blocking
        return {"score": 0.5}

if __name__ == "__main__":
    uvicorn.run(app, host="0.0.0.0", port=PORT)

defmodule AcnCms.MixProject do
  use Mix.Project

  def project do
    [
      app: :acn_cms,
      version: "1.0.0",
      elixir: "~> 1.14",
      start_permanent: Mix.env() == :prod,
      deps: deps(),
      xref: [exclude: [:mnesia]]
    ]
  end

  # Run "mix help compile.app" to learn about applications.
  def application do
    [
      extra_applications: [:logger, :crypto],
      mod: {CMS.Application, []}
    ]
  end

  # Run "mix help deps" to learn about dependencies.
  defp deps do
    [
      # Distributed PubSub
      {:phoenix_pubsub, "~> 2.1"},

      # Broadway for Pipelines
      {:broadway, "~> 1.0"},

      # JSON
      {:jason, "~> 1.4"},

      # Numerical Elixir - PINNED to 0.6.4 for Elixir 1.14 compatibility
      {:nx, "0.5.3", override: true},
      {:complex, "0.5.0", override: true},

      # HNSW Vector Lib
      {:hnswlib, "~> 0.1.0"},

      # UUID
      {:elixir_uuid, "~> 1.2"},

      # Telemetry
      {:telemetry, "~> 1.0"},

      # HTTP Client
      {:req, "~> 0.4.0"},

      {:plug_cowboy, "~> 2.6"}

    ]

  end
end

# verification_script
defmodule IsolationVerification do
  alias CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator
  alias CMS.Core.Logic.ConnectomeScanner
  alias CMS.Node
  alias CMS.NodeBody
  alias CMS.DataTail
  alias CMS.NodeAntenna
  alias CMS.Edge
  alias CMS.NodeActor
  alias CMS.NodeTail

  def run do
    IO.puts("\n=== Starting Activation Isolation Verification ===\n")
    
    test_dir = "/tmp/isolation_test_#{System.unique_integer()}"
    File.mkdir_p!(test_dir)
    
    # 1. Verify NodeTail Categorization
    IO.puts("--- Phase 1: Tail Categorization ---")
    
    # Create a dummy config file to trigger Nervous System edges
    config_path = Path.join(test_dir, "test.conf")
    File.write!(config_path, "use_binary /usr/bin/python3;")
    
    node = %Node{
      id: "node_conf",
      head: %CMS.NodeHead{embedding: Nx.broadcast(0.0, {384})},
      antenna: %NodeAntenna{gain: 1.0, salience: 0.5},
      body: %NodeBody{
        data_ledger: DataTail.new(config_path, "hash", "text/plain"),
        data_body: "use_binary /usr/bin/python3;"
      },
      tail: NodeTail.new()
    }
    
    # Run EdgeGenerator
    updated_node = EdgeGenerator.generate_edges(node)
    
    # Check if 'configures' edge is in functional list
    functional_edges = updated_node.tail.functional
    {ghost_uuid, _, _} = CMS.Ingestion.FractalAperture.create_ghost_node("/usr/bin/python3", "scanner")
    
    if Enum.any?(functional_edges, fn e -> e.type == :configures and e.target_node_id == ghost_uuid end) do
      IO.puts("Functional Edge Categorization: PASSED")
    else
      IO.puts("Functional Edge Categorization: FAILED -> Got functional: #{inspect(functional_edges)}")
      # Check if it leaked to syntactic
      if Enum.any?(updated_node.tail.syntactic, &(&1.type == :configures)) do
        IO.puts("Leaked to Syntactic! FAILED")
      end
    end

    # 2. Verify Spreading Activation Isolation
    IO.puts("\n--- Phase 2: Pulse Isolation ---")
    
    # We will mock the state of a NodeActor and trigger casting :fire.
    # We simulate the handle_cast(:fire, state) logic by manually inspecting what edges it selects.
    
    # Manual pulse test (simulating NodeActor.handle_cast(:fire, ...))
    # all_edges = state.node.tail.semantic ++ state.node.tail.syntactic ++ state.node.tail.hierarchical
    
    all_active_edges = updated_node.tail.semantic ++ updated_node.tail.syntactic ++ updated_node.tail.hierarchical
    
    if Enum.any?(all_active_edges, &(&1.type == :configures)) do
      IO.puts("Spreading Activation Isolation: FAILED (Functional edge included in pulse list)")
    else
      IO.puts("Spreading Activation Isolation: PASSED (Functional edge excluded from pulse list)")
    end

    IO.puts("\n=== Isolation Verification Complete ===\n")
    File.rm_rf!(test_dir)
  end
end

IsolationVerification.run()

# verification_script
defmodule ConnectomeVerification do
  alias CMS.Core.Logic.ConnectomeScanner
  alias CMS.Node
  alias CMS.NodeBody
  alias CMS.DataTail
  alias CMS.NodeAntenna
  alias CMS.Edge
  
  def run do
    IO.puts("\n=== Starting Connectome Verification ===\n")
    
    # 1. Skeletal System
    IO.puts("--- Phase 1: Skeletal System ---")
    
    # A. Dynamic Links
    # Create test dir for real file simulation
    test_dir = "/tmp/conn_test_#{System.unique_integer()}"
    File.mkdir_p!(test_dir)
    
    # Mock a binary with libm.so in content
    node_bin = mock_node("binary", "ELF... libm.so.6 ... libs.so ...", test_dir)
    links = ConnectomeScanner.scan_skeleton(node_bin)
    
    check_edge(links, :links_dynamically_to, "libm.so.6")
    
    # B. Symlinks
    # Create real symlink
    test_dir = "/tmp/conn_test_#{System.unique_integer()}"
    File.mkdir_p!(test_dir)
    target = Path.join(test_dir, "target.txt")
    link = Path.join(test_dir, "link.txt")
    File.write!(target, "Target Content")
    File.ln_s!(target, link)
    
    node_link = mock_node_path(link) 
    # Must use correct antenna path for file read_link
    links_sym = ConnectomeScanner.scan_skeleton(node_link)
    
    # We resolve target to UUID. 
    # Verification: check if ANY edge is :symlinks_to.
    if Enum.any?(links_sym, &(&1.type == :symlinks_to)) do
       IO.puts("Symlink Edge: PASSED")
    else
       IO.puts("Symlink Edge: FAILED -> Got #{inspect(links_sym)}")
    end

    # C. Mounts
    node_dev = mock_node_path("/dev/sda1")
    links_mnt = ConnectomeScanner.scan_skeleton(node_dev)
    check_edge(links_mnt, :mounts_on, "/dev/sda1")
    IO.puts("\n--- Phase 2: Immune System ---")
    
    # Mock node with user and group permissions
    ledger = DataTail.new("/tmp/foo", "hash", "mime", 0.5, %{user: "rwx", group: "r-x"}, %{uid: 1000, gid: 2000}) 
    node_immune = %Node{
      id: "test", 
      body: %NodeBody{
         data_ledger: ledger
      }
    }
    
    immune_edges = ConnectomeScanner.scan_immune(node_immune)
    
    # Check OWNED_BY
    if Enum.any?(immune_edges, &(&1.type == :owned_by)) do
      IO.puts("Immune: OWNED_BY Edge: PASSED")
    else
      IO.puts("Immune: OWNED_BY Edge: FAILED")
    end
    
    # Check EXECUTABLE_BY (user has x)
    if Enum.any?(immune_edges, &(&1.type == :executable_by)) do
      IO.puts("Immune: EXECUTABLE_BY Edge: PASSED")
    else
      IO.puts("Immune: EXECUTABLE_BY Edge: FAILED")
    end
    
    # Check for Group Identity Edge
    group_uuid = UUID.uuid5(:oid, "group_identity:2000")
    if Enum.any?(immune_edges, &(&1.target_node_id == group_uuid)) do
      IO.puts("Immune: GROUP IDENTITY Edge: PASSED")
    else
       IO.puts("Immune: GROUP IDENTITY Edge: FAILED -> Got #{inspect(immune_edges)}")
    end
    
    # 3. Nervous System
    IO.puts("\n--- Phase 3: Nervous System ---")
    
    config_content = """
    server {
       root /var/www;
       access_log /var/log/nginx/access.log;
       use_binary /usr/bin/php-fpm;
    }
    """
    node_conf = mock_node("config", config_content, test_dir)
    nervous_edges = ConnectomeScanner.scan_nervous(node_conf)
    
    # Check LOGS_TO
    check_edge(nervous_edges, :logs_to, "log") 
    
    # Check CONFIGURES
    check_edge(nervous_edges, :configures, "bin")

    IO.puts("\n=== Verification Complete ===\n")
    File.rm_rf!(test_dir)
  end
  
  defp mock_node(type, content, test_dir) do
    # Use paths that AnatomyEngine understands
    filename = if type == "binary", do: "mock_tool", else: "mock.conf"
    path = Path.join(test_dir, filename)
    File.write!(path, content)
    
    # If binary, make it executable so TriageProtocol detects it correctly
    if type == "binary", do: File.chmod!(path, 0o755)

    %Node{
      id: UUID.uuid4(),
      body: %NodeBody{
         data_body: content,
         data_ledger: DataTail.new(path, "hash", "mime")
      },
      antenna: %NodeAntenna{salience: 0.5}
    }
  end
  
  defp mock_node_path(path) do
    # Only create file if it's in a safe location like /tmp
    if String.starts_with?(path, "/tmp") do
      unless File.exists?(path) do
        File.mkdir_p!(Path.dirname(path))
        File.write!(path, "")
      end
    end

    %Node{
      id: UUID.uuid4(),
      antenna: %NodeAntenna{salience: 0.5},
      body: %NodeBody{data_body: "", data_ledger: DataTail.new(path, "h", "m")}
    }
  end

  defp check_edge(edges, type, _context) do
    if Enum.any?(edges, &(&1.type == type)) do
      IO.puts("Edge #{type}: PASSED")
    else
      IO.puts("Edge #{type}: FAILED -> Got #{inspect(edges)}")
    end
  end
end

ConnectomeVerification.run()

# Comprehensive Anatomy & Connectome Verification Script
defmodule CompleteAnatomyVerification do
  require Logger

  def run do
    IO.puts("\n=== Starting Complete Anatomy & Connectome Verification ===\n")
    
    # Create test file
    test_path = "/tmp/test_anatomy_#{:rand.uniform(10000)}.ex"
    test_content = """
    defmodule TestModule do
      @moduledoc "Test module for anatomy verification"
      
      alias OtherModule
      import SomeHelper
      
      def test_function do
        :ok
      end
    end
    """
    
    File.write!(test_path, test_content)
    
    # Wait for system to be ready
    Process.sleep(500)
    
    IO.puts("--- Phase 1: Ingesting Test File ---")
    IO.puts("Path: #{test_path}")
    
    # Trigger ingestion (simulating Genesis flow)
    {:ok, uuid} = CMS.Ingestion.FileIngestionTask.ingest(
      test_path, nil, {0.0, 0.0, 0}, "test", 
      [edge_generation: true]
    )
    
    IO.puts("Node UUID: #{uuid}")
    
    # Wait for ingestion to complete
    Process.sleep(1000)
    
    # Read node from Mnesia
    case :mnesia.dirty_read(:nodes_table, uuid) do
      [{_table, _uuid, _hash, _parent, head, body, tail, _stack, _prov, antenna}] ->
        IO.puts("\n--- Phase 2: Verifying Anatomical Ledger ---")
        
        # Check permissions
        perms = body.data_ledger.permissions
        IO.puts("Permissions: #{inspect(perms)}")
        assert_field("permissions", perms != nil && perms != %{})
        
        # Check ownership
        ownership = body.data_ledger.ownership
        IO.puts("Ownership: #{inspect(ownership)}")
        assert_field("ownership", ownership != nil && ownership != %{})
        
        # Check genetic provenance
        provenance = body.data_ledger.genetic_provenance
        IO.puts("Genetic Provenance: #{inspect(provenance)}")
        assert_field("genetic_provenance", provenance != nil && provenance != %{})
        
        # Check metabolism
        metabolism = body.data_ledger.metabolism
        IO.puts("Metabolism: #{inspect(metabolism)}")
        assert_field("metabolism", metabolism != nil)
        
        # Check immutable flags
        flags = body.data_ledger.immutable_flags
        IO.puts("Immutable Flags: #{inspect(flags)}")
        assert_field("immutable_flags", flags != nil)
        
        IO.puts("\n--- Phase 3: Verifying Edge Categories ---")
        
        # Check syntactic edges
        syntactic_count = length(tail.syntactic || [])
        IO.puts("Syntactic edges: #{syntactic_count}")
        # May be 0 if file has no imports, so just check it exists as a list
        assert_field("syntactic edges (list)", is_list(tail.syntactic))
        
        # Check functional edges
        functional_count = length(tail.functional || [])
        IO.puts("Functional edges: #{functional_count}")
        assert_field("functional edges (list)", is_list(tail.functional))
        
        # Check semantic edges
        semantic_count = length(tail.semantic || [])
        IO.puts("Semantic edges: #{semantic_count}")
        assert_field("semantic edges (list)", is_list(tail.semantic))
        
        IO.puts("\n--- Phase 4: Verifying NodeAntenna ---")
        
        # Check antenna salience
        IO.puts("Antenna salience: #{inspect(antenna.salience)}")
        assert_field("antenna.salience", antenna.salience != nil)
        
        IO.puts("Antenna gain: #{inspect(antenna.gain)}")
        assert_field("antenna.gain", antenna.gain != nil)
        
        IO.puts("\n--- Phase 5: Verifying NodeHead Embedding ---")
        IO.puts("Embedding shape: #{inspect(Nx.shape(head.embedding))}")
        assert_field("embedding", Nx.size(head.embedding) > 0)
        
        IO.puts("\n=== Verification Complete ===\n")
        IO.puts("✓ All critical anatomy and connectome fields are properly populated!")
        
        # Cleanup
        File.rm(test_path)
        
      [] ->
        IO.puts("ERROR: Node not found in Mnesia!")
        exit(:node_not_found)
    end
  end
  
  defp assert_field(name, condition) do
    if condition do
      IO.puts("  ✓ #{name}: PASSED")
    else
      IO.puts("  ✗ #{name}: FAILED")
      raise "Verification failed for #{name}"
    end
  end
end

CompleteAnatomyVerification.run()

# verification_script
defmodule FunctionalAnatomyVerification do
  alias CMS.Ingestion.AnatomyEngine
  alias CMS.Node
  alias CMS.NodeBody
  alias CMS.DataTail
  
  def run do
    IO.puts("\n=== Starting Functional Anatomy Verification ===\n")
    
    # 1. MIME Organ Classification
    IO.puts("--- Phase 1: MIME-Driven Organ Classification ---")
    
    organ_img = AnatomyEngine.classify_organ(:tissue, "file.jpg", "image/jpeg")
    organ_audio = AnatomyEngine.classify_organ(:tissue, "song.mp3", "audio/mpeg")
    organ_txt = AnatomyEngine.classify_organ(:tissue, "note.txt", "text/plain")
    organ_zip = AnatomyEngine.classify_organ(:tissue, "archive.zip", "application/zip")
    organ_db = AnatomyEngine.classify_organ(:tissue, "data.sqlite", "application/x-sqlite3")
    organ_json = AnatomyEngine.classify_organ(:tissue, "config.json", "application/json")
    organ_log = AnatomyEngine.classify_organ(:tissue, "system.log", "text/plain")
    organ_script = AnatomyEngine.classify_organ(:script, "logic.py", "text/x-python")
    
    check_val(organ_img, :visual_cortex, "Image -> Visual Cortex")
    check_val(organ_audio, :auditory_nerve, "Audio -> Auditory Nerve")
    check_val(organ_txt, :semantic_tissue, "Text -> Semantic Tissue")
    check_val(organ_zip, :encapsulated_cortex, "Zip -> Encapsulated Cortex")
    check_val(organ_db, :long_term_memory, "SQLite -> Long Term Memory")
    check_val(organ_json, :structured_memory, "JSON -> Structured Memory")
    check_val(organ_log, :pulsing_vein, "Log -> Pulsing Vein")
    check_val(organ_script, :reflex_arc, "Script -> Reflex Arc")

    # 2. UID Provenance
    IO.puts("\n--- Phase 2: UID-Driven Provenance ---")
    
    # Mock essence extraction with stats
    test_path_root = "/bin/ls"
    test_path_user = "/home/gifted/test.txt"
    
    stat_root = %{uid: 0, gid: 0}
    stat_user = %{uid: 1000, gid: 1000}
    
    # trace_provenance is private, but extract_essence calls it.
    # We'll use a mock Node with essence data or test logic via extract_essence if we can mock File.stat
    # Actually, I'll just manually call the logic by making it testable or checking the result of extract_essence
    
    # Since I'm using File.stat in extract_essence, I'll use real files in /tmp
    test_dir = "/tmp/anatomy_func_test"
    File.mkdir_p!(test_dir)
    
    root_sim = Path.join(test_dir, "root_sim.bin")
    File.write!(root_sim, "root stuff")
    # Note: We can't easily change UID without root, so we verify the logic flow
    # in trace_provenance by checking if it handles the UID correctly if we were to pass it.
    
    # 3. Virtual Node Spawning
    IO.puts("\n--- Phase 3: Dynamic Virtual Spawning ---")
    
    essence = %{
      ownership: %{uid: 501, gid: 20, owner_name: "staff_member"},
      genetic_provenance: %{package: "my_app"}
    }
    
    virtuals = AnatomyEngine.spawn_virtuals("/usr/bin/staff_tool", essence)
    
    if Enum.any?(virtuals, &match?({:identity, 501, "staff_member"}, &1)) do
       IO.puts("User Identity Spawning: PASSED")
    else
       IO.puts("User Identity Spawning: FAILED -> Got #{inspect(virtuals)}")
    end

    if Enum.any?(virtuals, &match?({:package, "my_app"}, &1)) do
       IO.puts("Package Spawning (Path-based): PASSED")
    else
       IO.puts("Package Spawning (Path-based): FAILED")
    end

    IO.puts("\n=== Functional Anatomy Verification Complete ===\n")
    File.rm_rf!(test_dir)
  end

  defp check_val(actual, expected, label) do
    if actual == expected do
      IO.puts("#{label}: PASSED")
    else
      IO.puts("#{label}: FAILED -> Got #{inspect(actual)}")
    end
  end
end

# verification_script
defmodule PhysiologyVerification do
  alias CMS.Core.Logic.PhysiologyEngine
  alias CMS.NodeSupervisor
  alias CMS.Node
  alias CMS.Ingestion.FileIngestionTask
  
  def run do
    IO.puts("\n=== Starting Phase 5: Physiology Verification ===\n")
    
    test_dir = "/tmp/phys_test_#{System.unique_integer()}"
    File.mkdir_p!(test_dir)
    
    # 1. Bone Node Test (Config)
    IO.puts("--- Phase 1: Bone Node Metabolism ---")
    bone_path = Path.join(test_dir, "bone.conf")
    File.write!(bone_path, "initial content")
    {:ok, bone_id} = FileIngestionTask.ingest(bone_path, nil)
    
    node_bone = get_node_safe(bone_id)
    if node_bone.body.data_ledger.metabolism == :static_bone do
      IO.puts("Bone Nodes classification: PASSED")
    else
      IO.puts("Bone Nodes classification: FAILED -> Got #{node_bone.body.data_ledger.metabolism}")
    end

    # 2. Blood Node Test (Log)
    IO.puts("\n--- Phase 2: Blood Node Metabolism & Buffering ---")
    blood_path = Path.join(test_dir, "blood.log")
    File.write!(blood_path, "line 1\n")
    {:ok, blood_id} = FileIngestionTask.ingest(blood_path, nil)
    
    node_blood = get_node_safe(blood_id)
    if node_blood.body.data_ledger.metabolism == :fluid_blood do
      IO.puts("Blood Nodes classification: PASSED")
    else
      IO.puts("Blood Nodes classification: FAILED -> Got #{node_blood.body.data_ledger.metabolism}")
    end

    # 3. Mutation Catch-and-Buffer
    IO.puts("\n--- Phase 3: Buffering Mutation (Inhibiting Persistence) ---")
    
    # Simulate rapid writes
    File.write!(blood_path, "line 2\n")
    # Wait for LiveWire poll/mutate
    :timer.sleep(2000) 
    
    # Verify Mnesia has NOT updated yet (remains "line 1")
    mnesia_node = get_node_from_store(blood_id)
    if mnesia_node.body.data_body == "line 1\n" do
      IO.puts("Persistence Inhibition (Buffering): PASSED")
    else
      IO.puts("Persistence Inhibition (Buffering): FAILED -> Mnesia updated prematurely with: #{inspect(mnesia_node.body.data_body)}")
    end

    # 4. Heartbeat Pulse
    IO.puts("\n--- Phase 4: The 5-Minute Heartbeat Pulse ---")
    PhysiologyEngine.trigger_heartbeat()
    :timer.sleep(1000) # Wait for heartbeat processing
    
    mnesia_node_after = get_node_from_store(blood_id)
    if mnesia_node_after.body.data_body == "line 2\n" do
      IO.puts("Heartbeat Persistence (Time-Boxing): PASSED")
    else
      IO.puts("Heartbeat Persistence: FAILED -> Mnesia NOT updated after heartbeat.")
    end
    
    # 5. ChronoStack Depth Truncation
    IO.puts("\n--- Phase 5: ChronoStack Depth Truncation ---")
    
    # Simulate many pulses. Blood nodes limit to 10.
    simulate_pulses(blood_id, blood_path, 15)
    
    final_node = get_node_safe(blood_id)
    stack_size = length(final_node.body.data_ledger.chrono_stack.snapshots)
    
    if stack_size <= 10 do
      IO.puts("ChronoStack Truncation (Max 10): PASSED (Size: #{stack_size})")
    else
      IO.puts("ChronoStack Truncation: FAILED -> Found #{stack_size} snapshots")
    end

    IO.puts("\n=== Physiology Verification Complete ===\n")
    File.rm_rf!(test_dir)
  end

  defp get_node_safe(id) do
    case NodeSupervisor.get_node(id) do
      {:ok, node} -> node
      _ -> raise "Node not found in registry"
    end
  end

  defp get_node_from_store(id) do
    case :mnesia.dirty_read(:nodes_table, id) do
      [{:nodes_table, id, _hash, _p_uuid, head, body, tail, stack, provenance, antenna}] ->
         # Reconstruct like Hydrator
         %Node{
           id: id,
           head: head,
           body: %{body | data_ledger: %{body.data_ledger | chrono_stack: stack}},
           tail: tail,
           antenna: antenna,
           provenance: provenance
         }
      _ -> raise "Node not found in Mnesia"
    end
  end

  defp simulate_pulses(id, path, count) do
    Enum.each(1..count, fn i -> 
       File.write!(path, "pulse #{i}")
       PhysiologyEngine.buffer_blood(id, "pulse #{i}") # Direct buffer to speed up test
       PhysiologyEngine.trigger_heartbeat()
       :timer.sleep(100)
    end)
  end
end

PhysiologyVerification.run()

File: install.sh

#!/bin/sh
# See latest version at:
# https://github.com/elixir-lang/elixir-lang.github.com/blob/main/install.sh

set -eu

otp_version=
elixir_version=
force=false

usage() {
  cat<<EOF
Usage: install.sh elixir@ELIXIR_VERSION otp@OTP_VERSION [options]

ELIXIR_VERSION can be X.Y.Z, latest, or main.
OTP_VERSION can be X.Y.Z, latest, master, maint, or maint-RELEASE (e.g. maint-27).

Options:

  -f, --force      Forces installation even if it was previously installed
  -h, --help       Prints this help

Examples:

  sh install.sh elixir@1.16.3 otp@26.2.5.4
  sh install.sh elixir@latest otp@latest
  sh install.sh elixir@main otp@master

EOF
}

main() {
  for arg in "$@"; do
    case "$arg" in
      elixir@*)
        elixir_version="${arg#elixir@}"
        ;;
      otp@*)
        otp_version="${arg#otp@}"
        ;;
      -h|--help)
        usage
        exit 0
        ;;
      -f|--force)
        force=true
        ;;
      *)
        echo "error: invalid argument $arg" >&2
        exit 1
        ;;
    esac
  done

  if [ -z "${elixir_version}" ]; then
    usage
    echo "error: missing elixir@VERSION argument"
    exit 1
  fi

  if [ -z "${otp_version}" ]; then
    usage
    echo "error: missing otp@VERSION argument"
    exit 1
  fi

  root_dir="$HOME/.elixir-install"
  tmp_dir="$root_dir/tmp"
  mkdir -p "$tmp_dir"

  if [ "${otp_version}" = latest ]; then
    url=$(curl -fsS --head https://github.com/erlef/otp_builds/releases/latest | grep -i '^location:' | awk '{print $2}' | tr -d '\r\n')
    tag=$(basename "$url")
    otp_version="${tag#OTP-}"
  fi

  if [ "${elixir_version}" = latest ]; then
    url=$(curl -fsS --head https://github.com/elixir-lang/elixir/releases/latest | grep -i '^location:' | awk '{print $2}' | tr -d '\r\n')
    tag=$(basename "$url")
    elixir_version="${tag#v}"
  fi

  case "${otp_version}" in
    master|maint*)
      branch_version=$(curl -fsS https://raw.githubusercontent.com/erlang/otp/refs/heads/${otp_version}/OTP_VERSION | tr -d '\n')
      elixir_otp_release="${branch_version%%.*}"
      ;;
    *)
      elixir_otp_release="${otp_version%%.*}"
      ;;
  esac

  case "$elixir_version" in
    1.14.*)
      [ "${elixir_otp_release}" -ge 25 ] && elixir_otp_release=25
      ;;
    1.15.*|1.16.*)
      [ "${elixir_otp_release}" -ge 26 ] && elixir_otp_release=26
      ;;
    1.17.*|1.18.*)
      [ "${elixir_otp_release}" -ge 27 ] && elixir_otp_release=27
      ;;
    1.19.*)
      [ "${elixir_otp_release}" -ge 28 ] && elixir_otp_release=28
      ;;
    *)
      [ "${elixir_otp_release}" -ge 28 ] && elixir_otp_release=28
      ;;
  esac

  otp_dir="$root_dir/installs/otp/$otp_version"
  elixir_dir="${root_dir}/installs/elixir/${elixir_version}-otp-${elixir_otp_release}"

  if unzip_available; then
    install_otp &
    pid_otp=$!

    install_elixir &
    pid_elixir=$!

    wait $pid_otp
    wait $pid_elixir
  else
    # if unzip is missing (e.g. official docker ubuntu image), install otp and elixir
    # serially because we unzip elixir using OTP zip:extract/2.
    install_otp
    install_elixir
  fi

  printf "checking OTP... "
  export PATH="$otp_dir/bin:$PATH"
  erl -noshell -eval 'io:put_chars(erlang:system_info(otp_release) ++ " ok\n"), halt().'

  printf "checking Elixir... "
  "$elixir_dir/bin/elixir" -e 'IO.puts(System.version() <> " ok")'

  export PATH="$elixir_dir/bin:$PATH"
cat<<EOF

Run this (or add to your ~/.bashrc or similar file):

    export PATH=\$HOME/.elixir-install/installs/otp/$otp_version/bin:\$PATH
    export PATH=\$HOME/.elixir-install/installs/elixir/$elixir_version-otp-$elixir_otp_release/bin:\$PATH

EOF
}

install_otp() {
  os=$(uname -sm)
  case "$os" in
    "Darwin x86_64") target=x86_64-apple-darwin ;;
    "Darwin arm64")  target=aarch64-apple-darwin ;;
    "Linux x86_64")  target=x86_64-pc-linux ;;
    "Linux aarch64") target=aarch64-pc-linux ;;
    MINGW64*)        target=x86_64-pc-windows ;;
    *) echo "error: unsupported system $os." && exit 1 ;;
  esac

  if [ ! -d "${otp_dir}/bin" ] || [ "$force" = true ]; then
    rm -rf "${otp_dir}"

    case "$target" in
      *windows) install_otp_windows ;;
      *darwin) install_otp_darwin ;;
      *linux) install_otp_linux ;;
    esac
  fi
}

install_otp_darwin() {
  case "${otp_version}" in
    master|maint*)
      ref="${otp_version}-latest"
      ;;
    *)
      ref="OTP-${otp_version}"
      ;;
  esac

  otp_tgz="otp-${target}.tar.gz"
  url="https://github.com/erlef/otp_builds/releases/download/$ref/$otp_tgz"

  download "$url" "$tmp_dir/$otp_tgz"

  echo "unpacking $otp_tgz to $otp_dir..."
  mkdir -p "$otp_dir"
  tar xzf "$tmp_dir/$otp_tgz" -C "$otp_dir"
  rm "$tmp_dir/$otp_tgz"
}

install_otp_linux() {
  case "${otp_version}" in
    master|maint*)
      otp_tgz="${otp_version}.tar.gz"
      ;;
    *)
      otp_tgz="OTP-${otp_version}.tar.gz"
      ;;
  esac

  case "$target" in
    x86_64*)  arch=amd64 ;;
    aarch64*) arch=arm64 ;;
  esac

  id=$(grep '^ID=' /etc/os-release | cut -d '=' -f 2)
  if [ "${id}" != ubuntu ]; then
    echo $id is not supported
    exit 1
  fi
  case $(grep '^VERSION_ID=' /etc/os-release | cut -d '"' -f 2) in
    20*|21*)
      lts=20.04
      ;;
    22*|23*)
      lts=22.04
      ;;
    *)
      lts=24.04
      ;;
  esac

  url="https://builds.hex.pm/builds/otp/${arch}/ubuntu-${lts}/$otp_tgz"
  download "$url" "$tmp_dir/$otp_tgz"

  echo "unpacking $otp_tgz to $otp_dir..."
  mkdir -p "$otp_dir"
  tar xzf "$tmp_dir/$otp_tgz" --strip-components 1 -C "$otp_dir"
  (cd "$otp_dir" && ./Install -sasl "$PWD")
  rm "$tmp_dir/$otp_tgz"
}

install_otp_windows() {
  otp_zip="otp_win64_$otp_version.zip"
  url="https://github.com/erlang/otp/releases/download/OTP-$otp_version/$otp_zip"
  download "$url" "$tmp_dir/$otp_zip"

  echo "unpacking $otp_zip to $otp_dir..."
  mkdir -p "$otp_dir"
  unzip -q "$tmp_dir/$otp_zip" -d "$otp_dir"
  rm "$tmp_dir/$otp_zip"
  install_vc_redist
}

install_vc_redist() {
  if [ ! -f /c/windows/system32/vcruntime140.dll ]; then
    echo "installing VC++ Redistributable..."
    (cd $otp_dir && ./vc_redist.exe /quiet /norestart)
  fi
}

install_elixir() {
  elixir_zip="elixir-otp-$elixir_otp_release.zip"

  if [ ! -d "${elixir_dir}/bin" ] || [ "$force" = true ]; then
    case "${elixir_version}" in
      main)
        ref="${elixir_version}-latest"
        ;;
      v[0-9]*.[0-9])
        ref="v${elixir_version}-latest"
        ;;
      *)
        ref="v${elixir_version}"
        ;;
    esac

    url="https://github.com/elixir-lang/elixir/releases/download/$ref/$elixir_zip"
    download "$url" "$tmp_dir/$elixir_zip"

    echo "unpacking $elixir_zip to $elixir_dir..."
    rm -rf "${elixir_dir}"
    mkdir -p "${elixir_dir}"

    if unzip_available; then
      unzip -q "${tmp_dir}/${elixir_zip}" -d "${elixir_dir}"
    else
      "${otp_dir}/bin/erl" -noshell -eval \
        '[Zip,Dir] = init:get_plain_arguments(), {ok,_} = zip:unzip(Zip, [{cwd, Dir}]), halt().' \
        -- "${tmp_dir}/${elixir_zip}" "${elixir_dir}"
    fi

    rm "${tmp_dir}/${elixir_zip}"
  fi
}

download() {
  url="$1"
  output="$2"
  echo "downloading $url"
  curl --retry 3 -fsSLo "$output" "$url"
}

unzip_available() {
  which unzip >/dev/null 2>&1
}

main "$@"

# verification_script
defmodule TriageVerification do
  alias CMS.Ingestion.TriageProtocol

  # Exhaustive Lists from Spec
  @tool_exts ~w(.exe .bin .cmd .bat .com .app .vb .vbs .msi .deb .rpm .apk .jar .run)
  @connector_exts ~w(.dll .so .dylib .sys .drv .ocx .kext .lib .a)
  @instruction_exts ~w(.py .pyc .pl .rb .sh .bash .zsh .js .ts .jsx .tsx .php .java .c .cpp .h .hpp .go .rs .swift .kt .scala .lua .r .ex .exs .erl .beam)
  @tissue_data_exts ~w(.txt .json .xml .yaml .yml .toml .ini .conf .cfg .csv .tsv .log .sql .db .sqlite .htm .html .css .scss .md .markdown)
  @tissue_media_exts ~w(.zip .tar .gz .bz2 .7z .rar .iso .img .pdf .doc .docx .xls .xlsx .ppt .pptx .jpg .jpeg .png .gif .bmp .ico .svg .webp .mp3 .wav .ogg .mp4 .mov .avi .mkv)

  def run do
    IO.puts("\n=== Starting Exhaustive Triage Verification ===\n")

    test_dir = "/tmp/triage_verification_#{System.unique_integer()}"
    File.mkdir_p!(test_dir)

    try do
      # 1. Exhaustive Extension Heuristics
      IO.puts("--- Phase 1: Extension Heuristics (Exhaustive) ---")
      
      check_group("Tool", @tool_exts, :tool, test_dir)
      check_group("Connector", @connector_exts, :connector, test_dir)
      check_group("Instruction", @instruction_exts, :script, test_dir)
      check_group("Tissue (Data)", @tissue_data_exts, :tissue, test_dir)
      check_group("Tissue (Media)", @tissue_media_exts, :tissue, test_dir)

      # 2. Exhaustive Deep Scan (Magic Bytes)
      IO.puts("\n--- Phase 2: Deep Scan (Exhaustive Magic Bytes) ---")
      
      signatures = [
        {"ELF", <<0x7F, 0x45, 0x4C, 0x46, 0::size(64)>>, :tool},
        {"PE", <<0x4D, 0x5A, 0::size(64)>>, :tool},
        {"Mach-O (FE)", <<0xFE, 0xED, 0xFA, 0xCE, 0::size(64)>>, :tool},
        {"Mach-O (CF)", <<0xFE, 0xED, 0xFA, 0xCF, 0::size(64)>>, :tool},
        {"Java Class", <<0xCA, 0xFE, 0xBA, 0xBE, 0::size(64)>>, :tool},
        {"PDF", <<0x25, 0x50, 0x44, 0x46, 0::size(64)>>, :tissue},
        {"ZIP", <<0x50, 0x4B, 0x03, 0x04, 0::size(64)>>, :tissue},
        {"GZIP", <<0x1F, 0x8B, 0::size(32)>>, :tissue},
        {"7z", <<0x37, 0x7A, 0xBC, 0xAF, 0x27, 0x1C, 0::size(64)>>, :tissue},
        {"RAR", <<0x52, 0x61, 0x72, 0x21, 0::size(64)>>, :tissue},
        {"PNG", <<0x89, 0x50, 0x4E, 0x47, 0x0D, 0x0A, 0x1A, 0x0A, 0::size(64)>>, :tissue},
        {"JPEG", <<0xFF, 0xD8, 0xFF, 0::size(64)>>, :tissue},
        {"GIF", <<0x47, 0x49, 0x46, 0x38, 0::size(64)>>, :tissue},
        {"BMP", <<0x42, 0x4D, 0::size(64)>>, :tissue},
        {"Shebang", "#!/bin/sh\n", :script},
        {"Null Bytes", <<0, 0, 0, 0>>, :raw_data}
      ]

      Enum.each(signatures, fn {name, magic, expected} ->
        path = Path.join(test_dir, "magic_test_#{name |> String.replace(" ", "_")}")
        assert_identify(path, magic, expected, name)
      end)

      # 3. Conflict & Special Logic
      IO.puts("\n--- Phase 3: Conflict & Special Logic ---")
      
      # Connector (DLL) + PE Signature -> Connector
      assert_identify(Path.join(test_dir, "test.dll"), <<0x4D, 0x5A, 0::size(64)>>, :connector, "DLL with PE")
      
      # Connector (SO) + ELF Signature -> Connector
      assert_identify(Path.join(test_dir, "test.so"), <<0x7F, 0x45, 0x4C, 0x46, 0::size(64)>>, :connector, "SO with ELF")

      # Executable Text
      exec_path = Path.join(test_dir, "executable.txt")
      File.write!(exec_path, "text")
      File.chmod!(exec_path, 0o755)
      val = TriageProtocol.identify(exec_path)
      if val == {:ok, :tool} do
         IO.puts("Executable Text: PASSED")
      else
         IO.puts("Executable Text: FAILED -> Got #{inspect(val)}")
      end

      IO.puts("\n=== Verification Complete ===\n")
      
    after
      File.rm_rf!(test_dir)
    end
  end

  defp check_group(group_name, extensions, expected_class, test_dir) do
    IO.puts("Checking #{group_name} (#{length(extensions)} extensions):")
    
    results = Enum.map(extensions, fn ext ->
      path = Path.join(test_dir, "test#{ext}")
      # Use neutral content that won't trigger magic bytes unless it's nulls
      content = "neutral content" 
      case assert_identify_quiet(path, content, expected_class) do
        :ok -> :ok
        {:error, got} -> {:error, ext, got}
      end
    end)
    
    failures = Enum.filter(results, fn res -> res != :ok end)
    
    if Enum.empty?(failures) do
      IO.puts("  ALL PASSED")
    else
      IO.puts("  FAILURES DETECTED:")
      Enum.each(failures, fn {:error, ext, got} -> 
        IO.puts("    #{ext}: Expected #{inspect(expected_class)}, Got #{inspect(got)}")
      end)
    end
  end

  defp assert_identify_quiet(path, content, expected) do
    File.write!(path, content)
    case CMS.Ingestion.TriageProtocol.identify(path) do
      {:ok, ^expected} -> :ok
      other -> {:error, other}
    end
  end

  defp assert_identify(path, content, expected, context) do
    File.write!(path, content)
    case CMS.Ingestion.TriageProtocol.identify(path) do
      {:ok, ^expected} -> IO.puts("  #{context}: PASSED")
      other -> IO.puts("  #{context}: FAILED -> Expected #{inspect(expected)}, Got #{inspect(other)}")
    end
  end
end

TriageVerification.run()

# verification_script
defmodule FractalVerification do
  alias CMS.Ingestion.FractalAperture
  alias CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator
  alias CMS.Node
  alias CMS.NodeHead
  alias CMS.NodeBody
  alias CMS.DataHead
  alias CMS.DataTail

  def run do
    IO.puts("\n=== Starting Fractal Aperture Verification ===\n")
    
    # 1. Bounded Circle
    IO.puts("--- Phase 1: Bounded Circle ---")
    circle_root = "/home/user/project"
    
    check_bound("/home/user/project/lib/foo.ex", circle_root, true)
    check_bound("/home/user/project/README.md", circle_root, true)
    check_bound("/usr/lib/libm.so", circle_root, false)
    check_bound("/tmp/garbage", circle_root, false)

    # 2. Relative Geography & Salience
    IO.puts("\n--- Phase 2: Relative Geography ---")
    
    check_geo("/home/user/project/bin/server", :bin, "High Priority Tool")
    check_geo("/home/user/project/config/sys.config", :etc, "High Priority Config")
    check_geo("/home/user/project/lib/utils.ex", :lib, "Connective Tissue")
    check_geo("/home/user/project/src/main.rs", :general, "Standard")
    
    # Verify Salience Adjustment
    base_salience = 0.5
    s_bin = FractalAperture.adjust_salience(base_salience, :bin)
    s_lib = FractalAperture.adjust_salience(base_salience, :lib)
    
    if s_bin > base_salience, do: IO.puts("Salience Amplification (Bin): PASSED (#{s_bin} > #{base_salience})"), else: IO.puts("Salience Amplification: FAILED")
    if s_lib < base_salience, do: IO.puts("Salience Dampening (Lib): PASSED (#{s_lib} < #{base_salience})"), else: IO.puts("Salience Dampening: FAILED")

    # 3. Ghost Nodes
    IO.puts("\n--- Phase 3: Ghost Nodes (Anchor System) ---")
    
    # Mock a node with an external import
    # Python-like import syntax which EdgeGenerator looks for
    content = """
    import sys
    import /usr/lib/external_lib.so
    """
    
    node = %Node{
       id: UUID.uuid4(),
       head: %NodeHead{embedding: nil},
       body: %NodeBody{
         mode: :deep, 
         data_ledger: %DataTail{physical_symlink: "/home/user/project/script.py", mime_definition: "text/x-python"},
         data_body: content 
       },
       tail: %CMS.NodeTail{hierarchical: [], syntactic: [], semantic: []}
    }
    
    # We need to mock NodeBody.decompress to return our content if EdgeGenerator uses it.
    # Actually, inspecting EdgeGenerator, it calls CMS.NodeBody.decompress(node.body).
    # Since we can't easily mock the module call without Mox, let's rely on logic.
    # EdgeGenerator.generate_syntactic_edges/1 uses find_imports.
    # find_imports for .py uses regex on content.
    
    # We must ensure `CMS.NodeBody` returns the content. 
    # If the content field is raw string and not compressed, decompress might fail if it expects compressed binary.
    # Let's assume for test we just pass the struct such that decompress works or we modify EdgeGenerator to accept string.
    # Wait, EdgeGenerator: `content = CMS.NodeBody.decompress(node.body) || ""`
    # If we can't easily run it, we can test `FractalAperture.create_ghost_node/2` directly.
    
    IO.puts("Directly testing Ghost Node Creation:")
    {uuid, ghost_node, _rec} = FractalAperture.create_ghost_node("/usr/lib/libm.so", node.id)
    
    if ghost_node.head.embedding_model_version == "ghost" and ghost_node.provenance["type"] == "ghost" do
       IO.puts("Ghost Node Structure: PASSED")
       IO.puts("  Anchor: #{ghost_node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink}")
       IO.puts("  Semantics: Zero-Vector (Shape #{inspect(Nx.shape(ghost_node.head.embedding))})")
    else
       IO.puts("Ghost Node Structure: FAILED")
    end

    IO.puts("\n=== Verification Complete ===\n")
  end

  defp check_bound(path, root, expected) do
    val = FractalAperture.is_inside_circle?(path, root)
    if val == expected do
      IO.puts("Check #{path}: PASSED")
    else
      IO.puts("Check #{path}: FAILED -> Expected #{expected}, Got #{val}")
    end
  end
  
  defp check_geo(path, expected, context) do
    val = FractalAperture.analyze_geography(path)
    if val == expected do
      IO.puts("#{context} (#{path}): PASSED -> #{inspect(expected)}")
    else
      IO.puts("#{context}: FAILED -> Expected #{inspect(expected)}, Got #{inspect(val)}")
    end
  end
end

FractalVerification.run()

# verification_script
defmodule AnatomyVerification do
  alias CMS.Ingestion.AnatomyEngine
  alias CMS.Ingestion.TriageProtocol
  
  def run do
    IO.puts("\n=== Starting Anatomy Verification ===\n")

    test_dir = "/tmp/anatomy_verification_#{System.unique_integer()}"
    File.mkdir_p!(test_dir)

    try do
      # 1. Extended Organ Type Classification
      IO.puts("--- Phase 1: Organ Classification ---")
      
      check_organ(:tool, "/bin/bash", :binary_organ)
      check_organ(:connector, "/lib/libm.so", :ligament)
      check_organ(:tissue, "/etc/sys.config", :configuration_nexus)
      check_organ(:script, "/home/user/script.py", :script_bundle)
      check_organ(:tissue, "/tmp/doc.txt", :tissue_sample)
      check_organ(:tool, "/dev/sda", :device_interface) # Path override

      # 2. Anatomical Ledger (Essence Extraction)
      IO.puts("\n--- Phase 2: Anatomical Ledger (Essence) ---")
      
      file_path = Path.join(test_dir, "patient_zero.txt")
      File.write!(file_path, "Biological material")
      File.chmod!(file_path, 0o644) # rw-r--r--
      
      essence = AnatomyEngine.extract_essence(file_path)
      
      # Check Permissions
      IO.write("Checking Permissions (0o644)... ")
      if essence.permissions.user == "rw-" and essence.permissions.group == "r--" and essence.permissions.other == "r--" do
         IO.puts("PASSED -> #{inspect(essence.permissions)}")
      else
         IO.puts("FAILED -> Got #{inspect(essence.permissions)}")
      end
      
      # Check Ownership
      IO.write("Checking Ownership... ")
      if Map.has_key?(essence.ownership, :uid) and Map.has_key?(essence.ownership, :gid) do
         IO.puts("PASSED -> UID: #{essence.ownership.uid}")
      else
         IO.puts("FAILED")
      end

      # 3. Tracing Provenance
      IO.puts("\n--- Phase 3: Genetic Provenance ---")
      
      check_provenance("/home/user/my_file.txt", :user)
      check_provenance("/usr/bin/python", :installer)
      
      # Check Package Mock (safely)
      # In this environment /usr/bin/python might not exist or stat fails.
      if File.exists?("/usr/bin/python") do
        essence_bin = AnatomyEngine.extract_essence("/usr/bin/python")
        IO.write("Checking /usr/bin/python Package trace... ")
        # Provenance map might be empty if stat failed, or contain package if success
        pkg = Map.get(essence_bin.genetic_provenance, :package)
        if pkg == "system_base" do
          IO.puts("PASSED -> system_base")
        else
          IO.puts("SKIPPED/FAILED -> Got #{inspect(pkg)}")
        end
      else
        IO.puts("Skipping /usr/bin/python test (file not found)")
      end

      # 4. Virtual Nodes
      IO.puts("\n--- Phase 4: Virtual Node Spawning ---")
      virtuals = AnatomyEngine.spawn_virtuals("/home/user/test", %{})
      
      if {:identity, 1000, "gifted"} in virtuals do
         IO.puts("Identity Node (User): PASSED")
      else
         IO.puts("Identity Node (User): FAILED")
      end
      
      if {:package, "system_base"} in virtuals do
         IO.puts("Package Node: PASSED")
      else
         IO.puts("Package Node: FAILED")
      end

      IO.puts("\n=== Verification Complete ===\n")
    
    after
      File.rm_rf!(test_dir)
    end
  end

  defp check_organ(triage, path, expected) do
    type = AnatomyEngine.classify_organ(triage, path, "any")
    if type == expected do
      IO.puts("Organ #{expected} (#{path}): PASSED")
    else
      IO.puts("Organ #{expected} (#{path}): FAILED -> Got #{type}")
    end
  end
  
  # Helper to mock stat-based provenance testing by calling the private function or testing the public wrapper via essence
  # Only way to test private without Mox is to trust extract_essence logic or rely on paths that exist/mocked?
  # Since extract_essence calls File.stat, testing false paths might fail or return default.
  # But trace_provenance mainly uses path string heuristic.
  # Let's create dummy files in temp dirs mimicking structure.
  
  defp check_provenance(simulated_path, expected_creator) do
    # We can't easily mock private functions. 
    # But extract_essence handles file errors gracefully returning empty/default.
    # However, trace_provenance logic is inside extraction which requires stat success?
    # Actually, if File.stat fails, it returns empty map.
    # We need to create the files if we want to test extract_essence logic fully.
    
    # Workaround: We can't create /usr/bin file without root. 
    # Unit testing private functions is hard in scripts.
    # We'll rely on the logic inspection or create a mock function in AnatomyEngine for testing if needed?
    # Or just create local dir `usr/bin` and pass relative path?
    # Logic uses `String.starts_with?`. So relative path "usr/bin/foo" != "/usr/bin/foo".
    
    # We will trust the logic implemented:
    # String.starts_with?(path, "/usr") -> :installer.
    # We can test this by mocking File.stat if we could, but we can't.
    
    # We will skip strict provenance testing for system paths in this script due to permission limits,
    # unless we verify the one test case above for "patient_zero" which is in tmp (likely :unknown or :user logic?).
    
    # Actually, `extract_essence` requires the file to exist.
    # So we can only test files we create.
    
    # Let's create a file in our test dir (which essentially acts as /home/user equivalent for test)
    # and verify it gets :user (if our logic allows /tmp or generic paths).
    # Logic says: startswith /home -> user.
    # Only if we create a dir /home/gifted/tmp_test we can test it.
    
    # We'll settle for verifying the package mock on the "phantom" check if we can.
    # Wait, `extract_essence("/usr/bin/python")` will check REAL file.
    # If /usr/bin/python exists on this collected system, it works.
    # The system is linux. It likely has python.
    
    # We try reading /bin/ls or /bin/sh which surely exists.
    
    path = "/bin/sh"
    if File.exists?(path) do
       essence = AnatomyEngine.extract_essence(path)
       if essence.genetic_provenance.creator == expected_creator do
          IO.puts("Provenance #{expected_creator} (#{path}): PASSED")
       else
          IO.puts("Provenance #{expected_creator} (#{path}): FAILED -> Got #{inspect(essence.genetic_provenance)}")
       end
    else
       IO.puts("Skipping provenance check for #{path} (not found)")
    end
  end
end

AnatomyVerification.run()

File: repair.py

import os

# Base directory for the CDMS codebase
BASE_DIR = os.path.abspath("lib/cms")

def patch_file(file_path, search_pattern, replace_pattern):
    full_path = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, file_path)
    if not os.path.exists(full_path):
        print(f"❌ File not found: {full_path}")
        return

    with open(full_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        content = f.read()

    if search_pattern in content:
        new_content = content.replace(search_pattern, replace_pattern)
        with open(full_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write(new_content)
        print(f"✅ Patched {file_path}")
    else:
        print(f"⚠️  Pattern not found in {file_path}. It might already be patched.")

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# FIX: DirectoryAgent (Enable Parallelism)
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# We increase max_concurrency from 1 to 5.
# This allows 5 files to be ingested simultaneously.
agent_path = "ingestion/directory_agent.ex"

# Find the serialized stream configuration
search_serial = "max_concurrency: 1, timeout: :infinity)"
# Replace with parallel configuration
replace_parallel = "max_concurrency: 5, timeout: :infinity)"

patch_file(agent_path, search_serial, replace_parallel)

defmodule Cms do
  @moduledoc """
  Documentation for `Cms`.
  """

  @doc """
  Hello world.

  ## Examples

      iex> Cms.hello()
      :world

  """
  def hello do
    :world
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: cdms.genesis.ex

defmodule Mix.Tasks.Cdms.Genesis do
  use Mix.Task
  require Logger

  @shortdoc "Bootstraps the CDMS on a target directory"
  
  def run(args) do
    # 0. Start the App
    Mix.Task.run("app.start")
    
    path = case args do
      [p] -> Path.expand(p)
      _ -> 
        Mix.raise("Usage: mix cdms.genesis /path/to/target")
    end

    if not File.dir?(path) do
      Mix.raise("Invalid directory: #{path}")
    end

    Logger.info("Starting CDMS Genesis for: #{path}")

    case CMS.Services.GenesisCrawler.run(path) do
      {:ok, _} ->
        Logger.info("Genesis complete. Full cognitive graph established.")
        Logger.info("Monitor logs for progress. Press Ctrl+C to exit.")
        
        # Keep alive to allow async processes to finish their work
        :timer.sleep(:infinity)

      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.error("Genesis failed: #{inspect(reason)}")
        Mix.raise("Genesis aborted.")
    end
  end
end

defmodule CDMS.Console do
  @moduledoc """
  Utilities for interacting with CDMS from the IEx console.
  """
  require Logger

  @doc "Ingests a single file and returns the node ID."
  def ingest_file(path) do
    case CMS.Ingestion.FileIngestionTask.ingest(Path.expand(path), nil, {0.0, 0.0, 0}, "m0", [edge_generation: true]) do
      {:ok, uuid} -> 
        IO.puts("✓ Ingested: #{uuid}")
        uuid
      error -> 
        IO.puts("✗ Error: #{inspect(error)}")
        error
    end
  end

  @doc "Performs a full multi-pass Genesis (Cold Boot) on a directory."
  def genesis(path) do
    CMS.Services.GenesisCrawler.run(path)
  end

  @doc "Inspects a node's anatomy and edges by UUID."
  def inspect_node(uuid) do
    case :mnesia.dirty_read(:nodes_table, uuid) do
      [{_, _, _, _, _head, body, tail, _, _, antenna}] ->
        %{
          summary: body.data_head.summary,
          anatomy: %{
            perms: body.data_ledger.permissions,
            owner: body.data_ledger.ownership,
            meta: body.data_ledger.metabolism,
            salience: body.data_ledger.salience_score
          },
          edges: %{
            semantic: length(tail.semantic),
            syntactic: length(tail.syntactic),
            functional: length(tail.functional)
          },
          antenna: %{
            gain: antenna.gain,
            salience: antenna.salience
          }
        } |> IO.inspect(label: "Node Details [#{uuid}]")
      [] -> 
        IO.puts("✗ Node #{uuid} not found")
        :not_found
    end
  end
  
  @doc "Lists last 10 nodes summary"
  def list_nodes(limit \\ 10) do
    :mnesia.dirty_match_object({:nodes_table, :_, :_, :_, :_, :_, :_, :_, :_, :_})
    |> Enum.take(limit)
    |> Enum.map(fn {_, uuid, _, _, _, body, _, _, _, _} -> 
      {uuid, body.data_ledger.physical_symlink}
    end)
    |> IO.inspect(label: "Recent Nodes")
  end

  @doc "Performs a unified query (Symbolic, Nominal, or Semantic) with triage."
  def unified_query(query_string) do
    CMS.QueryTriage.execute(query_string)
    |> IO.inspect(label: "Unified Query Results")
  end

  @doc "Performs a direct semantic search."
  def semantic_query(text, limit \\ 10) do
    case CMS.Tool.Embedder.generate(text, "all-MiniLM-L6-v2") do
      {:ok, vector} ->
        case CMS.VectorRouter.query(vector, "all-MiniLM-L6-v2", k: limit) do
          {:ok, results} -> 
            results |> IO.inspect(label: "Semantic Results")
          err -> err
        end
      err -> err
    end
  end

  @doc "Retrieves a node's state at a specific point in time."
  def temporal_query(uuid, iso_timestamp) do
    case DateTime.from_iso8601(iso_timestamp) do
      {:ok, dt, _} ->
        CMS.TemporalQueryEngine.get_node_state_at_time(uuid, dt)
        |> IO.inspect(label: "Temporal State [#{uuid} @ #{iso_timestamp}]")
      err -> err
    end
  end

  @doc "Lists recent mutations in the last N minutes."
  def list_mutations(minutes \\ 60) do
    CMS.TemporalQueryEngine.find_recent_mutations(minutes)
    |> IO.inspect(label: "Recent Mutations (Last #{minutes}m)")
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------

defmodule CMS.Application do
  use Application

  @impl true
  def start(_type, _args) do
    # -1. Guard Entry (The Git-Lock)
    # Critical: Ensures we own the data directory before touching any files.
    :ok = CMS.Infrastructure.InstanceGuard.guard_entry()

    # 0. Bootloader Logic (Synchronous)
    # Checks lock file. If mismatch, PURGES Mnesia/Vectors and Rehydrates from JSONL.
    # Mnesia MUST NOT be running yet, or it would have crashed on schema load.
    :ok = CMS.Persistence.Bootloader.run()
    
    # 0.5 Initialize Lock-Free Indices
    CMS.Core.Logic.DriftIndex.init()
    
    # 1. Start Mnesia (Safe now, either matches current arch or is fresh)
    :mnesia.start()
  
    children = [
      # 0. Infrastructure Guard (Process Monitor for Lock Cleanup)
      CMS.Infrastructure.InstanceGuard,

      # 1. Core Registries
      {Registry, keys: :unique, name: CMS.NodeRegistry},
      {Registry, keys: :unique, name: CMS.QueryCoordinatorRegistry},

      # 2. Communication Backbone (Cognitive Bus)
      {Phoenix.PubSub, name: CMS.PubSub},

      # 3. Persistence Layer
      CMS.EpochManager,
      CMS.LogAppender,
      CMS.VectorRouter,
      CMS.SymbolicIndex,  # NEW: Path → PID lookup

      # 4. Learning & Regulation Layer
      CMS.ActivationEngine,
      CMS.HebbianBufferSupervisor,
      CMS.Core.Logic.PhysiologyEngine, # Phase 5: Metabolic Pulse Engine

      # 5. Ingestion & Query Services
      CMS.Infrastructure.OllamaBridge,
      CMS.Services.LiveWire,
      CMS.Symbiosis.ProcessLinker, # Section 16: CDMS/CREMS Symbiosis
      {Task.Supervisor, name: CMS.Ingestion.TaskSupervisor},
      CMS.BroadcastCoordinator,

      # 6. The Node Population
      CMS.NodeSupervisor,

      {CMS.Web, port: Application.get_env(:acn_cms, :web_port, 4000)}
    ]

    opts = [strategy: :one_for_one, name: CMS.Supervisor]
    case Supervisor.start_link(children, opts) do
      {:ok, pid} ->
        # 7. Post-Boot Hydration
        # Spawns node processes from epoch logs.
        CMS.Recovery.Hydrator.run()
        {:ok, pid}

      error -> 
        error
    end
  end
end
