defmodule CMS.Services.SystemMixer do
  @moduledoc """
  The System Mixer: Orchestrates the 'Binding' phase of the Genesis workflow.
  Triggers live nodes to re-establish their syntactic and semantic edges once
  the population is stabilized.
  """

  require Logger
  alias CMS.NodeSupervisor

  @doc """
  Performs a system-wide bind operation on all established nodes.
  """
  def bind_all do
    Logger.info("System Mixer: Starting Phase 2 (Binding)...")
    
    # 1. Retrieve all node UUIDs from Mnesia
    # Table: :nodes_table, Attributes: [:uuid, :path_hash, :parent_uuid, :head_data, :body_data, :tail_data, :chrono_stack]
    uuids = :mnesia.dirty_all_keys(:nodes_table)
    
    Logger.info("System Mixer: Establishing connections for #{length(uuids)} nodes.")

    # 2. Distribute Binding Tasks in parallel
    uuids
    |> Enum.map(fn uuid ->
      Task.async(fn -> bind_node(uuid) end)
    end)
    |> Enum.each(&Task.await(&1, :infinity))

    # 3. Stabilization Period (Scatter-Gather Wavefront)
    # We wait a brief moment for semantic proposals to be processed by the actors.
    Logger.info("System Mixer: Waiting for semantic gathering to stabilize...")
    Process.sleep(500)

    Logger.info("System Mixer: Phase 2 (Binding) complete.")
    :ok
  end

  @doc """
  Binds a single node to its peers.
  """
  def bind_node(uuid) do
    case NodeSupervisor.get_node_pid(uuid) do
      nil ->
        Logger.warning("System Mixer: Node #{uuid} not active. Skipping.")
        {:error, :not_active}
      pid ->
        # Trigger recompute_edges on the NodeActor
        case GenServer.call(pid, :recompute_edges, 60_000) do
          {:ok, _node} -> 
            Logger.debug("System Mixer: Bound node #{uuid}")
            :ok
          error -> 
            Logger.error("System Mixer: Failed to bind node #{uuid}: #{inspect(error)}")
            error
        end
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: genesis_crawler.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Services.GenesisCrawler do
  @moduledoc """
  Orchestrates the formal Genesis process (Cold Boot).
  Handles Phase 1 (Awakening/Crawl) and Phase 2 (Binding/Mixer).
  """
  require Logger

  @summary_model "qwen3:0.6b"

  @doc """
  Runs a full Genesis on the target path.
  """
  def run(path, options \\ []) do
    path = Path.expand(path)
    Logger.info("GENESIS: Initiating Cold Boot for #{path}")

    with :ok <- check_connectivity(),
         :ok <- __MODULE__.Phase1.run(path, options),
         :ok <- __MODULE__.Phase2.run() do
      Logger.info("GENESIS: Cold Boot COMPLETE. Full cognitive graph established.")
      {:ok, path}
    else
      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.error("GENESIS: Cold Boot ABORTED - #{inspect(reason)}")
        {:error, reason}
    end
  end

  defp check_connectivity do
    Logger.info("GENESIS: Checking Ollama Connectivity...")
    case CMS.Infrastructure.OllamaBridge.summarize("system check", @summary_model, nil) do
      {:ok, _} -> 
        Logger.info("GENESIS: Ollama Bridge ONLINE.")
        :ok
      {:error, reason} ->
        {:error, {:ollama_unreachable, reason}}
    end
  end

  defmodule Phase1 do
    @moduledoc "Phase 1: Awakening (Hierarchy Expansion)"
    require Logger

    def run(path, options) do
      Logger.info("GENESIS: Phase 1 (Awakening) starting...")
      
      # Start the Directory Agent to crawl
      # We use self() to listen for the wavefront_complete signal
      {:ok, pid} = CMS.NodeSupervisor.start_directory_agent(
        path, 
        nil, 
        {0.0, 0.0, 0}, 
        "m0", 
        Keyword.merge(options, [notify_pid: self()])
      )

      Logger.info("GENESIS: Wavefront expansion active...")

      # Wait for completion (sync barrier)
      receive do
        {:wavefront_complete, ^pid} -> 
          Logger.info("GENESIS: Phase 1 COMPLETE.")
          :ok
      after
        # 15 minute timeout for large crawls
        900_000 -> 
          {:error, :wavefront_timeout}
      end
    end
  end

  defmodule Phase2 do
    @moduledoc "Phase 2: Binding (Synaptic Integration)"
    require Logger

    def run do
      Logger.info("GENESIS: Phase 2 (Binding) starting...")
      
      # Establish semantic and connectome links
      try do
        CMS.Services.SystemMixer.bind_all()
        Logger.info("GENESIS: Phase 2 COMPLETE.")
        :ok
      rescue
        e -> 
          Logger.error("GENESIS: Phase 2 CRASHED - #{inspect(e)}")
          {:error, {:binding_failure, e}}
      end
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: live_wire.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Services.LiveWire do
  @moduledoc """
  The LiveWire Sentinel: Keeps the CDMS in sync with the physical file system.
  """

  use GenServer
  require Logger
  alias CMS.Infrastructure.OllamaBridge

  @poll_interval 1000
  @debounce_ms 500

  def start_link(_opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, %{watched_paths: %{}}, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @doc """
  Adds a path to be watched by LiveWire.
  """
  def watch(path) do
    GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, {:watch, path})
  end

  # --- Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(state) do
    schedule_poll()
    {:ok, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:watch, path}, state) do
    # When watching, we store the current mtime/hash if possible
    mtime = get_mtime(path)
    {:noreply, %{state | watched_paths: Map.put(state.watched_paths, path, mtime)}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:poll_fs, state) do
    # Hybrid: For now, we poll the watched_paths
    Enum.each(state.watched_paths, fn {path, last_mtime} ->
      current_mtime = get_mtime(path)
      if current_mtime && current_mtime != last_mtime do
        # Trigger Mutation Loop with debounce
        Process.send_after(self(), {:mutate, path}, @debounce_ms)
      end
    end)

    schedule_poll()
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info({:mutate, path}, state) do
    # Step 6.2: The Mutation Loop
    Logger.info("LiveWire: mutation detected on #{path}")
    
    case handle_mutation(path) do
      {:ok, _uuid} ->
        # Update last known mtime to avoid re-triggering immediately
        new_mtime = get_mtime(path)
        {:noreply, %{state | watched_paths: Map.put(state.watched_paths, path, new_mtime)}}
      _ ->
        {:noreply, state}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(_msg, state) do
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  # --- Mutation Logic ---

  defp handle_mutation(path) do
    if !File.exists?(path) do
      # Gap 6.3: Self-Healing (ENOENT Detection)
      uuid = UUID.uuid5(:oid, path)
      Logger.info("LiveWire: Detected DELETION of #{path}. Scouring node #{uuid}.")
      CMS.NodeSupervisor.terminate_node(uuid)
    else
      if File.dir?(path) do
        # 4.1 Foreign Object Detection (Structural Mutation)
        handle_directory_mutation(path)
      else
        uuid = UUID.uuid5(:oid, path)
        
        # 1. Verify Hash
        case File.read(path) do
          {:ok, content} ->
            new_hash = :crypto.hash(:sha256, content) |> Base.encode16(case: :lower)
            
            # Get current node state
            case CMS.NodeSupervisor.get_node(uuid) do
              {:ok, old_node} ->
                if old_node.body.data_ledger.metabolism == :fluid_blood do
                  # Phase 5: Blood Node Metabolism
                  # Instead of full reincarnation, we buffer for the heartbeat.
                  Logger.debug("LiveWire: routing Blood Node mutation to Physiology Buffer: #{path}")
                  CMS.Core.Logic.PhysiologyEngine.buffer_blood(uuid, content)
                  {:ok, uuid}
                else
                  # Bone Node logic (Standard change tracking)
                  if old_node.body.data_ledger.integrity_hash == new_hash do
                    Logger.debug("LiveWire: hash matches, ignoring touch event.")
                    {:ok, uuid}
                  else
                    perform_full_reincarnaion(old_node, content, new_hash)
                  end
                end
              _ ->
                Logger.warning("LiveWire: detected change for unknown node #{uuid}")
                {:error, :node_not_found}
            end
          {:error, _} ->
            # Race condition: file deleted between exists? and read
            handle_mutation(path) 
        end
      end
    end
  end

  defp handle_directory_mutation(path) do
    parent_uuid = UUID.uuid5(:oid, path)
    case CMS.NodeSupervisor.get_node(parent_uuid) do
      {:ok, parent_node} ->
        case File.ls(path) do
          {:ok, current_items} ->
            # 1. Old Objects (from Connectome)
            old_items = parent_node.tail.hierarchical
                        |> Enum.filter(fn e -> e.type == :parent_of end)
                        |> Enum.map(fn e -> 
                          case :mnesia.dirty_read(:nodes_table, e.target_node_id) do
                            [{_, _, _, _, _, body, _, _}] -> Path.basename(body.data_ledger.physical_symlink)
                            _ -> nil
                          end
                        end) |> Enum.filter(& &1)

            # 2. Deletions (Orphan Logic)
            deleted_items = old_items -- current_items
            Enum.each(deleted_items, fn item ->
               item_id = UUID.uuid5(:oid, Path.join(path, item))
               CMS.NodeSupervisor.terminate_node(item_id)
            end)

            # 3. New Objects (Genesis Logic)
            # FILTER: Apply ExclusionCortex to stop ingestion of deps, _build, etc.
            new_items = 
              (current_items -- old_items)
              |> Enum.filter(fn item -> 
                 CMS.Ingestion.ExclusionCortex.should_traverse?(Path.join(path, item))
              end)
            
            if new_items != [] do
              Logger.info("LiveWire: Structural Mutation detected in #{path}. New valid objects: #{inspect(new_items)}")
              # Trigger Mitosis for new items
              {_x, _y, z} = parent_node.head.spatial_coords
              
              Enum.with_index(new_items) |> Enum.each(fn {item, idx} ->
                item_path = Path.join(path, item)
                
                # Sibling index offset
                offset = idx + 100 
                r = :math.sqrt(offset)
                theta = 2.4 * offset
                coords = {r * :math.cos(theta), r * :math.sin(theta), z + 1}
                
                # Width Descriptor
                parent_base = String.replace(parent_node.head.width_descriptor, ~r/\d+$/, "")
                child_char = <<65 + rem(offset, 26)>>
                width = "#{parent_base}#{child_char}0"

                if File.dir?(item_path) do
                   CMS.NodeSupervisor.start_directory_agent(item_path, parent_uuid, coords, width)
                else
                   CMS.Ingestion.FileIngestionTask.ingest(item_path, parent_uuid, coords, width)
                end
              end)
            end
            {:ok, parent_uuid}
          _ -> {:error, :scan_failed}
        end
      _ -> {:error, :parent_node_not_found}
    end
  end

  defp perform_full_reincarnaion(old_node, content, new_hash) do
    # Step 6.2: Re-Cognition & Mutation Loop
    Logger.info("LiveWire: performing Re-Cognition for #{old_node.id}")
    
    # 1. Chrono-Shift: Push current state to History Stack
    # We use the old_node's data before modifying it.
    old_body_content = 
      if old_node.body.mode == :deep do
        # Decompress if needed, but for diffing we might want raw string.
        # Check if NodeBody has decompress helper? Yes I added it.
        CMS.NodeBody.decompress(old_node.body) || ""
      else
        "" # Mode B has no content to diff against
      end

    new_stack = CMS.ChronoStack.push(
      old_node.body.data_ledger.chrono_stack || CMS.ChronoStack.new(), # Ensure stack exists
      old_body_content,
      content,
      old_node.body.data_ledger.integrity_hash,
      :livewire_mutation
    )

    # 2. Antenna Boost
    new_antenna = %{old_node.antenna | 
      gain: min(1.0, old_node.antenna.gain + 0.2),
      frequency: old_node.antenna.frequency + 1,
      resonance_pattern: "high_write_frequency"
    }

    # 3. Vector Refresh & Body Update (Async to avoid blocking Sentinel)
    Task.start(fn ->
      path = old_node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink
      
      # Re-Cognize with Robust Fallback
      {summary, concepts, embedding} = 
        case OllamaBridge.summarize(content) do
          {:ok, %{"summary" => s, "concepts" => c}} ->
             # Summary succeeded, try embedding
             case OllamaBridge.embed("#{Path.basename(path)}: #{s}") do
               {:ok, vec_list} -> 
                  {s, c, Nx.tensor(vec_list)}
               _ -> 
                  Logger.warning("LiveWire: Embedding timed out for \#{path}. Using new summary but old vector.")
                  {s, c, old_node.head.embedding}
             end
          _ -> 
             # Total cognitive failure (Timeout/Busy) -> Preserve old cognitive state
             Logger.warning("LiveWire: Cognition timed out for \#{path}. Preserving old semantics.")
             {old_node.body.data_head.summary, old_node.body.data_head.concepts, old_node.head.embedding}
        end

      new_head = %{old_node.head | embedding: embedding}
      new_data_head = CMS.DataHead.new(summary, concepts)
      new_ledger = %{old_node.body.data_ledger | integrity_hash: new_hash, last_mutation: DateTime.utc_now(), chrono_stack: new_stack} # Store stack in ledger? Spec says "Chrono Stack" is separate in Mnesia schema 8.1, but Node structure usually has it attached.
      # Wait, Schema 8.1 says `chrono_stack` is a column in `nodes_table`.
      # And Node struct (Step 2.3) doesn't explicitly list it in Body, but Section 5.2 says "Every Node maintains a stack".
      # I'll enable it in `NodeBody` or just pass it to persistence.
      # For now, I'll attach it to the `ledger` since it wasn't in the top-level Node struct definition I reviewed earlier (Node struct had head, body, tail, antenna).
      # Actually, let's keep it in the Mnesia record for now, or update Node struct. 
      # I'll update the Node struct to carry it in `body.data_ledger` if possible, or just assume NodeActor handles it.
      # Check `NodeBody` struct... `data_ledger`. `DataTail` struct... I'll check `DataTail`.
      # DataTail had `:last_mutation`.
      # I'll add `chrono_stack` to `DataTail` (Ledger) effectively via Map.put for now to pass it along.
      
      new_body = CMS.NodeBody.new(new_data_head, content, new_ledger, :deep) # Mode A refresh
      
      updated_node = %{old_node | head: new_head, body: new_body, antenna: new_antenna, last_fired: DateTime.utc_now()}
      
      # Persistence & Broadcast
      CMS.NodeSupervisor.update_node(updated_node)
      
      # Force Edge Regeneration (Hebbian Priming)
      CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator.generate_edges(updated_node) |> CMS.NodeSupervisor.update_node()

      Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(CMS.PubSub, "cdms:mutations", {:mutation, old_node.id})
    end)

    {:ok, old_node.id}
  end

  # --- Helpers ---

  defp get_mtime(path) do
    case File.stat(path) do
      {:ok, %File.Stat{mtime: mtime}} -> mtime
      _ -> nil
    end
  end

  defp schedule_poll do
    Process.send_after(self(), :poll_fs, @poll_interval)
  end
end



------------------------------------------------------------
File: symbolic_index.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.SymbolicIndex do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @moduledoc """
  Mode A: The Symbolic Index - O(1) Path Lookup

  Maintains a mapping of absolute filesystem paths to Node PIDs.
  Provides instant access to nodes when the exact path is known.
  
  Primary use case: Automated agents and scripts that know exact file paths.
  """

  @table_name :symbolic_index

  # API

  def start_link(opts \\ []) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @doc """
  Registers a node's path with its PID.
  Called during file/directory ingestion.
  """
  def register_path(path, node_pid) when is_binary(path) and is_pid(node_pid) do
    :ets.insert(@table_name, {path, node_pid})
    :ok
  end

  @doc """
  Looks up a node by its absolute path.
  Returns {:ok, pid} or {:error, :not_found}.
  
  Complexity: O(1)
  """
  def get_node_by_path(path) when is_binary(path) do
    case :ets.lookup(@table_name, path) do
      [{^path, pid}] when is_pid(pid) ->
        if Process.alive?(pid) do
          {:ok, pid}
        else
          # Clean up dead PID
          :ets.delete(@table_name, path)
          {:error, :not_found}
        end
      [] ->
        {:error, :not_found}
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Unregisters a path (e.g., when node is terminated).
  """
  def unregister_path(path) when is_binary(path) do
    :ets.delete(@table_name, path)
    :ok
  end

  @doc """
  Returns all registered paths (for debugging/admin).
  """
  def list_all_paths do
    :ets.tab2list(@table_name)
    |> Enum.map(fn {path, pid} -> {path, pid, Process.alive?(pid)} end)
  end

  # Callbacks

  @impl true
  def init(_opts) do
    # Create ETS table with public read access
    :ets.new(@table_name, [
      :named_table,
      :set,           # Each path maps to one PID
      :public,        # Allow other processes to read
      read_concurrency: true
    ])

    Logger.info("SymbolicIndex: Path registry initialized")
    {:ok, %{}}
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: node_drift_manager.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.NodeDriftManager do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  alias CMS.{NodeSupervisor, SemanticRegion, LogAppender}

  @moduledoc """
  Monitors and repairs Semantic Region Drift.

  Implements Section 11.4: Node Migration Logic (Gap 11).

  CRITICAL UPDATE: Uses Task.async_stream for high-concurrency, non-blocking checks.
  """

  # Configuration
  @check_interval :timer.minutes(30)
  @batch_size 100 # Batch size is now used as the max_concurrency for throttling checks

  def start_link(_opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, :ok, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @impl true
  def init(:ok) do
    Process.send_after(self(), :check_drift, @check_interval)
    {:ok, %{}}
  end

  @impl true
  # --- CONCURRENCY REFACTOR: Using Task.async_stream for efficient checking ---
  def handle_info(:check_drift, state) do
    Logger.debug("NodeDriftManager: Initiating topology alignment check.")

    nodes_to_check = NodeSupervisor.get_all_active_node_pids()

    # Task.async_stream provides throttled concurrency control
    nodes_to_check
    |> Task.async_stream(&check_node/1, max_concurrency: @batch_size)
    |> Enum.each(fn
      {:ok, result} ->
        # Result is either :migrated or :noop, which is handled by check_node/1's side effects
        result
      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.error("NodeDriftManager: Async check failed: #{inspect(reason)}")
    end)

    Logger.info("NodeDriftManager: Dispatched checks for #{length(nodes_to_check)} nodes.")

    Process.send_after(self(), :check_drift, @check_interval)
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  # --- FUNCTION REFACTOR: Removed redundant Task.start wrapper ---
  defp check_node(pid) do
    try do
      # Note: This is a synchronous call, but the caller (handle_info) runs it concurrently.
      {node_id, embedding, model_version, current_region_id} =
        GenServer.call(pid, :get_drift_info, 5000)

      correct_region_id = SemanticRegion.compute_region_hash(embedding, model_version)

      if correct_region_id != current_region_id do
        migrate_node(pid, node_id, current_region_id, correct_region_id)
        :migrated
      else
        :noop
      end
    catch
      _, _ -> :noop # Node likely decayed, crashed, or call timed out during check
    end
  end

  def migrate_node(pid, node_id, old_region, new_region) do
    CMS.SemanticRegion.unsubscribe(old_region, node_id)
    GenServer.cast(pid, {:update_region, new_region})
    CMS.SemanticRegion.subscribe(new_region, node_id)

    # CRITICAL: Append migration event to the log for auditability
    LogAppender.append_node_event(node_id, :node_migrated, %{
      previous_region: old_region,
      new_region: new_region,
      reason: "semantic_drift_correction"
    })

    Logger.info("Node #{node_id} migrated to Region #{new_region}.")
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: query_triage.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.QueryTriage do
  @moduledoc """
  The Query Router Triage Logic
  
  Analyzes input query strings and routes them to the appropriate mode:
  - Mode A (Symbolic): Path detection → O(1) path lookup
  - Mode B (Nominal): Filename detection → Name-based search with disambiguation
  - Mode C (Semantic): Natural language → Vector search
  """

  require Logger

  @doc """
  Routes a query string to the appropriate mode.
  
  Returns: `{:symbolic, path}` | `{:nominal, filename}` | `{:semantic, text}`
  """
  def route(query_string, opts \\ [])
  
  def route(query_string, opts) when is_binary(query_string) do
    # Allow manual override
    case Keyword.get(opts, :mode) do
      mode when mode in [:symbolic, :nominal, :semantic] ->
        {mode, query_string}
      _ ->
        auto_route(query_string)
    end
  end

  defp auto_route(query_string) do
    cond do
      # Gate 1: Symbolic Check (Path Detection)
      is_path?(query_string) ->
        {:symbolic, expand_path(query_string)}

      # Gate 2: Nominal Check (Filename Detection)
      is_filename?(query_string) ->
        {:nominal, query_string}

      # Gate 3: Semantic Check (Natural Language)
      true ->
        {:semantic, query_string}
    end
  end

  # --- GATE 1: Path Detection ---
  
  defp is_path?(str) do
    # Heuristics:
    # - Starts with root indicator: /, ~/, C:\
    # - Contains path separators
    # - Starts with ./ or ../
    Regex.match?(~r{^(/|~/|[A-Z]:\\|\\./|\\.\\./)}i, str) or 
    String.contains?(str, "/") and not has_nl_indicators?(str)
  end

  defp expand_path(path) do
    # Expand ~ and relative paths
    Path.expand(path)
  end

  # --- GATE 2: Filename Detection ---
  
  defp is_filename?(str) do
    # Heuristics:
    # - Single token (no spaces or only one space)
    # - Has file extension
    # - No natural language indicators
    is_single_token?(str) and
    has_file_extension?(str) and
    not has_nl_indicators?(str)
  end

  defp is_single_token?(str) do
    # Allow one space for "config json" but not multiple
    parts = String.split(str, " ", trim: true)
    length(parts) <= 2
  end

  defp has_file_extension?(str) do
    ext = Path.extname(str)
    ext != "" and String.length(ext) <= 6  # Reasonable extension length
  end

  # --- GATE 3: Natural Language Detection ---
  
  defp has_nl_indicators?(str) do
    # Indicators of natural language:
    # - Contains question marks
    # - Contains multiple spaces
    # - Contains common verbs/articles
    String.contains?(str, "?") or
    String.contains?(str, "!") or
    length(String.split(str, " ", trim: true)) >= 4 or
    has_common_words?(str)
  end

  defp has_common_words?(str) do
    lower = String.downcase(str)
    
    # Common English words that indicate natural language
    indicators = [
      "how", "what", "why", "when", "where", "who",
      "is", "are", "was", "were", "the", "a", "an",
      "does", "do", "can", "should", "would"
    ]
    
    Enum.any?(indicators, fn word -> String.contains?(lower, " #{word} ") or String.starts_with?(lower, "#{word} ") end)
  end

  @doc """
  Executes an optimized query based on the detected mode.
  
  Returns standardized result format:
  `{:ok, mode, results}` | `{:error, reason}`
  """
  def execute(query_string, opts \\ []) do
    {mode, processed_query} = route(query_string, opts)
    Logger.debug("QueryTriage: Detected mode=#{mode} for query: #{String.slice(query_string, 0, 50)}")
    
    case mode do
      :symbolic ->
        execute_symbolic(processed_query, opts)
      
      :nominal ->
        execute_nominal(processed_query, opts)
      
      :semantic ->
        execute_semantic(processed_query, opts)
    end
  end

  # --- Mode A: Symbolic Execution ---
  
  defp execute_symbolic(path, _opts) do
    case CMS.SymbolicIndex.get_node_by_path(path) do
      {:ok, pid} ->
        case GenServer.call(pid, :get_state_snapshot, 5000) do
          {:ok, node} -> {:ok, :symbolic, [node]}
          node when is_map(node) -> {:ok, :symbolic, [node]}
          _ -> {:error, :node_unreachable}
        end
      {:error, :not_found} ->
        {:ok, :symbolic, []}
    end
  rescue
    _ -> {:error, :lookup_failed}
  end

  # --- Mode B: Nominal Execution ---
  
  defp execute_nominal(filename, _opts) do
    case CMS.VectorRouter.find_by_name(filename) do
      {:ok, uuid} ->
        # Single match - return it
        case fetch_node(uuid) do
          {:ok, node} ->
            # Add disambiguation context
            parent_path = get_parent_path(node)
            result = Map.put(node, :context, parent_path)
            {:ok, :nominal, [result]}
          _ ->
            {:ok, :nominal, []}
        end
      
      {:error, :not_found} ->
        # Try to find all matches (future enhancement)
        {:ok, :nominal, []}
    end
  end

  defp fetch_node(uuid) do
    case CMS.NodeSupervisor.get_node_pid(uuid) do
      nil -> {:error, :not_found}
      pid ->
        try do
          case GenServer.call(pid, :get_state_snapshot, 5000) do
            {:ok, node} -> {:ok, node}
            node when is_map(node) -> {:ok, node}
            _ -> {:error, :invalid_response}
          end
        catch
          _, _ -> {:error, :call_failed}
        end
    end
  end

  defp get_parent_path(node) do
    # Extract parent path from node for disambiguation
    # Use direct struct access instead of get_in/2 since CMS.Node doesn't implement Access
    path = case node do
      %CMS.Node{body: %{data_ledger: %{physical_symlink: symlink}}} when is_binary(symlink) ->
        symlink
      %{body: %{data_ledger: %{physical_symlink: symlink}}} when is_binary(symlink) ->
        symlink
      _ ->
        "unknown"
    end
    
    if path != "unknown" do
      Path.dirname(path)
    else
      "unknown"
    end
  end

  # --- Mode C: Semantic Execution ---
  
  defp execute_semantic(text, opts) do
    # Delegate to decentralized search
    model = Keyword.get(opts, :model, "all-MiniLM-L6-v2")
    
    case CMS.Tool.Embedder.generate(text, model) do
      {:ok, vector} ->
        query_id = UUID.uuid4()
        
        # Prepare Query Context
        query_context = %{
          query_id: query_id,
          query_vector: vector,
          query_text: text,
          agent_id: "anonymous", # Default for triage
          trace: MapSet.new(),
          embedding_model: model,
          min_relevance: Keyword.get(opts, :threshold, 0.35),
          reasoning_mode: :normal,
          system_mode: :normal
        }

        # Start a coordinator to collect decentralized responses
        # We need a way to receive the results synchronously here for the TRIAGE execute/2 call
        # We'll use the same pattern as CMS.Web.Router (waiting for ready signal and results)
        
        target_regions = [CMS.SemanticRegion.compute_region_hash(vector, model)]
        
        coord_opts = [max_results: Keyword.get(opts, :max_results)]
        {:ok, coord_pid} = CMS.QueryCoordinator.start_link(query_id, target_regions, self(), coord_opts)
        ref = Process.monitor(coord_pid)

        receive do
          {:coordinator_ready, ^query_id} ->
            Logger.debug("QueryTriage: Coordinator #{query_id} confirmed ready. Dispatching decentralized broadcast.")
            CMS.BroadcastCoordinator.broadcast_query(query_context)
          {:DOWN, ^ref, :process, _pid, _reason} ->
            :ok
        after
          2000 -> Logger.error("QueryTriage: Timeout waiting for coordinator ready signal.")
        end

        # Wait for results
        results =
          receive do
            {:query_result, ^query_id, received_results} ->
              Process.demonitor(ref, [:flush])
              # Standardize format: results are {score, node} tuples from coordinator
              Enum.map(received_results, fn {score, node} -> %{node: node, score: score} end)
            {:DOWN, ^ref, :process, _pid, _reason} ->
              []
          after 10000 ->
            Process.demonitor(ref, [:flush])
            []
          end

        {:ok, :semantic, results}
      
      {:error, _} ->
        {:error, :embedding_failed}
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: process_linker.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Symbiosis.ProcessLinker do
  @moduledoc """
  The Definition Linker: Bridges Anatomy (CDMS Files) and Physiology (CREMS Processes).
  
  Periodically scans the host system for running processes, identifies their
  source executables, and establishes `:defines` edges in the CDMS.
  """
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @sweep_interval :timer.seconds(30)

  def start_link(opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  def init(_opts) do
    schedule_sweep()
    {:ok, %{last_sweep: nil}}
  end

  def handle_info(:sweep, state) do
    Logger.info("ProcessLinker: Starting physiological sweep...")
    active_pids()
    |> Enum.each(&link_process/1)
    
    schedule_sweep()
    {:noreply, %{state | last_sweep: DateTime.utc_now()}}
  end

  defp schedule_sweep do
    Process.send_after(self(), :sweep, @sweep_interval)
  end

  # --- Linkage Logic ---

  defp link_process(pid) do
    case get_executable_path(pid) do
      {:ok, path} ->
        # Look up the Anatomy Node (CDMS) for this path
        case CMS.SymbolicIndex.get_node_by_path(path) do
          {:ok, cdms_node_pid} ->
            establish_link(pid, path, cdms_node_pid)
          _ ->
            # Node not found in CDMS, likely ignored or not yet ingested
            :ok
        end
      _ -> :ok
    end
  end

  defp establish_link(os_pid, path, cdms_node_pid) do
    # 1. Get the CDMS Node UUID
    case GenServer.call(cdms_node_pid, :get_state_snapshot) do
      {:ok, %CMS.Node{id: cdms_uuid}} ->
        Logger.debug("ProcessLinker: Link established PID #{os_pid} -> #{path} (#{cdms_uuid})")
        
        # Broadcast the link for real-time visualization
        Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(CMS.PubSub, "cdms:symbiosis", {:process_link, os_pid, cdms_uuid})
        
        # Physiological activity boosts activation (Pulse)
        # We use a specific pulse strength to signal active execution
        GenServer.cast(cdms_node_pid, :fire)
      _ -> :ok
    end
  rescue
    e -> Logger.warning("ProcessLinker: Failed to establish link for PID #{os_pid}: #{inspect(e)}")
  end

  # --- OS Specifics (/proc scan) ---

  defp active_pids do
    case File.ls("/proc") do
      {:ok, items} ->
        items
        |> Enum.filter(&Regex.match?(~r/^\d+$/, &1))
        |> Enum.map(&String.to_integer/1)
      _ -> []
    end
  end

  defp get_executable_path(pid) do
    link_path = "/proc/#{pid}/exe"
    case File.read_link(link_path) do
      {:ok, path} -> {:ok, path}
      _ -> {:error, :not_accessible}
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: security.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Security do
  @moduledoc """
  Manages Access Control Lists (ACLs) and permission enforcement.

  CRITICAL UPDATE (Gap 11/12): Now supports structured ACL maps for granular control.
  """

  # Pre-defined system roles/IDs that always grant access
  @system_roles ["root", "system"]
  @public_role "public"

  @doc """
  Checks if an Agent ID has permission to perform a WRITE action on a specific structured ACL.
  """
  @spec can_write?(String.t(), map()) :: boolean()
  def can_write?(agent_id, acls) do
    # In a real system, this checks signatures or tokens.
    # For Version 2.0, we check strict membership in the :write list or 'root' privileges.

    write_list = Map.get(acls, :write, [])

    # Agent must be explicitly listed in the write ACLs or be a recognized system role
    Enum.member?(write_list, agent_id) || Enum.member?(@system_roles, agent_id)
  end

  @doc """
  Checks if an Agent ID has permission to perform a READ action on a specific structured ACL.
  """
  @spec can_read?(String.t(), map()) :: boolean()
  def can_read?(agent_id, acls) do
    read_list = Map.get(acls, :read, [])

    # Access is granted if:
    # 1. The node is publicly readable.
    # 2. The agent is explicitly listed in the read ACLs.
    # 3. The agent is a recognized system role (can read anything).
    Enum.member?(read_list, @public_role) || Enum.member?(read_list, agent_id) || Enum.member?(@system_roles, agent_id)
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: meta_node.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.MetaNode do
  @moduledoc """
  Factory for creating Meta-Nodes (nodes about nodes).
  """

  # FIX: Removed unused Edge alias
  alias CMS.Node

  @spec spawn_conflict(String.t(), map()) :: {:ok, CMS.Node.t()}
  def spawn_conflict(description, context_data) do
    head_text = "CONFLICT DETECTED: #{description}"
    data_head = CMS.DataHead.new(head_text)

    payload = %CMS.DataBodyPayload.Object{type: :object, object_type: :conflict_context, data: context_data}
    data_tail = CMS.DataTail.new("system://meta/conflict", "hash-placeholder", "application/x-cms-meta", 1.0)
    body = CMS.NodeBody.new(data_head, [payload], data_tail)

    {:ok, embedding} = CMS.Tool.Embedder.generate(head_text, "all-MiniLM-L6-v2")
    
    # Correctly derive UUID via content-addressability
    provenance = %{source: "CMS.IngestionEngine", method: "dialectical_merge"}
    uuid = CMS.NodeFactory.derive_content_addressable_id(body, provenance)
    
    head = CMS.NodeHead.new(uuid, embedding, "all-MiniLM-L6-v2", 0.99)
    antenna = CMS.NodeAntenna.new(uuid)
    
    {:ok, Node.new(head, body, antenna, provenance)}
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: ingestion_engine.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.IngestionEngine do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  alias CMS.{Node, LogAppender, NodeSupervisor, Security, MetaNode, Edge}

  @moduledoc """
  The Gatekeeper. Orchestrates the Zero-Friction Ingestion Protocol.

  Implements:
  - Gap 4: Conflict Detection & Trust-Based Arbitration.
  - Plan Step 7: Automated Association (Hebbian Priming).

  Responsibilities:
  1. Validate incoming data payloads (FIDs).
  2. Enforce Security (ACLs).
  3. Generate Embeddings & Salience Scores.
  4. Detect Conflicts via direct similarity calculation.
  5. Finalize Persistence (Log, Spawn).
  """

  # Configuration
  @min_associative_score 0.3
  @conflict_similarity_threshold 0.8
  @trust_supersede_threshold 0.2 # If new node is > 0.2 more trusted, it wins.


  def start_link(_opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, :ok, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @doc """
  Ingests a new atomic thought/fact into the CMS.

  Returns {:ok, node_id} or {:ok, :ingested_with_conflict_resolution, meta_id} or {:error, reason}.
  """
  def ingest(request) do
    # Timeout increased to 30s to allow for Embedding generation and Disk I/O
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:ingest, request}, 30_000)
  end

  # Callbacks

  @impl true
  def init(:ok) do
    {:ok, %{}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call({:ingest, req}, _from, state) do
    # Step 1: Validation & Security
    with :ok <- validate_payloads(req.description_payloads),
         true <- Security.can_write?(req.agent_id, req.acls || %{read: ["public"], write: ["system"]}) do

      # Step 2: Embedding & Identity Construction
      # We default to the standard model version
      {:ok, embedding} = CMS.Tool.Embedder.generate(req.fact_text, "all-MiniLM-L6-v2")

      node_uuid = CMS.Services.Identity.generate_uuid(req.fact_text) # Use content as path substitute? Or random.
      node_head = CMS.NodeHead.new(node_uuid, embedding, "all-MiniLM-L6-v2")
      data_head = CMS.DataHead.new(req.fact_text)

      # Calculate Salience (Hooked up from SalienceEngine)
      salience = CMS.SalienceEngine.calculate(req.fact_text, req.provenance || %{})

      # *** IMPLEMENTATION: HEBBIAN PRIMING (SCATTER-GATHER) ***
      # Fetch initial associative links *before* creating the DataTail
      # This connects the new node to the existing knowledge graph immediately.
      {:ok, initial_edges} = prime_initial_edges(embedding, "all-MiniLM-L6-v2")

      data_tail = CMS.DataTail.new(req.fact_text, "sha256-placeholder", "text/plain", salience)

      node_body = CMS.NodeBody.new(data_head, req.description_payloads, data_tail)

      # Create Node Struct (ID is derived here via Content Addressability)
      antenna = CMS.NodeAntenna.new(node_uuid)
      new_node = Node.new(node_head, node_body, antenna, req.provenance)
      
      # Attach initial semantic edges
      new_node = %{new_node | tail: %{new_node.tail | semantic: initial_edges}}

      # --- LOGGING: Inspect the DataTail to verify relationships ---
      Logger.info("[Ingestion] Node Created: #{new_node.id} | Relationships: #{length(initial_edges)}")
      Logger.debug("""
      [Ingestion] Constructed DataTail for new fact: "#{String.slice(req.fact_text, 0, 50)}..."
      Relationships: #{length(initial_edges)} found.
      DataTail Dump: #{inspect(data_tail)}
      """)
      # -------------------------------------------------------------

      # Step 3: Conflict Detection & Trust Arbitration (Fix 4)
      # We check if a very similar node already exists (optimized with Scatter-Gather)
      case check_for_conflict(new_node) do
        {:conflict, existing_id} ->
          # Trigger Trust-Based Arbitration
          result = resolve_conflict_with_arbitration(new_node, existing_id)
          {:reply, result, state}

        :ok ->
          # Step 4: Finalize & Activate
          result = finalize_ingestion(new_node)
          {:reply, result, state}
      end

    else
      false -> {:reply, {:error, :permission_denied}, state}
      error -> {:reply, error, state}
    end
  end

  # Internals

  defp validate_payloads(payloads) do
    Enum.reduce_while(payloads, :ok, fn payload, _acc ->
      case CMS.FIDValidator.validate(payload) do
        :ok -> {:cont, :ok}
        err -> {:halt, err}
      end
    end)
  end

  # --- FIX 1: Automated Association / Hebbian Priming Logic (Scatter-Gather) ---
  defp prime_initial_edges(embedding, _model_version) do
    # Use the unified EdgeGenerator logic for Scatter-Gather
    candidates = CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator.prime_candidates(embedding)

    # --- LOGGING: Relationship Calculation Debug ---
    Logger.info("""
    [Hebbian Priming] Scanned candidates.
    Candidates (score >= #{@min_associative_score}): #{length(candidates)}
    Top Matches: #{inspect(Enum.take(candidates, 3))}
    """)
    # -----------------------------------------------

    edges =
      candidates
      |> Enum.map(fn {target_id, score} ->
        # Use score as the initial weight for the semantic link
        Edge.new(target_id, :semantic, score)
      end)

    {:ok, edges}
  end
  # ------------------------------------------------------------------------


  defp check_for_conflict(new_node) do
    # We can reuse prime_candidates to find high-similarity pairs
    candidates = CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator.prime_candidates(new_node.head.embedding, new_node.id)
    
    # Conflict check: Look for any candidate >= 0.8
    case Enum.find(candidates, fn {_, score} -> score >= @conflict_similarity_threshold end) do
      {existing_id, _score} ->
        {:conflict, existing_id}
      _ ->
        :ok
    end
  end


  # --- FIX 4: Trust-Based Arbitration & Dialectical Merge ---
  defp resolve_conflict_with_arbitration(new_node, existing_id) do
    Logger.info("Potential conflict detected between New Node #{new_node.id} and Existing #{existing_id}")

    # 1. Fetch Provenance of Existing Node
    existing_trust = get_node_trust_score(existing_id)
    # FIX: Use Map.get safely for provenance as struct definition might vary
    new_provenance = Map.get(new_node, :provenance, %{})
    new_trust = Map.get(new_provenance, "trust_score", 0.5)

    trust_diff = new_trust - existing_trust

    cond do
      # Case A: New node is significantly more trusted -> SUPERSEDE
      trust_diff > @trust_supersede_threshold ->
        Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(CMS.PubSub, "global:signals",
          {:conflict_resolved, %{type: :superseded, winner: new_node.id, loser: existing_id}}
        )

        handle_supersedence(new_node, existing_id)

      # Case B: Trust is similar or Old node is trusted more -> DIALECTICAL MERGE
      true ->
        Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(CMS.PubSub, "global:signals",
          {:conflict_resolved, %{type: :dialectical_merge, node_a: new_node.id, node_b: existing_id}}
        )

        handle_dialectical_merge(new_node, existing_id)
    end
  end

  defp get_node_trust_score(node_id) do
    # Attempt to get trust score from active actor
    case NodeSupervisor.get_node_pid(node_id) do
      nil ->
        # Node not active, fetch from history (expensive fallback)
        case CMS.TemporalQueryEngine.get_node_state_at_time(node_id, DateTime.utc_now()) do
          {:ok, node} ->
            prov = Map.get(node, :provenance, %{})
            Map.get(prov, "trust_score", 0.5)
          _ -> 0.5
        end
      pid ->
        # FIX: Explicit GenServer Call to the NodeActor
        try do
          GenServer.call(pid, :get_trust_score, 1000)
        catch
          # If node crashes or times out during arbitration, default to neutral
          :exit, _ -> 0.5
          _, _ -> 0.5
        end
    end
  end

  defp handle_supersedence(new_node, existing_id) do
    Logger.info("Arbitration: New Node #{new_node.id} supersedes #{existing_id} (Trust Diff > #{@trust_supersede_threshold})")

    # 1. Update Existing Node: Mark as superseded
    superseded_edge = Edge.new(new_node.id, :superseded_by, 1.0)

    if pid = NodeSupervisor.get_node_pid(existing_id) do
       GenServer.cast(pid, {:add_edge, superseded_edge})
       # Log the deprecation event
       LogAppender.append_node_event(existing_id, :node_updated, %{reason: "superseded", by: new_node.id})
    end

    # 2. Update New Node: Link back to old (lineage)
    supersedes_edge = Edge.new(existing_id, :supersedes, 1.0)

    updated_tail = CMS.NodeTail.add_edge(new_node.tail, supersedes_edge)
    final_node = %{new_node | tail: updated_tail}

    # 3. Finalize
    finalize_ingestion(final_node)
  end

  defp handle_dialectical_merge(new_node, existing_id) do
    Logger.warning("Arbitration: Ambiguous trust. Initiating Dialectical Merge between #{new_node.id} and #{existing_id}")

    # 1. Spawn MetaNode to document the conflict
    context = %{
      node_a: new_node.id,
      node_b: existing_id,
      reason: "High Semantic Similarity / Content Divergence"
    }
    {:ok, meta_node} = MetaNode.spawn_conflict("Semantic Conflict Detected", context)

    # 2. Modify New Node: Add edges to MetaNode and the Conflicting Node

    # Edge to MetaNode
    edge_to_meta = Edge.new(meta_node.id, :semantic, 0.9)

    # Edge to Conflicting Node (CRITICAL: Explicit Link Typing)
    edge_to_existing = Edge.new(existing_id, :contradicts, 1.0)

    # Filter out the conflicting node if it was accidentally primed as a semantic link
    # Remove any existing edges to the conflicting node from semantic edges
    filtered_semantic = Enum.filter(new_node.tail.semantic, fn edge -> edge.target_node_id != existing_id end)
    
    # Update tail with filtered edges plus new conflict edges
    updated_tail = %{new_node.tail | semantic: [edge_to_meta, edge_to_existing | filtered_semantic]}
    final_node = %{new_node | tail: updated_tail}

    # 3. Finalize MetaNode
    finalize_ingestion(meta_node)

    # Asynchronously update the *existing* node to link back
    if pid = NodeSupervisor.get_node_pid(existing_id) do
      # Edge from existing node to the new node
      GenServer.cast(pid, {:add_edge, Edge.new(final_node.id, :contradicts, 1.0)})
      # Edge from existing node to the meta-node
      GenServer.cast(pid, {:add_edge, Edge.new(meta_node.id, :semantic, 0.9)})
    end

    # Finalize the new conflicting node
    _ = finalize_ingestion(final_node)

    {:ok, :ingested_with_conflict_resolution, meta_node.id}
  end

  defp finalize_ingestion(node) do
    # 1. Persist to Epoch Log (Immutable History)
    LogAppender.append_node_event(node.id, :node_created, node)

    # 2. Spawn NodeActor (Active Memory) - No vector index needed in decentralized model
    case NodeSupervisor.start_child(node) do
      {:ok, _pid} -> {:ok, node.id}
      {:error, {:already_started, _}} -> {:ok, node.id} # Idempotency
      err -> err
    end
  end
end


================================================================================
