defmodule CMS.VectorRouter do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @moduledoc """
  Manages the Hierarchical Navigable Small Worlds (HNSW) index for vector embeddings.

  CRITICAL REMEDIATION:
  1. FIX: Updated 'add' and 'query' to pass Nx.Tensor directly to HNSWLib as required by the binding.
  2. FIX: Ensures 1D vectors are correctly converted to 2D batches (Nx.new_axis).
  3. FIX: Uses safer ID generation to prevent NIF integer overflow issues.
  4. QUERY FIX: Added logic to respect the `threshold` option.
  """

  # Configuration
  @base_index_dir "priv/data/hnsw_indices"
  @dim 384
  @max_elements 100_000
  @persist_interval :timer.minutes(5)
  @persist_threshold 100
  @id_map_table :vector_id_mapping

  # Supported Models
  @supported_models ["nomic-embed-text", "all-MiniLM-L6-v2", "custom-bert-v1"]
  @nominal_table :nominal_index

  defstruct [
    :indices,           # Map: %{model_version => index_ref}
    :insertion_count,   # Map: %{model_version => count}
    :dirty_models       # MapSet: Tracks models with unpersisted changes
  ]

  # --- Client API ---

  def find_by_name(name) do
    case :mnesia.dirty_read(@nominal_table, name) do
      [{_, ^name, uuid}] -> {:ok, uuid}
      _ -> {:error, :not_found}
    end
  end

  def register_name(name, uuid) do
    :mnesia.dirty_write({@nominal_table, name, uuid})
  end

  def resolve_path(path) do
    find_by_name(path)
  end


  def start_link(opts \\ []) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  def add_embedding(node_id, embedding, model_version) do
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:add, node_id, embedding, model_version}, 30_000)
  end

  def query(query_vector, model_version, opts \\ []) do
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:query, query_vector, model_version, opts}, 10_000)
  end

  def persist do
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, :persist)
  end

  @doc "Destroys all indices on disk. Hazardous."
  def clean_slate do
    File.rm_rf(@base_index_dir)
  end
  
  @doc "Optimized bulk insertion for Rehydration."
  def bulk_add(items, model_version) do
    # items is a list of {node_id, embedding_tensor}
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:bulk_add, items, model_version}, 60_000)
  end

  # --- Server Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(_opts) do
    # 1. Ensure Index directory exists
    File.mkdir_p!(@base_index_dir)

    # 2. Initialize Mnesia (Safe to call multiple times if handled)
    init_mnesia()

    # 3. Load or Create indices
    {initial_indices, initial_counts} =
      Enum.reduce(@supported_models, {[], []}, fn model, {indices_acc, counts_acc} ->
        path = index_file_path(model)
        
        case attempt_load_index(model, path) do
          {:ok, index, count} ->
            Logger.info("CMS.VectorRouter: Loaded existing index for #{model} (#{count} items).")
            {[{model, index} | indices_acc], [{model, count} | counts_acc]}
            
          {:error, :not_found} ->
            Logger.info("CMS.VectorRouter: No index found for #{model}. Creating fresh.")
            {:ok, index} = HNSWLib.Index.new(:cosine, @dim, @max_elements)
            {[{model, index} | indices_acc], [{model, 0} | counts_acc]}

          {:error, reason} ->
            Logger.error("CMS.VectorRouter: Failed to load index for #{model}: #{inspect(reason)}. Creating fresh.")
            File.rm(path)
            {:ok, index} = HNSWLib.Index.new(:cosine, @dim, @max_elements)
            {[{model, index} | indices_acc], [{model, 0} | counts_acc]}
        end
      end)

    Process.send_after(self(), :periodic_persist, @persist_interval)

    {:ok, %__MODULE__{
      indices: Map.new(initial_indices),
      insertion_count: Map.new(initial_counts),
      dirty_models: MapSet.new()
    }}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call({:add, node_id, embedding, model_version}, _from, state) do
    case Map.fetch(state.indices, model_version) do
      {:ok, index} when not is_nil(index) ->
        try do
          # CRITICAL FIX 1: Ensure embedding is a 2D Nx.Tensor (a batch of 1 vector)
          vector_tensor =
            case Nx.rank(embedding) do
              1 -> Nx.new_axis(embedding, 0) # Convert 1D [384] to 2D [1][384]
              _ -> embedding
            end

          # CRITICAL FIX 2: Dimension Check - use Nx.shape for a 2D tensor
          tensor_dim = elem(Nx.shape(vector_tensor), 1)

          if tensor_dim != @dim do
            Logger.error("Dimension mismatch: Expected #{@dim}, got #{tensor_dim}.")
            {:reply, {:error, :dimension_mismatch}, state}
          else
            # 2. Generate ID
            # FIX 3: Use sequential IDs to avoid exceeding max_elements (if it limits label range) and ensuring valid range.
            # Since we only add, insertion_count is roughly the next ID.
            current_id = Map.get(state.insertion_count, model_version, 0)
            item_id = current_id + 1

            # 3. Add to HNSW (Pass the Nx.Tensor directly)
            case HNSWLib.Index.add_items(index, vector_tensor, ids: [item_id]) do
              :ok ->
                :mnesia.dirty_write({@id_map_table, item_id, node_id})
                new_count = current_id + 1
                new_dirty = MapSet.put(state.dirty_models, model_version)

                if new_count >= @persist_threshold, do: send(self(), :periodic_persist)

                {:reply, :ok, %{state | insertion_count: Map.put(state.insertion_count, model_version, new_count), dirty_models: new_dirty}}

              {:error, reason} ->
                Logger.error("HNSWLib.add_items failed: #{inspect(reason)}")
                {:reply, {:error, :add_failed}, state}
            end
          end
        rescue
          e ->
            Logger.error("VectorRouter Crash on Add: #{inspect(e)}")
            {:reply, {:error, :add_crashed}, state}
        catch
          :exit, reason ->
             Logger.error("VectorRouter NIF Crash (Exit): #{inspect(reason)}")
             {:reply, {:error, :nif_crash}, state}
        end

      _ ->
        {:reply, {:error, :unsupported_model}, state}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call({:bulk_add, items, model_version}, _from, state) do
    case Map.fetch(state.indices, model_version) do
      {:ok, index} when not is_nil(index) ->
        # 1. Prepare batch
        # items is [{node_id, tensor}]
        # Need to separate into a Tensor Batch and a List of IDs
        
        {ids, tensors} = Enum.reduce(items, {[], []}, fn {node_id, tensor}, {id_acc, t_acc} ->
             # Generate ID
             item_id = :erlang.phash2(node_id, 2_147_483_647)
             
             # Shape Check
             t = case Nx.rank(tensor) do
               1 -> Nx.new_axis(tensor, 0)
               _ -> tensor
             end
             
             {[item_id | id_acc], [t | t_acc]}
        end)
        
        # Nx.concatenate requires same-shaped tensors. 
        # Since we new_axis'd them to [1, 384], concat on axis 0 gives [N, 384].
        if Enum.empty?(tensors) do
           {:reply, :ok, state}
        else
           # Reverse to maintain order (reduce prepends)
           final_tensors = Enum.reverse(tensors) |> Nx.concatenate(axis: 0)
           final_ids = Enum.reverse(ids)
           
           # Direct HNSW Bulk Add
           case HNSWLib.Index.add_items(index, final_tensors, ids: final_ids) do
             :ok ->
               # Bulk Write Mnesia Mappings
               mappings = Enum.zip(final_ids, Enum.map(items, fn {nid, _} -> nid end))
               
               :mnesia.transaction(fn -> 
                  Enum.each(mappings, fn {iid, nid} ->
                    :mnesia.write({@id_map_table, iid, nid})
                  end)
               end)
               
               new_count = Map.get(state.insertion_count, model_version, 0) + length(items)
               new_dirty = MapSet.put(state.dirty_models, model_version)
               
               {:reply, :ok, %{state | insertion_count: Map.put(state.insertion_count, model_version, new_count), dirty_models: new_dirty}}
               
             {:error, reason} ->
                Logger.error("HNSW Bulk Add Failed: #{inspect(reason)}")
                {:reply, {:error, reason}, state}
           end
        end
        
      _ ->
        {:reply, {:error, :unsupported_model}, state}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call({:query, query_vector, model_version, opts}, _from, state) do
    case Map.fetch(state.indices, model_version) do
      {:ok, index} when not is_nil(index) ->
        try do
          k = Keyword.get(opts, :k, 10)
          current_count = Map.get(state.insertion_count, model_version, 0)
          
          # CRITICAL FIX: Cap k by current_count to prevent HNSWLib error
          k = min(k, current_count)

          threshold = Keyword.get(opts, :threshold, 0.0)

          # CRITICAL FIX 4: Pass the Nx.Tensor directly
          query_tensor =
            case Nx.rank(query_vector) do
              1 -> Nx.new_axis(query_vector, 0)
              _ -> query_vector
            end

          # Optimization: Check if index is empty
          if current_count == 0 do
             {:reply, {:ok, []}, state}
          else
            # HNSWLib.Index.knn_query expects a 2D Nx.Tensor and returns 1D Nx.Tensors
            case HNSWLib.Index.knn_query(index, query_tensor, k: k) do
              {:ok, labels_tensor, distances_tensor} ->
                # Convert Nx.Tensors to lists
                flat_labels = Nx.to_flat_list(labels_tensor)
                flat_dists = Nx.to_flat_list(distances_tensor)

                results =
                  Enum.zip(flat_labels, flat_dists)
                  # HNSWLib returns distance. For cosine, similarity = 1.0 - distance.
                  |> Enum.map(fn {label, dist} -> {label, 1.0 - dist} end)
                  |> Enum.filter(fn {_label, similarity} -> similarity >= threshold end)
                  |> Enum.map(fn {int_id, similarity} ->
                    case :mnesia.dirty_read(@id_map_table, int_id) do
                      [{_, ^int_id, uuid}] -> {uuid, similarity}
                      _ -> nil
                    end
                  end)
                  |> Enum.reject(&is_nil/1)

                {:reply, {:ok, results}, state}

              {:error, msg} ->
                if is_binary(msg) and String.contains?(msg, "too small"), do: {:reply, {:ok, []}, state}, else: {:reply, {:error, msg}, state}

              nil ->
                {:reply, {:ok, []}, state}

              other ->
                Logger.error("VectorRouter: Unknown NIF return: #{inspect(other)}")
                {:reply, {:ok, []}, state}
            end
          end
        rescue
          e ->
            Logger.error("VectorRouter Query Crash: #{inspect(e)}")
            {:reply, {:ok, []}, state}
        end

      _ ->
        {:reply, {:ok, []}, state}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call(:persist, _from, state) do
    Enum.each(state.indices, fn {model, index} -> save_to_disk(model, index) end)
    {:reply, :ok, %{state | dirty_models: MapSet.new(), insertion_count: Map.new()}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:periodic_persist, state) do
    Enum.each(MapSet.to_list(state.dirty_models), fn model ->
      if index = Map.get(state.indices, model) do
        save_to_disk(model, index)
      end
    end)
    Process.send_after(self(), :periodic_persist, @persist_interval)
    {:noreply, %{state | dirty_models: MapSet.new(), insertion_count: Map.new()}}
  end

  # --- Internals ---

  defp attempt_load_index(_model, path) do
    if File.exists?(path) do
      case HNSWLib.Index.load_index(:cosine, @dim, path, max_elements: @max_elements) do
        {:ok, index} ->
          count = :mnesia.async_dirty(fn -> 
            :mnesia.table_info(@id_map_table, :size)
          end)
          {:ok, index, count}
        {:error, reason} -> {:error, reason}
      end
    else
      {:error, :not_found}
    end
  end

  defp init_mnesia do
    nodes = [Node.self()]
    :mnesia.create_table(@id_map_table, [
      attributes: [:int_id, :uuid],
      disc_copies: nodes,
      type: :set
    ])
    :mnesia.create_table(@nominal_table, [
      attributes: [:name, :uuid],
      disc_copies: nodes,
      type: :set
    ])
    :mnesia.wait_for_tables([@id_map_table, @nominal_table], 5000)
  end


  defp save_to_disk(model_version, index) do
    path = index_file_path(model_version)
    if index do
      HNSWLib.Index.save_index(index, path)
    end
  end

  defp index_file_path(model_version) do
    File.mkdir_p!(@base_index_dir)
    Path.join(@base_index_dir, "#{String.replace(model_version, "-", "_")}.hnsw.bin")
  end

  # Removed: to_flat_list_sanitized/1 as it is no longer needed.
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: epoch_manager.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.EpochManager do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @moduledoc """
  Manages the lifecycle of Epoch Log files and the Mnesia index.

  Responsibilities:
  1. Creates new Epoch Log files (rotation).
  2. Updates Mnesia with metadata (start_time, end_time, path).
  3. Ensures proper closure of old logs.
  """

  # Mnesia Table Definition
  @table_name :epoch_log_index
  @nodes_table :nodes_table
  @vector_index :vector_index

  @log_dir "priv/data/epochs"
  @rotation_interval :timer.hours(1)
  @max_size_bytes 100 * 1024 * 1024 # 100MB

  defstruct [:current_file_path, :current_log_id, :file_handle, :bytes_written, :rotation_timer]

  # API

  def start_link(_opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, :ok, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  def get_active_handle do
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, :get_active_handle)
  end

  def notify_bytes_written(count) do
    GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, {:bytes_written, count})
  end

  def rotate_log do
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, :rotate)
  end

  def list_epoch_files do
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, :list_files)
  end

  # Callbacks

  @impl true
  def init(:ok) do
    # Initialize Mnesia Schema and Directory
    init_mnesia()
    File.mkdir_p!(@log_dir)

    # Open first log
    state = start_new_epoch()

    # Schedule time-based rotation
    timer = Process.send_after(self(), :time_rotate, @rotation_interval)

    {:ok, %{state | rotation_timer: timer}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call(:get_active_handle, _from, state) do
    {:reply, state.file_handle, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call(:rotate, _from, state) do
    new_state = rotate_epoch(state)
    {:reply, :ok, new_state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call(:list_files, _from, state) do
    # Fetch all epochs from Mnesia, sorted by start time
    files = :mnesia.transaction(fn ->
      :mnesia.match_object({@table_name, :_, :_, :_, :_})
    end)
    
    sorted_paths = case files do
      {:atomic, list} ->
        list
        |> Enum.sort_by(fn {_, _, start_time, _, _} -> start_time end, DateTime)
        |> Enum.map(fn {_, _, _, _, path} -> path end)
      _ -> []
    end
    
    {:reply, sorted_paths, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:bytes_written, count}, state) do
    new_bytes = state.bytes_written + count
    if new_bytes >= @max_size_bytes do
      Logger.info("EpochManager: Max size reached. Rotating log.")
      {:noreply, rotate_epoch(state)}
    else
      {:noreply, %{state | bytes_written: new_bytes}}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:time_rotate, state) do
    Logger.info("EpochManager: Time interval reached. Rotating log.")
    new_state = rotate_epoch(state)
    timer = Process.send_after(self(), :time_rotate, @rotation_interval)
    {:noreply, %{new_state | rotation_timer: timer}}
  end

  # Internals

  defp init_mnesia do
    # 1. Get Directory from Config
    raw_dir = Application.get_env(:mnesia, :dir) || "priv/data/mnesia_store"

    # FIX: Convert binary string to charlist for Erlang Mnesia compatibility.
    # config.exs usually supplies a binary string, which causes :function_clause errors in Mnesia.
    mnesia_dir = if is_binary(raw_dir), do: String.to_charlist(raw_dir), else: raw_dir

    # 2. Ensure the directory exists on disk (using binary path for Elixir File module)
    File.mkdir_p!(to_string(mnesia_dir))

    # 3. Update Application Env to force Mnesia to use this Charlist path
    Application.put_env(:mnesia, :dir, mnesia_dir)

    nodes = [Node.self()]

    # 4. Stop Mnesia to perform schema operations
    :mnesia.stop()

    # 5. Create Schema
    case :mnesia.create_schema(nodes) do
      :ok ->
        Logger.info("Mnesia schema created on disk at #{mnesia_dir}.")
      {:error, {_, {:already_exists, _}}} ->
        Logger.info("Mnesia schema already exists.")
      error ->
        Logger.error("Mnesia create_schema failed: #{inspect(error)}")
    end

    # 6. Start Mnesia
    :mnesia.start()

    # 7. Create the Table
    case :mnesia.create_table(@table_name, [
      attributes: [:id, :start_time, :end_time, :file_path],
      disc_copies: nodes
    ]) do
      {:atomic, :ok} -> Logger.info("Mnesia table #{@table_name} created.")
      {:aborted, {:already_exists, _}} -> :ok
      other -> Logger.error("Mnesia create_table failed: #{inspect(other)}")
    end

    # 8. Create Nodes Table
    case :mnesia.create_table(@nodes_table, [
      attributes: [:uuid, :path_hash, :parent_uuid, :head_data, :body_data, :tail_data, :chrono_stack, :provenance, :antenna_data],
      disc_copies: nodes
    ]) do
      {:atomic, :ok} -> Logger.info("Mnesia table #{@nodes_table} created.")
      {:aborted, {:already_exists, _}} -> :ok
      other -> Logger.error("Mnesia create_table nodes_table failed: #{inspect(other)}")
    end

    # 9. Create Vector Index Table
    case :mnesia.create_table(@vector_index, [
      attributes: [:id, :vector],
      disc_copies: nodes,
      type: :ordered_set # For auto-increment simulation if needed
    ]) do
      {:atomic, :ok} -> Logger.info("Mnesia table #{@vector_index} created.")
      {:aborted, {:already_exists, _}} -> :ok
      other -> Logger.error("Mnesia create_table vector_index failed: #{inspect(other)}")
    end
  end

  defp start_new_epoch do
    id = UUID.uuid4()
    timestamp = DateTime.utc_now()
    filename = "epoch_#{DateTime.to_iso8601(timestamp) |> String.replace(":", "-")}_#{id}.jsonl"
    path = Path.join(@log_dir, filename)

    {:ok, handle} = File.open(path, [:append, :utf8])

    :mnesia.transaction(fn ->
      :mnesia.write({@table_name, id, timestamp, :infinity, path})
    end)

    Logger.info("Started new Epoch Log: #{path}")

    %__MODULE__{
      current_file_path: path,
      current_log_id: id,
      file_handle: handle,
      bytes_written: 0
    }
  end

  defp rotate_epoch(state) do
    File.close(state.file_handle)

    now = DateTime.utc_now()
    old_id = state.current_log_id

    :mnesia.transaction(fn ->
      [{_tag, _id, start, _end, path}] = :mnesia.read(@table_name, old_id)
      :mnesia.write({@table_name, old_id, start, now, path})
    end)

    start_new_epoch()
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: log_appender.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.LogAppender do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @moduledoc """
  High-throughput append-only logger for the CMS.

  Receives:
  1. Node Snapshots (Create/Update) - CRITICAL: Stores full state for Chrono Stack.
  2. Hebbian Updates (Link weights)
  3. System/Failure Events (Query failures, etc.)

  Writes to the active file handle provided by EpochManager.
  """

  @buffer_size 50 # Flush after 50 items
  @flush_interval 1000 # Or every 1 second

  def start_link(_opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, :ok, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  # Client API

  @doc """
  Logs a full Node snapshot.
  Type: :node_created | :node_updated | :node_decayed

  CRITICAL PATCH: Now accepts the *full* CMS.Node.t() for all updates,
  ensuring the log is a full Chrono Stack history.
  """
  @spec append_node_event(CMS.Node.id(), atom(), CMS.Node.t() | map()) :: :ok
  def append_node_event(node_id, type, %CMS.Node{} = full_node) when type in [:node_created, :node_updated, :node_migrated] do
    GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, {:log, %{
      timestamp: DateTime.utc_now(),
      entity: :node,
      id: node_id,
      type: type,
      # Store the *entire node struct* for full Chrono Stack auditability (Plan Sec 9)
      data: full_node
    }})
  end

  # Special case for node_fired and node_decayed, which are state changes but don't require the full struct to be logged.
  def append_node_event(node_id, type, data) when type in [:node_fired, :node_decayed] and is_map(data) do
    GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, {:log, %{
      timestamp: DateTime.utc_now(),
      entity: :node,
      id: node_id,
      type: type,
      data: data
    }})
  end

  @doc """
  Logs a batch of Hebbian weight updates.
  """
  def append_hebbian_updates(updates) do
    GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, {:log, %{
      timestamp: DateTime.utc_now(),
      entity: :hebbian,
      data: updates
    }})
  end

  # --- NEW: System Event/Failure Reporting API ---
  @doc """
  Logs a critical system event, such as a query failure or system congestion.
  """
  @spec append_system_event(atom(), map()) :: :ok
  def append_system_event(type, data) do
    GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, {:log, %{
      timestamp: DateTime.utc_now(),
      entity: :system,
      type: type,
      data: data
    }})
  end

  # Server Callbacks
  # ... (init, handle_cast, handle_info, flush_buffer remain unchanged)

  @impl true
  def init(:ok) do
    Process.send_after(self(), :flush, @flush_interval)
    {:ok, %{buffer: []}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:log, entry}, state) do
    new_buffer = [entry | state.buffer]
    if length(new_buffer) >= @buffer_size do
      flush_buffer(new_buffer)
      {:noreply, %{buffer: []}}
    else
      {:noreply, %{buffer: new_buffer}}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:flush, state) do
    unless Enum.empty?(state.buffer) do
      flush_buffer(state.buffer)
    end
    Process.send_after(self(), :flush, @flush_interval)
    {:noreply, %{buffer: []}}
  end

  defp flush_buffer(buffer) do
    # Get handle from EpochManager (Step 7.1)
    file = CMS.EpochManager.get_active_handle()

    # 1. Reverse buffer (since we prepended)
    # 2. Serialize to JSONL
    data_chunk =
      buffer
      |> Enum.reverse()
      |> Enum.map(&Jason.encode!/1)
      |> Enum.join("\n")

    # 3. Write newline at end
    blob = data_chunk <> "\n"

    case IO.write(file, blob) do
      :ok ->
        CMS.EpochManager.notify_bytes_written(byte_size(blob))
      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.error("LogAppender failed to write to epoch log: #{inspect(reason)}")
        # In prod, we might buffer back or crash to ensure no data loss
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: temporal_query_engine.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.TemporalQueryEngine do
  @moduledoc """
  Provides querying capabilities for the immutable history of the CMS.

  Implements Gap 3: Chrono-Stack & Temporal Queries.
  It queries the Mnesia index to find relevant Epoch Logs, then streams those logs
  to reconstruct the state of a node at a specific timestamp.

  CRITICAL PATCH: Uses deep merging to correctly apply partial node updates.
  """

  require Logger
  @table_name :epoch_log_index

  @doc """
  Retrieves the full state of a Node as it existed at `target_time`.
  """
  @spec get_node_state_at_time(String.t(), DateTime.t()) :: {:ok, map()} | {:error, any()} # Returns map as full struct conversion is complex
  def get_node_state_at_time(node_id, target_time) do
    # 1. Find relevant epoch files from Mnesia
    files = find_epoch_files_before(target_time)

    if Enum.empty?(files) do
      {:error, :no_history_found}
    else
      # 2. Reconstruct State
      reconstruct_node(node_id, files, target_time)
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Reconstructs the ENTIRE state of the knowledge graph at a specific point in time.
  Used for Temporal Vector Search.
  """
  @spec get_system_state_at_time(DateTime.t()) :: {:ok, [map()]} | {:error, any()}
  def get_system_state_at_time(target_time) do
    files = find_epoch_files_before(target_time)

    if Enum.empty?(files) do
      {:ok, []} # No history means empty system
    else
      reconstruct_system_state(files, target_time)
    end
  end

  defp find_epoch_files_before(target_time) do
    matcher = :mnesia.transaction(fn ->
      # FIX: Prefixed unused 'id' with underscore
      :mnesia.foldl(fn {_tag, _id, start, _end, path}, acc ->
        if DateTime.compare(start, target_time) != :gt do
          [{start, path} | acc]
        else
          acc
        end
      end, [], @table_name)
    end)

    case matcher do
      {:atomic, result} ->
        # Sort by start_time ascending
        result
        |> Enum.sort_by(fn {start, _} -> start end, DateTime)
        |> Enum.map(fn {_, path} -> path end)
      _ -> []
    end
  end

  defp reconstruct_node(node_id, file_paths, target_time) do
    # Initialize accumulator to nil (meaning node not found yet)
    initial_state = nil

    # Reduce through files (oldest -> newest)
    final_state = Enum.reduce(file_paths, initial_state, fn path, acc_node ->
      if File.exists?(path) do
        File.stream!(path)
        |> Stream.map(&Jason.decode!/1)
        |> Enum.reduce(acc_node, fn entry, curr_node ->
          # Check if this log entry is relevant to our node and time
          if entry["entity"] == "node" and entry["id"] == node_id and Map.has_key?(entry, "data") do
            entry_ts = parse_ts(entry["timestamp"])

            if DateTime.compare(entry_ts, target_time) != :gt do
              apply_event(curr_node, entry)
            else
              curr_node # Skip events after target_time
            end
          else
            curr_node # Skip non-node or non-matching ID entries
          end
        end)
      else
        acc_node # Skip file if not found
      end
    end)

    if final_state do
      Logger.debug("TemporalQueryEngine reconstructed state for #{node_id} at #{DateTime.to_iso8601(target_time)}.")
      {:ok, final_state}
    else
      {:error, :node_not_found_at_timestamp}
    end
  end

  defp reconstruct_system_state(file_paths, target_time) do
    # Accumulator is a Map: %{node_id => node_data}
    final_map =
      Enum.reduce(file_paths, %{}, fn path, acc_system ->
        if File.exists?(path) do
          File.stream!(path)
          |> Stream.map(&Jason.decode!/1)
          |> Enum.reduce(acc_system, fn entry, curr_system ->
            apply_system_event(curr_system, entry, target_time)
          end)
        else
          acc_system
        end
      end)

    {:ok, Map.values(final_map)}
  end

  defp parse_ts(iso_string) do
    case DateTime.from_iso8601(iso_string) do
      {:ok, dt, _} -> dt
      _ -> raise "Invalid timestamp format in epoch log: #{iso_string}"
    end
  end

  # --- New Function: Recursive Deep Map Merge ---
  # FIX: Removed @doc attribute for private function
  defp deep_merge_recursive(v1, v2) when is_map(v1) and is_map(v2) do
    Map.merge(v1, v2, fn _key, val1, val2 -> deep_merge_recursive(val1, val2) end)
  end
  defp deep_merge_recursive(_v1, v2), do: v2
  # ----------------------------------------------


  # Event Application Logic (The "Replay")

  # If it's a creation event, the data is the full node map.
  defp apply_event(_curr, %{"type" => "node_created", "data" => data}), do: data

  # --- LOGIC REFACTOR: Use deep merge for updates/migrations ---
  # If it's an update event, the data is either a delta or a full snapshot.
  # We use deep merge to correctly integrate the data, maintaining fields not present in 'data'.
  defp apply_event(curr, %{"type" => type, "data" => data}) when type in ["node_updated", "node_migrated"] do
    if curr != nil do
      # Deep merge the current state with the incoming update/delta map
      deep_merge_recursive(curr, data)
    else
      Logger.warning("TemporalQueryEngine encountered a #{type} event for non-existent node. Ignoring.")
      curr
    end
  end

  # Ignore other events (like hebbian updates which modify state *during* runtime)
  defp apply_event(curr, _), do: curr

  defp apply_system_event(system_map, %{"entity" => "node"} = entry, target_time) do
    entry_ts = parse_ts(entry["timestamp"])

    # Stop processing if event is in the future relative to target
    if DateTime.compare(entry_ts, target_time) == :gt do
      system_map
    else
      node_id = entry["id"]
      type = entry["type"]
      data = entry["data"]

      case type do
        "node_created" ->
          Map.put(system_map, node_id, data)

        "node_updated" ->
          Map.update(system_map, node_id, data, fn existing ->
             deep_merge_recursive(existing, data)
          end)

        "node_migrated" ->
           Map.update(system_map, node_id, data, fn existing ->
             deep_merge_recursive(existing, data)
           end)

        "node_decayed" ->
           # If decayed/deleted, remove from the snapshot
           Map.delete(system_map, node_id)

        _ -> system_map
      end
    end
  end

  defp apply_system_event(system_map, _, _), do: system_map
  
  # ============================================================================
  # CHRONO-FORENSICS FEATURES
  # ============================================================================
  
  @doc """
  Feature 1: Stack Traversal - Query node history with depth limit.
  
  Returns the last N snapshots from a node's ChronoStack.
  """
  @spec get_node_history(String.t(), keyword()) :: {:ok, [map()]} | {:error, any()}
  def get_node_history(node_id, opts \\ []) do
    depth = Keyword.get(opts, :depth, 10)
    
    # Fetch current node state from Mnesia
    case :mnesia.dirty_read(:nodes_table, node_id) do
      [{_table, _uuid, _hash, _parent, _head, body, _tail, _stack}] ->
        # Extract chrono_stack from body.data_ledger
        chrono_stack = get_in(body, [:data_ledger, :chrono_stack]) || %{snapshots: []}
        snapshots = chrono_stack[:snapshots] || chrono_stack["snapshots"] || []
        
        # Take last N snapshots
        limited_snapshots = Enum.take(snapshots, depth)
        
        # Format snapshots
        formatted = Enum.map(limited_snapshots, fn snapshot ->
          %{
            timestamp: snapshot[:timestamp] || snapshot["timestamp"],
            hash: snapshot[:hash] || snapshot["hash"],
            cause: snapshot[:cause] || snapshot["cause"],
            diff: snapshot[:diff] || snapshot["diff"]
          }
        end)
        
        {:ok, formatted}
      
      [] ->
        {:error, :node_not_found}
    end
  end
  
  @doc """
  Feature 2: Differential Diagnosis - Find all nodes mutated in time range.
  
  Scans all nodes for mutations within the specified time range.
  Returns list of {node_id, path, last_mutation, cause}.
  """
  @spec find_recent_mutations(integer()) :: {:ok, [map()]} | {:error, any()}
  def find_recent_mutations(minutes_ago) do
    cutoff_time = DateTime.add(DateTime.utc_now(), -minutes_ago * 60, :second)
    
    # Scan all nodes in Mnesia using transaction
    result = :mnesia.transaction(fn ->
      :mnesia.foldl(fn {_table, uuid, _hash, _parent, _head, body, _tail, _stack}, acc ->
        last_mutation = get_in(body, [:data_ledger, :last_mutation])
        
        if last_mutation && DateTime.compare(last_mutation, cutoff_time) == :gt do
          path = get_in(body, [:data_ledger, :physical_symlink]) || "unknown"
          
          # Get mutation cause from chrono_stack
          chrono_stack = get_in(body, [:data_ledger, :chrono_stack]) || %{snapshots: []}
          snapshots = chrono_stack[:snapshots] || chrono_stack["snapshots"] || []
          cause = if length(snapshots) > 0 do
            latest = List.first(snapshots)
            latest[:cause] || latest["cause"] || "unknown"
          else
            "genesis"
          end
          
          mutation = %{
            node_id: uuid,
            path: path,
            last_mutation: last_mutation,
            mutation_cause: cause,
            time_ago: format_time_ago(last_mutation)
          }
          
          [mutation | acc]
        else
          acc
        end
      end, [], :nodes_table)
    end)
    
    case result do
      {:atomic, mutations} ->
        # Sort by most recent first
        sorted = Enum.sort_by(mutations, fn m -> m.last_mutation end, {:desc, DateTime})
        {:ok, sorted}
      {:aborted, reason} ->
        {:error, reason}
    end
  end
  
  @doc """
  Feature 3: Directory State Reconstruction - Virtual view at point in time.
  
  Reconstructs how a directory looked at a specific timestamp.
  """
  @spec reconstruct_directory_state(String.t(), DateTime.t()) :: {:ok, [map()]} | {:error, any()}
  def reconstruct_directory_state(dir_path, target_time) do
    # 1. Find directory node by path
    dir_uuid = UUID.uuid5(:oid, dir_path)
    
    # 2. Get directory's children from epoch logs at that time
    case get_node_state_at_time(dir_uuid, target_time) do
      {:ok, dir_node} ->
        # Extract children from hierarchical edges
        tail = dir_node["tail"] || %{}
        hierarchical = tail["hierarchical"] || []
        
        child_ids = hierarchical
        |> Enum.filter(fn edge -> edge["type"] == "parent_of" end)
        |> Enum.map(fn edge -> edge["target_node_id"] end)
        
        # 3. Reconstruct each child's state at target_time
        children = Enum.map(child_ids, fn child_id ->
          case get_node_state_at_time(child_id, target_time) do
            {:ok, child_node} ->
              %{
                node_id: child_id,
                path: get_in(child_node, ["body", "data_ledger", "physical_symlink"]),
                name: Path.basename(get_in(child_node, ["body", "data_ledger", "physical_symlink"]) || "unknown"),
                mode: get_in(child_node, ["body", "mode"]),
                hash: get_in(child_node, ["body", "data_ledger", "integrity_hash"])
              }
            _ -> nil
          end
        end)
        |> Enum.filter(&(&1 != nil))
        
        {:ok, %{
          directory: dir_path,
          timestamp: target_time,
          children: children,
          count: length(children)
        }}
      
      {:error, reason} ->
        {:error, reason}
    end
  end
  
  # Helper to format time ago
  defp format_time_ago(dt) do
    seconds_ago = DateTime.diff(DateTime.utc_now(), dt, :second)
    cond do
      seconds_ago < 60 -> "#{seconds_ago} seconds ago"
      seconds_ago < 3600 -> "#{div(seconds_ago, 60)} minutes ago"
      seconds_ago < 86400 -> "#{div(seconds_ago, 3600)} hours ago"
      true -> "#{div(seconds_ago, 86400)} days ago"
    end
  end
  
  
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: bus_pipeline.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.BusPipeline do
  use Broadway
  require Logger

  @moduledoc """
  The Back-Pressure Pipeline for the Cognitive Bus.

  Implements Gap 8.
  Consumes events from CMS.QueryRouter and broadcasts them to
  Semantic Regions via Phoenix.PubSub.
  """

  def start_link(_opts) do
    Broadway.start_link(__MODULE__,
      name: __MODULE__,
      producer: [
        module: {CMS.QueryRouter, []},
        concurrency: 1,
        # REMEDIATION: Transformer added to wrap raw events in Broadway.Message structs
        transformer: {__MODULE__, :transform_entry, []}
      ],
      processors: [
        default: [
          concurrency: 50, # Max 50 concurrent broadcasts allowed
          max_demand: 10   # Prefetch 10 items per processor
        ]
      ],
      batchers: [
        default: [
          batch_size: 10,
          batch_timeout: 50
        ]
      ]
    )
  end

  @doc """
  Transforms raw events from the producer into Broadway Messages.
  Required because CMS.QueryRouter emits raw tuples, not structs.
  """
  def transform_entry(event, _opts) do
    %Broadway.Message{
      data: event,
      acknowledger: {Broadway.NoopAcknowledger, nil, nil}
    }
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_message(_processor, message, _context) do
    {query_context, target_regions} = message.data

    # FIX: Utilize the variables for observability instead of ignoring them
    Logger.metadata(
      query_id: query_context[:query_id],
      target_region_count: length(target_regions)
    )

    Broadway.Message.put_batcher(message, :default)
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_batch(:default, messages, _batch_info, _context) do
    # Execute the broadcasts
    Enum.each(messages, fn message ->
      {query_context, target_regions} = message.data
      do_broadcast(query_context, target_regions)
    end)

    # Clean up metadata
    Logger.metadata(query_id: nil, target_region_count: nil)

    messages
  end

  defp do_broadcast(query_context, target_regions) do
    query_id = Map.get(query_context, :query_id)

    # Broadcast to each target semantic region
    Enum.each(target_regions, fn region_id ->
      topic = "region:#{region_id}"

      # 1. The payload is the query context
      # NodeActors subscribed to this topic will wake up
      Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(
        CMS.PubSub,
        topic,
        {:query, query_context}
      )

      # 2. CRITICAL FIX: Notify Coordinator that this region has been contacted.
      # Without this, QueryCoordinator waits for 10s timeout (Status 504).
      if query_id do
        CMS.QueryCoordinator.region_complete(query_id, region_id)
      end
    end)
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: broadcast_coordinator.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.BroadcastCoordinator do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @moduledoc """
  Coordinates the broadcasting of queries to ALL nodes in the system.
  
  This coordinator implements the decentralized cognitive model where every node
  receives every query and makes its own autonomous decision about relevance.
  """

  def start_link(opts \\ []) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @doc """
  Broadcasts a query to all nodes in the system.
  """
  def broadcast_query(query_context) do
    GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, {:broadcast_query, query_context})
  end

  @doc """
  Broadcasts a node's semantic profile for others to 'Gather' relevance.
  """
  def scatter_semantic_profile(source_uuid, embedding) do
    GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, {:scatter_semantic_profile, source_uuid, embedding})
  end

  def init(_opts) do
    {:ok, %{}}
  end

  def handle_cast({:broadcast_query, query_context}, state) do
    # Get all active node PIDs and broadcast the query to each one
    all_pids = CMS.NodeSupervisor.get_all_active_node_pids()
    
    Enum.each(all_pids, fn pid ->
      send(pid, {:query, query_context})
    end)
    
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  def handle_cast({:scatter_semantic_profile, source_uuid, embedding}, state) do
    # Broadcast to a specific PubSub topic for semantic gathering
    Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(CMS.PubSub, "semantic_gather", {:semantic_scatter, source_uuid, embedding})
    {:noreply, state}
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: rehydrator.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Recovery.Rehydrator do
  require Logger
  alias CMS.VectorRouter

  @moduledoc """
  The "Builder". Executes the protocol to wipe incompatible caches and rebuild
  state from the immutable Epoch Logs (Source of Truth).
  """

  def rehydrate do
    Logger.warning("REHYDRATOR: Beginning Scorched Earth Protocol...")

    # 1. Purge Caches (Destructive)
    purge_caches()

    # 2. Re-Initialize Infrastructure
    Logger.info("REHYDRATOR: Temporarily starting persistence layer for restoration...")
    
    # Ensure mnesia is stopped before we try starting it for re-init
    :mnesia.stop()
    :mnesia.start()
    
    # We use start_link to ensure they are up
    {:ok, _} = CMS.EpochManager.start_link([])
    {:ok, _} = CMS.VectorRouter.start_link([])
    
    # 3. Stream & Reconstruct
    epoch_dir = "priv/data/epochs"
    File.mkdir_p!(epoch_dir)
    epoch_files = 
      File.ls!(epoch_dir)
      |> Enum.filter(&String.ends_with?(&1, ".jsonl"))
      |> Enum.sort() 
      |> Enum.map(&Path.join(epoch_dir, &1))

    Logger.info("REHYDRATOR: Found #{length(epoch_files)} epoch files. Starting reconstruction stream.")
    
    stats = Enum.reduce(epoch_files, %{nodes: 0, vectors: 0, errors: 0}, fn path, acc ->
       process_epoch_file(path, acc)
    end)
    
    Logger.info("REHYDRATOR: Reconstruction Complete. Stats: #{inspect(stats)}")
    
    # 4. Commit to Disk
    VectorRouter.persist()
    
    # 5. Cleanup
    GenServer.stop(CMS.EpochManager)
    GenServer.stop(CMS.VectorRouter)
    # CRITICAL: Stop mnesia so the main Application can start it properly with its own config
    :mnesia.stop()
    
    Logger.info("REHYDRATOR: Protocol Complete. System ready for fresh boot.")
  end
  
  defp purge_caches do
    :mnesia.stop()
    mnesia_dir = Application.get_env(:mnesia, :dir) || "priv/data/mnesia_store"
    File.rm_rf!(mnesia_dir)
    File.mkdir_p!(mnesia_dir) 
    
    vector_dir = "priv/data/hnsw_indices" 
    File.rm_rf!(vector_dir)
    Logger.info("REHYDRATOR: Burnt Mnesia and Vector Caches.")
  end
  
  defp process_epoch_file(path, stats) do
    File.stream!(path)
    |> Enum.reduce(stats, fn line, acc -> 
      try do
        event = Jason.decode!(line)
        apply_event(event, acc)
      rescue
        _ -> Map.update!(acc, :errors, &(&1 + 1))
      end
    end)
  end
  
  defp apply_event(%{"type" => "node_created", "data" => node_map}, acc) do
     restore_node(node_map, acc)
  end
  
  defp apply_event(%{"type" => "node_updated", "data" => node_map}, acc) do
     restore_node(node_map, acc)
  end
  
  # Silent catch for hebbian updates in rehydrator V1
  defp apply_event(%{"type" => "hebbian_update"}, acc), do: acc

  defp apply_event(_, acc), do: acc 
  
  defp restore_node(node_map, acc) do
     id = node_map["id"]
     head = node_map["head"]
     
     acc = Map.update!(acc, :nodes, &(&1 + 1))
     
     # Reconstruct the full Node struct to ensure we have all fields
     # We use the Hydrator's logic which handles Base64 decoding, tuples, etc.
     try do
       node = CMS.Recovery.Hydrator.reconstruct_node(node_map)
       
       # Restore to Mnesia
       # Table schema: [:uuid, :path_hash, :parent_uuid, :head_data, :body_data, :tail_data, :chrono_stack, :provenance]
       path_hash = UUID.uuid5(:oid, node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink)
       parent_uuid = case Enum.find(node.tail.hierarchical, fn e -> e.type == :child_of end) do
         %CMS.Edge{target_node_id: pid} -> pid
         _ -> nil
       end
       
       :mnesia.dirty_write({:nodes_table, node.id, path_hash, parent_uuid, node.head, node.body, node.tail, node.body.data_ledger.chrono_stack, node.provenance, node.antenna})

       if head do
         embedding_list = head["embedding"]
         model = head["embedding_model_version"]
         
         if embedding_list do
           tensor = Nx.tensor(embedding_list)
           VectorRouter.add_embedding(id, tensor, model)
           Map.update!(acc, :vectors, &(&1 + 1))
         else
           acc
         end
       else
         acc
       end
     rescue
       e ->
         Logger.error("REHYDRATOR: Failed to restore node #{id}: #{inspect(e)}")
         Map.update!(acc, :errors, &(&1 + 1))
     end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: hydrator.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Recovery.Hydrator do
  require Logger
  alias CMS.{Node, NodeHead, NodeBody, NodeTail, NodeAntenna, DataHead, DataTail, Edge}
  alias CMS.NodeSupervisor

  @moduledoc """
  Responsible for "Hydrating" (loading) existing nodes from Epoch Logs into active RAM processes.
  Refined for CDMS Phase 1.
  """

  @epoch_dir "priv/data/epochs"

  @doc """
  Main entry point for hydration. Scans epoch logs and spawns node processes.
  """
  def run do
    Logger.info("HYDRATOR: Beginning CDMS Hydration Protocol...")
    
    File.mkdir_p!(@epoch_dir)
    
    files = 
      File.ls!(@epoch_dir)
      |> Enum.filter(&String.ends_with?(&1, ".jsonl"))
      |> Enum.sort()
      |> Enum.map(&Path.join(@epoch_dir, &1))

    if files == [] do
      Logger.info("HYDRATOR: No epoch logs found. Skipping hydration.")
    else
      Logger.info("HYDRATOR: Found #{length(files)} files. Loading nodes...")
      
      nodes_map = load_nodes_from_logs(files)
      
      Logger.info("HYDRATOR: Loaded #{map_size(nodes_map)} nodes from disk. Spawning processes...")
      
      spawn_nodes(nodes_map)
    end
    
    :ok
  end

  defp load_nodes_from_logs(files) do
    Enum.reduce(files, %{}, fn path, acc_sys ->
      File.stream!(path)
      |> Stream.map(fn line -> 
        case Jason.decode(line) do
          {:ok, data} -> data
          {:error, _} -> nil
        end
      end)
      |> Enum.reduce(acc_sys, fn 
           %{"entity" => "node", "type" => type, "data" => d}, acc 
             when type in ["node_created", "node_updated"] -> 
               Map.put(acc, d["id"], d)
           _, acc -> acc
         end)
    end)
  end

  defp spawn_nodes(nodes_map) do
    Enum.each(nodes_map, fn {id, node_map} ->
      try do
        node = reconstruct_node(node_map)
        case NodeSupervisor.start_child(node) do
          {:ok, pid} -> 
            # Register with LiveWire for file monitoring
            if node.body && node.body.data_ledger && node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink do
              path = node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink
              CMS.Services.LiveWire.watch(path)
              CMS.SymbolicIndex.register_path(path, pid)
              Logger.debug("HYDRATOR: Registered node #{id} with LiveWire and SymbolicIndex: #{path}")
            end
            :ok
          {:error, {:already_started, pid}} -> 
            # Also register already-started nodes
            if node.body && node.body.data_ledger && node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink do
              path = node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink
              CMS.Services.LiveWire.watch(path)
              CMS.SymbolicIndex.register_path(path, pid)
            end
            :ok
          {:error, reason} -> 
            Logger.error("HYDRATOR: Failed to start node #{id}: #{inspect(reason)}")
        end
      rescue
        e -> 
          Logger.error("HYDRATOR: Error reconstructing node #{id}: #{inspect(e)}")
          Logger.error(Exception.format(:error, e, __STACKTRACE__))
      end
    end)
  end

  def reconstruct_node(node_map) do
    head_map = node_map["head"]
    body_map = node_map["body"]
    head_data_map = body_map["data_head"]
    ledger_map = body_map["data_ledger"]
    antenna_map = node_map["antenna"]
    tail_map = node_map["tail"]

    # 1. Reconstruct Head
    # Convert spatial_coords from list back to tuple
    coords = case head_map["spatial_coords"] do
      [x, y, z] -> {x, y, z}
      coords when is_tuple(coords) -> coords  # Backward compatibility
      _ -> {0.0, 0.0, 0}  # Default fallback
    end
    
    node_head = %NodeHead{
      uuid: node_map["id"],
      embedding: Nx.tensor(head_map["embedding"]),
      embedding_model_version: head_map["embedding_model_version"],
      relevance_threshold: head_map["relevance_threshold"] || 0.35,
      internal_state: String.to_atom(head_map["internal_state"] || "genesis"),
      spatial_coords: coords,
      width_descriptor: head_map["width_descriptor"] || "m0"
    }

    # 2. Reconstruct Body Components
    data_head = %DataHead{
      summary: head_data_map["summary"],
      concepts: head_data_map["concepts"] || []
    }

    data_ledger = %DataTail{
      physical_symlink: ledger_map["physical_symlink"],
      integrity_hash: ledger_map["integrity_hash"],
      mime_definition: ledger_map["mime_definition"],
      last_mutation: parse_datetime(ledger_map["last_mutation"]),
      salience_score: ledger_map["salience_score"] || 0.5,
      chrono_stack: reconstruct_chrono_stack(ledger_map["chrono_stack"])
    }

    raw_body = body_map["data_body"]
    
    body_content = 
      if body_map["data_body_is_base64"] && is_binary(raw_body) do
        case Base.decode64(raw_body) do
          {:ok, decoded} -> decoded
          _ -> raw_body
        end
      else
        raw_body
      end

    node_body = %NodeBody{
      data_head: data_head,
      data_body: body_content,
      data_ledger: data_ledger,
      mode: String.to_atom(body_map["mode"] || "deep")
    }

    # 3. Reconstruct Tail (Connectome)
    node_tail = %NodeTail{
      hierarchical: reconstruct_edges(tail_map["hierarchical"]),
      syntactic: reconstruct_edges(tail_map["syntactic"]),
      semantic: reconstruct_edges(tail_map["semantic"])
    }

    # 4. Reconstruct Antenna
    node_antenna = %NodeAntenna{
      gain: antenna_map["gain"] || 1.0,
      frequency: antenna_map["frequency"] || 100,
      resonance_pattern: antenna_map["resonance_pattern"] || "stable",
      dampening_factors: antenna_map["dampening_factors"] || ["signal_established"]
    }

    %Node{
      id: node_map["id"],
      head: node_head,
      body: node_body,
      tail: node_tail,
      antenna: node_antenna,
      created_at: parse_datetime(node_map["created_at"]),
      last_fired: parse_datetime(node_map["last_fired"])
    }
  end

  defp reconstruct_edges(nil), do: []
  defp reconstruct_edges(list) when is_list(list) do
    Enum.map(list, fn e ->
      %Edge{
        target_node_id: e["target_node_id"],
        type: String.to_atom(e["type"]),
        weight: e["weight"] || 1.0,
        last_used_at: parse_datetime(e["last_used_at"])
      }
    end)
  end

  defp parse_datetime(nil), do: DateTime.utc_now()
  defp parse_datetime(string) when is_binary(string) do
    case DateTime.from_iso8601(string) do
      {:ok, dt, _offset} -> dt
      _ -> DateTime.utc_now()
    end
  end
  defp parse_datetime(_), do: DateTime.utc_now()

  defp reconstruct_chrono_stack(%{"snapshots" => snapshots}) when is_list(snapshots) do
    %CMS.ChronoStack{
      snapshots: Enum.map(snapshots, &reconstruct_snapshot/1)
    }
  end
  defp reconstruct_chrono_stack(_), do: %CMS.ChronoStack{snapshots: []}

  defp reconstruct_snapshot(s) do
    %CMS.ChronoStack.Snapshot{
      timestamp: parse_datetime(s["timestamp"]),
      hash: s["hash"],
      diff: s["diff"],
      cause: String.to_atom(s["cause"] || "unknown")
    }
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: connectome_scanner.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Core.Logic.ConnectomeScanner do
  @moduledoc """
  The Connectome Scanner: Forges Phase 4 Functional Edges.
  Covers the Skeletal, Immune, and Nervous systems of the Digital Body.
  """
  
  alias CMS.{Node, Edge}
  alias CMS.Ingestion.{AnatomyEngine, TriageProtocol}
  alias CMS.VectorRouter
  require Logger

  # --- 1. The Skeletal System (Dependencies) ---
  
  @doc """
  Scans for structural dependencies.
  - LINKS_DYNAMICALLY_TO: Binary -> Library
  - SYMLINKS_TO: Link -> Target
  - MOUNTS_ON: Device -> Directory
  """
  def scan_skeleton(%Node{} = node) do
    path = node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink
    mime = node.body.data_ledger.mime_definition
    
    # Re-derive triage class if not in provenance (Phase 1 logic)
    # We use identify/1 which re-stats. This is acceptable for local ingestion.
    triage_class = case TriageProtocol.identify(path) do
      {:ok, class} -> class
      _ -> :unknown
    end
    
    organ_type = AnatomyEngine.classify_organ(triage_class, path, mime)
    
    edges = []
    
    # LINKS_DYNAMICALLY_TO: Only for Binary Organs (Executables)
    edges = if organ_type == :binary_organ do
      edges ++ scan_dynamic_links(node)
    else
      edges
    end
    
    # SYMLINKS_TO: All nodes
    edges = edges ++ scan_symlinks(node)
    
    # MOUNTS_ON: Only for Device Interfaces
    edges = if organ_type == :device_interface do
      edges ++ scan_mounts(node)
    else
      edges
    end
    
    edges
  end
  
  defp scan_dynamic_links(node) do
    # Only for Binary Organs
    # In a real implementation, we would parse headers (ELF/PE).
    # Here, we simulate by checking for "lib*.so" strings in content if available.
    
    body_content = CMS.NodeBody.decompress(node.body)
    
    if is_binary(body_content) and byte_size(body_content) > 0 do
       # Simple regex to find library-like strings.
       # Matches: lib[name].so(.version)?
       regex = ~r/lib[a-zA-Z0-9_\-]+\.so(\.[0-9]+)*/
       
       Regex.scan(regex, body_content)
       |> Enum.uniq()
       |> Enum.map(fn [lib_name | _] -> 
          resolve_dependency(lib_name, :links_dynamically_to)
       end)
       |> Enum.reject(&is_nil/1)
    else
       []
    end
  end
  
  defp scan_symlinks(node) do
    path = node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink
    
    case File.read_link(path) do
      {:ok, target} ->
        # Create edge to target path (might need resolution)
        expanded_target = Path.expand(target, Path.dirname(path))
        [resolve_dependency(expanded_target, :symlinks_to)] |> Enum.reject(&is_nil/1)
      {:error, _} -> []
    end
  end
  
  defp scan_mounts(node) do
    path = node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink
    
    # Mocking mount check. Real system would read /proc/mounts.
    if String.starts_with?(path, "/dev/sda") do
       [resolve_dependency("/", :mounts_on)] |> Enum.reject(&is_nil/1)
    else
       []
    end
  end

  # --- 2. The Immune System (Authority) ---

  @doc """
  Scans for authority and territory.
  - OWNED_BY: Node -> Identity (User)
  - EXECUTABLE_BY: Identity (User/Group) -> Binary
  """
  def scan_immune(%Node{body: body} = _node) do
    ledger = body.data_ledger
    
    # 1. Ownership (The Territory)
    # Link every File Node to an Identity Node.
    uid = ledger.ownership[:uid]
    owner_edge = if uid, do: create_virtual_edge(uid, :identity, :owned_by), else: nil
    
    # 2. Execution Authority (The Attack Surface)
    # If executable by Group, draws a link from the Group Identity Node.
    gid = ledger.ownership[:gid]
    
    exec_edges = []
    
    # User Execution
    exec_edges = if ledger.permissions[:user] && String.contains?(ledger.permissions[:user], "x") do
       [create_virtual_edge(uid, :identity, :executable_by) | exec_edges]
    else
       exec_edges
    end
    
    # Group Execution
    exec_edges = if ledger.permissions[:group] && String.contains?(ledger.permissions[:group], "x") do
       [create_virtual_edge(gid, :group_identity, :executable_by) | exec_edges]
    else
       exec_edges
    end
    
    # Other/Universal Execution
    exec_edges = if ledger.permissions[:other] && String.contains?(ledger.permissions[:other], "x") do
       [create_virtual_edge("universal", :identity, :executable_by) | exec_edges]
    else
       exec_edges
    end
    
    ([owner_edge] ++ exec_edges) |> Enum.reject(&is_nil/1)
  end

  # --- 3. The Nervous System (Control & Flow) ---

  @doc """
  The Nervous System describes control and flow.
  The system uses a pattern-matching scanner on Configuration Nexuses.
  - CONFIGURES: Config -> Binary (Text file controls binary)
  - LOGS_TO: Config -> Log File (Reveals the Circulatory System)
  """
  def scan_nervous(%Node{} = node) do
    path = node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink
    mime = node.body.data_ledger.mime_definition
    
    triage_class = case TriageProtocol.identify(path) do
      {:ok, class} -> class
      _ -> :unknown
    end
    
    organ_type = AnatomyEngine.classify_organ(triage_class, path, mime)
    
    # Only for Configuration Nexuses
    if organ_type == :configuration_nexus do
      content = CMS.NodeBody.decompress(node.body)
      
      if content do
        # Pattern-matching scanner on Configuration Nexuses
        Regex.scan(~r/(\/[a-zA-Z0-9_\-\.\/]+)/, content)
        |> Enum.map(fn [path, _] -> path end)
        |> Enum.map(fn target_path ->
           cond do
             # LOGS_TO reveals the Circulatory System
             String.ends_with?(target_path, ".log") -> 
               resolve_dependency(target_path, :logs_to)
               
             # CONFIGURES shows which text file controls which binary
             String.starts_with?(target_path, "/bin") or String.starts_with?(target_path, "/usr/bin") -> 
               resolve_dependency(target_path, :configures)
               
             true -> nil
           end
        end)
        |> Enum.reject(&is_nil/1)
      else
        []
      end
    else
      []
    end
  end
  
  # --- Helpers ---
  
  defp resolve_dependency(target, type) do
    # Resolve target string to UUID.
    # LINKS_DYNAMICALLY_TO creates rigid links to Library Nodes (or Ghost Nodes).
    # If this link breaks, the organ dies.
    
    case VectorRouter.resolve_path(target) do
      {:ok, uuid} -> Edge.new(uuid, type, 1.0)
      _ -> 
         if String.contains?(target, "/") or String.starts_with?(target, "lib") do
           # Generate Ghost on the fly for paths or libraries
           {ghost_uuid, _, _} = CMS.Ingestion.FractalAperture.create_ghost_node(target, "scanner")
           Edge.new(ghost_uuid, type, 1.0)
         else
           nil
         end
    end
  end
  
  defp create_virtual_edge(id, type, edge_type) do
    # Virtual Nodes for Identities are indexed by "identity:<uid>" or "group_identity:<gid>"
    prefix = if type == :group_identity, do: "group_identity", else: "identity"
    uuid = UUID.uuid5(:oid, "#{prefix}:#{id}")
    Edge.new(uuid, edge_type, 1.0)
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: salience_engine.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.SalienceEngine do
  @moduledoc """
  Calculates the intrinsic importance (Salience) of a new Memory Node.

  Implements Section 2.4.3: The Weighting Engine.
  High Salience -> High Antenna Gain -> Wider Broadcast.

  FIX 5 IMPLEMENTED: ML Bridge with Heuristic Fallback.
  """

  require Logger

  # Configuration for the local Python Bridge (e.g., LLM or BERT-based classifier)
  @bridge_url "http://localhost:5000/api/v1/salience"
  @default_timeout 2_000 # 2 seconds - Fail fast to keep ingestion speed high

  # Keywords that imply high criticality (Fallback Logic)
  @critical_keywords ~w(error crash panic critical security breach alert fail exception timeout deadlock)
  @warning_keywords ~w(warning retry slow deprecated latency)

  @doc """
  Calculates a score (0.0 to 1.0) based on content analysis and context.
  It attempts to use the ML Bridge first; otherwise, it falls back to heuristics.
  """
  @spec calculate(String.t(), map()) :: float()
  def calculate(fact_text, provenance_metadata) do
    # 1. Attempt External Semantic Analysis (Fix 5)
    case call_ml_bridge(fact_text, provenance_metadata) do
      {:ok, score} ->
        score

      {:error, _reason} ->
        # 2. Fallback to Heuristic Analysis
        calculate_heuristic(fact_text, provenance_metadata)
    end
  end

  # --- Internal: ML Bridge ---

  defp call_ml_bridge(text, metadata) do
    payload = %{
      text: text,
      provenance: metadata
    }

    try do
      # Requires 'req' library, similar to Tool.Embedder
      case Req.post(@bridge_url, json: payload, receive_timeout: @default_timeout) do
        {:ok, %Req.Response{status: 200, body: %{"score" => score}}} when is_number(score) ->
          # Clamp score between 0.0 and 1.0 just in case
          safe_score = max(0.0, min(1.0, score))
          {:ok, safe_score}

        {:ok, %Req.Response{status: code}} ->
          Logger.warning("Salience Bridge returned non-200 status: #{code}. Switching to heuristic.")
          {:error, :bridge_error}

        {:error, exception} ->
          Logger.warning("Salience Bridge connection failed: #{inspect(exception)}. Switching to heuristic.")
          {:error, :connection_failed}
      end
    rescue
      e ->
        Logger.error("Salience Bridge crashed: #{inspect(e)}")
        {:error, :crash}
    end
  end

  # --- Internal: Heuristic Fallback ---

  defp calculate_heuristic(fact_text, provenance_metadata) do
    base_score = 0.5 # Neutral baseline

    # 1. Contextual Boost (from Provenance)
    # If the agent explicitly flags this as high priority
    context_score = case Map.get(provenance_metadata, "priority", "normal") do
      "critical" -> 1.0
      "high" -> 0.8
      "low" -> 0.2
      _ -> 0.0
    end

    # 2. Content Analysis
    text = String.downcase(fact_text)

    content_score = cond do
      contains_any?(text, @critical_keywords) -> 0.9
      contains_any?(text, @warning_keywords) -> 0.7
      true -> 0.0
    end

    # 3. Maximize
    # If it's explicitly critical OR implicitly critical, we take the max
    max(base_score, max(context_score, content_score))
  end

  defp contains_any?(text, keywords) do
    Enum.any?(keywords, fn k -> String.contains?(text, k) end)
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: physiology_engine.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Core.Logic.PhysiologyEngine do
  @moduledoc """
  The Physiology Engine: Manages metabolic rates and temporal flow.
  Implements the Sliding Window Buffer and the 5-Minute Heartbeat.
  """
  
  use GenServer
  require Logger
  alias CMS.ChronoStack

  @table :physiology_buffer
  @window_size 3000
  @heartbeat_interval 300_000 # 5 minutes

  # --- Client API ---

  def start_link(_) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, %{}, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @doc """
  Buffers content for a Blood Node. 
  Only keeps the latest 3,000 characters.
  """
  def buffer_blood(node_id, content) do
    # Take latest 3,000 chars
    slice = if String.length(content) > @window_size do
      String.slice(content, -@window_size..-1)
    else
      content
    end
    
    :ets.insert(@table, {node_id, slice, DateTime.utc_now()})
  end

  @doc """
  Manually triggers the heartbeat for testing.
  """
  def trigger_heartbeat do
    send(__MODULE__, :heartbeat)
  end

  # --- Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(_) do
    :ets.new(@table, [:set, :public, :named_table])
    
    # Start the 5-minute heartbeat
    schedule_heartbeat()
    
    {:ok, %{}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:heartbeat, state) do
    IO.puts("\n[HEARTBEAT] Rhythmic Sampling of Blood Nodes...\n")
    
    # Process all buffered nodes
    :ets.tab2list(@table)
    |> Enum.each(fn {node_id, buffer_content, _ts} -> 
       process_blood_pulse(node_id, buffer_content)
    end)
    
    schedule_heartbeat()
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  # --- Private Helpers ---

  defp schedule_heartbeat do
    Process.send_after(self(), :heartbeat, @heartbeat_interval)
  end

  defp process_blood_pulse(node_id, buffer_content) do
    # 1. Fetch node from registry/mnesia
    case CMS.NodeSupervisor.get_node(node_id) do
      {:ok, node} ->
        old_body_content = CMS.NodeBody.decompress(node.body)
        
        # 2. The Check: Compare current window with last saved
        if old_body_content != buffer_content do
          Logger.info("PhysiologyEngine: Heartbeat detected change for Blood Node #{node_id}. Pulsing...")
          
          # 3. The Pulse: Push snapshot (Max 10 for Blood)
          old_hash = node.body.data_ledger.integrity_hash
          new_hash = :crypto.hash(:sha256, buffer_content) |> Base.encode16(case: :lower)
          
          new_stack = ChronoStack.push(node.body.data_ledger.chrono_stack, old_body_content, buffer_content, old_hash, :metabolic_pulse, 10)
          
          # Update Ledger & Body
          new_ledger = %{node.body.data_ledger | 
            integrity_hash: new_hash, 
            last_mutation: DateTime.utc_now(),
            chrono_stack: new_stack
          }
          
          new_body = %{node.body | data_body: buffer_content, data_ledger: new_ledger}
          new_node = %{node | body: new_body}
          
          # 4. Result: Time-lapse update
          # Notify actor and persist
          case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, node_id) do
            [{pid, _}] -> GenServer.cast(pid, {:update_node, new_node})
            _ -> :ok # If actor died, persistence handled by direct mnesia if desired?
                    # For now, we rely on NodeActor to handle persistence via update_node.
          end
        end
      _ -> 
        # Node might have been deleted, clean up buffer
        :ets.delete(@table, node_id)
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: edge_generator.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator do
  @moduledoc """
  The Edge Generator: Forges the links of the Connectome.
  """

  require Logger
  alias CMS.{Edge, NodeTail}

  @doc """
  Generates all edges for a single node.
  """
  def generate_edges(node) do
    # Phase 4 Upgrade: Connectome Scanning
    # We apply this after initial generation phases
    structural_edges = CMS.Core.Logic.ConnectomeScanner.scan_skeleton(node)
    immune_edges = CMS.Core.Logic.ConnectomeScanner.scan_immune(node)
    nervous_edges = CMS.Core.Logic.ConnectomeScanner.scan_nervous(node)
    
    # Merge all edges manually from the previous pipe approach
    # We need to construct the node with valid tails from the previous steps first or just merge results?
    # The previous code piped functions which return 'node'. 
    # But generate_syntactic_edges returns 'node' with updated tail.
    # To correctly integrate, we chain the calls and then inspect/update the final node.
    
    node = node
    |> generate_hierarchical_edges()
    |> generate_syntactic_edges()
    |> generate_semantic_edges()
    
    # Now append Phase 4 edges
    new_tail = node.tail
    
    new_tail = Enum.reduce(structural_edges ++ nervous_edges, new_tail, fn edge, acc ->
      CMS.NodeTail.add_edge(acc, edge)
    end)
    
    # Immune edges might be semantic or own category. Spec calls them Authority.
    # We map them to semantic for generic storage or assume add_edge handles checks.
    # Let's map OWNED_BY/EXECUTABLE_BY to semantic or add support in NodeTail (which defaults generic to syntactic).
    # NodeTail.add_edge handles unknowns by defaulting to syntactic. Spec says Authority Edges.
    # Let's effectively treat them as structural (rigid) or semantic (social)?
    # "Authority Edges describe defense mechanism".
    # Implementation Plan: just add them via standard add_edge.
    
    new_tail = Enum.reduce(immune_edges, new_tail, fn edge, acc ->
      CMS.NodeTail.add_edge(acc, edge)
    end)
    
    %{node | tail: new_tail}
  end

  @doc """
  Generates hierarchical edges (PARENT_OF/CHILD_OF) based on file path.
  Note: This is usually handled during Genesis, but can be re-run.
  """
  def generate_hierarchical_edges(node) do
    # Implementation depends on knowing the directory structure
    # For now, we'll assume the Genesis engine handles this.
    node
  end

  @doc """
  Generates syntactic edges by parsing the file content for imports/dependencies.
  """
  def generate_syntactic_edges(node) do
    if node.body.mode == :deep do
      # Decompress content for analysis
      content = CMS.NodeBody.decompress(node.body) || ""
      path = node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink
      
      imports = find_imports(content, path)
      
      Enum.reduce(imports, node, fn import_name, acc ->
        # Find node by name
        case CMS.VectorRouter.find_by_name(import_name) do
          {:ok, target_id} ->
            edge = Edge.new(target_id, :imports, 1.0)
            %{acc | tail: NodeTail.add_edge(acc.tail, edge)}
          _ -> 
            # Not found in system. Check if it's a file path to generate a Ghost Node.
            # (Simple heuristic: if it looks like a path or specific system lib)
            if String.contains?(import_name, "/") do
               # Check Bounded Circle
               # NOTE: We need circle_root from node.provenance or opts, but EdgeGenerator 
               # usually runs post-ingest. For Phase 2, we assume we might have circle_root in provenance
               # or just assume specific system paths are external.
               
               # Let's assume for this specific requirement, if we see a path-like import 
               # that isn't in Mnesia, we treat it as external.
               
               {ghost_uuid, _ghost_node, mn_record} = CMS.Ingestion.FractalAperture.create_ghost_node(import_name, node.id)
               
               # Write Ghost to Mnesia (if not exists)
               # Use a safe write or check existence
               case :mnesia.dirty_read(:nodes_table, ghost_uuid) do
                 [] -> :mnesia.dirty_write(mn_record)
                 _ -> :ok
               end
               
               edge = Edge.new(ghost_uuid, :anchored_to, 1.0)
               %{acc | tail: NodeTail.add_edge(acc.tail, edge)}
            else
               acc
            end
        end
      end)
    else
      node
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Generates semantic edges using the "Hebbian Priming" Scatter-Gather protocol.
  Queries all active NodeActors in parallel to find semantic neighbors.
  """
  def generate_semantic_edges(node) do
    # Configuration matches IngestionEngine Spec
    conflict_threshold = 0.8
    my_id = node.id

    # Get raw candidates with scores using Scatter-Gather
    candidates = prime_candidates(node.head.embedding, node.id)
    
    Enum.reduce(candidates, node, fn {target_id, score}, acc ->
      if score >= conflict_threshold do
        # --- SPECIAL CASE: DIALECTICAL MERGE ---
        # 1. Add Contradicts Link to Peer
        acc = %{acc | tail: NodeTail.add_edge(acc.tail, Edge.new(target_id, :contradicts, 1.0))}
        
        # 2. Spawn MetaNode for the conflict
        context = %{node_a: my_id, node_b: target_id, similarity: score}
        case CMS.MetaNode.spawn_conflict("Semantic overlap detected during Genesis", context) do
          {:ok, meta_node} ->
             # Register and spawn MetaNode actor
             CMS.LogAppender.append_node_event(meta_node.id, :node_created, meta_node)
             CMS.NodeSupervisor.spawn_node(meta_node)
             
             # 3. Add Semantic Link to MetaNode
             edge_to_meta = Edge.new(meta_node.id, :semantic, 0.9)
             %{acc | tail: NodeTail.add_edge(acc.tail, edge_to_meta)}
          _ ->
             acc
        end
      else
        # Normal Semantic Relationship
        edge = Edge.new(target_id, :semantic, score)
        %{acc | tail: NodeTail.add_edge(acc.tail, edge)}
      end
    end)
  end

  @doc """
  Generic Hebbian Priming: Returns a list of semantic candidate pairs {id, score} for a given embedding.
  Queries all active NodeActors in parallel (Scatter-Gather).
  """
  def prime_candidates(embedding, my_id \\ nil) do
    # Configuration matches IngestionEngine Spec
    min_score = 0.3
    max_links = 10
    
    # 1. Fetch all drift info from the lock-free ETS index
    # This eliminates GenServer deadlocks during Scatter-Gather.
    all_drift_info = CMS.Core.Logic.DriftIndex.get_all_drift_info()

    similarities =
      all_drift_info
      |> Enum.reduce([], fn {node_id, remote_embedding, _region_id}, acc ->
        if node_id != my_id and remote_embedding do
           score = cosine_similarity(embedding, remote_embedding)
           if score >= min_score do
             Logger.debug("EdgeGenerator: Node #{my_id || "NEW"} evaluating #{node_id} -> Score: #{:io_lib.format("~.4f", [score])}")
             [{node_id, score} | acc]
           else
             acc
           end
        else
           acc
        end
      end)

    if length(similarities) > 0 do
       Logger.info("EdgeGenerator: Found #{length(similarities)} semantic candidates for #{my_id || "NEW"} (Min Score: #{min_score})")
    end

    similarities
    |> Enum.sort_by(fn {_, score} -> score end, &>=/2)
    |> Enum.take(max_links)
  end

  @doc """
  Calculates the cosine similarity between two vectors using native Nx operations for maximum performance.
  """
  def cosine_similarity(vec_a, vec_b) do
    try do
      # Directly use tensors if possible, or convert to tensors (much faster than list zip/sum)
      t_a = if is_struct(vec_a, Nx.Tensor), do: vec_a, else: Nx.tensor(vec_a)
      t_b = if is_struct(vec_b, Nx.Tensor), do: vec_b, else: Nx.tensor(vec_b)

      # cosine_similarity = (A . B) / (||A|| * ||B||)
      dot_product = Nx.dot(t_a, t_b) |> Nx.to_number()
      norm_a = Nx.sum(Nx.pow(t_a, 2)) |> Nx.sqrt() |> Nx.to_number()
      norm_b = Nx.sum(Nx.pow(t_b, 2)) |> Nx.sqrt() |> Nx.to_number()

      if norm_a == 0 or norm_b == 0, do: 0.0, else: dot_product / (norm_a * norm_b)
    rescue
      _ -> 0.0
    end
  end

  # --- Import Extraction ---

  defp find_imports(content, path) do
    ext = Path.extname(path)
    cond do
      ext in [".ex", ".exs"] -> 
        find_imports_elixir(content)
      ext == ".py" ->
        # Functional approximation for Python in Phase 1
        (Regex.scan(~r/^import\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)/m, content) |> Enum.map(fn [_, name] -> name end))
        ++ (Regex.scan(~r/^from\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\s+import/, content) |> Enum.map(fn [_, name] -> name end))
      true ->
        []
    end
  end

  defp find_imports_elixir(content) do
    case Code.string_to_quoted(content) do
      {:ok, quoted} ->
        {_, names} = Macro.prewalk(quoted, [], fn
          {key, _, [{:__aliases__, _, names} | _]}, acc when key in [:alias, :import, :require, :use] ->
             {nil, [Enum.join(names, ".") | acc]}
          node, acc -> {node, acc}
        end)
        Enum.uniq(names)
      _ ->
        []
    end
  end
end

defmodule CMS.Core.Logic.DriftIndex do
  @moduledoc """
  A lock-free, read-optimized registry for Node embeddings and metadata.
  
  Uses an ETS table to allow NodeActors to perform semantic lookups (Scatter-Gather)
  without blocking other actors or causing circular GenServer deadlocks.
  """

  @table :drift_index

  @doc """
  Initializes the ETS table. Called during application boot.
  """
  def init do
    :ets.new(@table, [:set, :public, :named_table, read_concurrency: true])
  end

  @doc """
  Registers or updates a node's drift information in the index.
  """
  def register(node_id, embedding, region_id) do
    :ets.insert(@table, {node_id, embedding, region_id})
  end

  @doc """
  Removes a node from the index.
  """
  def unregister(node_id) do
    :ets.delete(@table, node_id)
  end

  @doc """
  Retrieves all active drift information records.
  Returns a list of {node_id, embedding, region_id}.
  """
  def get_all_drift_info do
    :ets.tab2list(@table)
  end
end

defmodule CMS.SemanticRegion do
  @moduledoc """
  Manages Semantic Partitioning logic.

  Nodes are assigned to one of 256 regions based on their semantic embedding.
  """

  @num_regions 256

  @doc """
  Computes the region ID (0-255) for a given embedding and model version.
  """
  @spec compute_region_hash(Nx.Tensor.t() | list(), String.t()) :: integer()
  def compute_region_hash(embedding, model_version) do
    # 1. Ensure we have a binary representation (efficient for hashing)
    raw_data = if is_struct(embedding, Nx.Tensor), do: Nx.to_binary(embedding), else: :erlang.term_to_binary(embedding)

    # 2. Hashing and mapping to region space
    :crypto.hash(:sha256, "#{model_version}:" <> raw_data)
    |> :binary.decode_unsigned()
    |> rem(@num_regions)
  end

  @doc """
  Subscribes the current process to the relevant region topics.
  """
  def subscribe(region_id, node_id) do
    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(CMS.PubSub, "region:#{region_id}")
    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(CMS.PubSub, "node_pulse:#{node_id}")
    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(CMS.PubSub, "global:signals")
  end

  @doc """
  Unsubscribes from a region topic (used during Node Migration).
  """
  def unsubscribe(region_id, _node_id) do
    Phoenix.PubSub.unsubscribe(CMS.PubSub, "region:#{region_id}")
  end
end

defmodule CMS.FIDValidator do
  @moduledoc """
  Validates the structural integrity of DataBodyPayloads during Ingestion.
  """

  alias CMS.DataBodyPayload.{Text, Code, Number, Link, Object}

  @spec validate(CMS.DataBodyPayload.t()) :: :ok | {:error, String.t()}

  def validate(%Text{content: c}) when is_binary(c) and byte_size(c) > 0, do: :ok
  def validate(%Text{}), do: {:error, "FID: Text content must be a non-empty string"}

  def validate(%Code{language: l, content: c}) when is_atom(l) and is_binary(c), do: :ok
  def validate(%Code{}), do: {:error, "FID: Code must have an atom language and string content"}

  def validate(%Number{value: v}) when is_number(v), do: :ok
  def validate(%Number{}), do: {:error, "FID: Number value must be integer or float"}

  def validate(%Link{uri: u}) when is_binary(u) do
    if Regex.match?(~r/^(http|https|gm|ipfs):\/\//, u) do
      :ok
    else
      {:error, "FID: Link URI must start with http, https, gm, or ipfs"}
    end
  end
  def validate(%Link{}), do: {:error, "FID: Link URI missing"}

  def validate(%Object{data: d}) when is_map(d), do: :ok
  def validate(%Object{}), do: {:error, "FID: Object data must be a map"}

  def validate(unknown), do: {:error, "Unknown struct type: #{inspect(unknown)}"}
end
