defmodule CMS.QueryTriageTest do
  use ExUnit.Case
  require Logger
  
  setup do
    # Ensure services are running
    ensure_started({Phoenix.PubSub, name: CMS.PubSub})
    ensure_started({Registry, keys: :unique, name: CMS.NodeRegistry})
    ensure_started(CMS.SymbolicIndex)
    ensure_started(CMS.VectorRouter)
    :ok
  end
  
  defp ensure_started(module_or_spec) do
    case start_supervised(module_or_spec) do
      {:ok, _} -> :ok
      {:error, {:already_started, _}} -> :ok
      {:error, _} =err -> err
    end
  end
  
  describe "Query Triage Routing" do
    test "detects path queries (Mode A)" do
      assert {:symbolic, _} = CMS.QueryTriage.route("/home/user/file.ex")
      assert {:symbolic, _} = CMS.QueryTriage.route("~/project/lib/app.ex")
      assert {:symbolic, _} = CMS.QueryTriage.route("./relative/path.ex")
    end
    
    test "detects filename queries (Mode B)" do
      assert {:nominal, "config.json"} = CMS.QueryTriage.route("config.json")
      assert {:nominal, "app.ex"} = CMS.QueryTriage.route("app.ex")
      assert {:nominal, "README.md"} = CMS.QueryTriage.route("README.md")
    end
    
    test "detects natural language queries (Mode C)" do
      assert {:semantic, _} = CMS.QueryTriage.route("how does hebbian learning work?")
      assert {:semantic, _} = CMS.QueryTriage.route("what is the activation engine")
      assert {:semantic, _} = CMS.QueryTriage.route("show me files about cognition")
    end
    
    test "allows manual mode override" do
      assert {:symbolic, _} = CMS.QueryTriage.route("anything", mode: :symbolic)
      assert {:nominal, _} = CMS.QueryTriage.route("anything", mode: :nominal)
      assert {:semantic, _} = CMS.QueryTriage.route("anything", mode: :semantic)
    end
  end
  
  describe "Mode A: Symbolic Index" do
    test "returns empty results for non-existent paths" do
      {:ok, :symbolic, results} = CMS.QueryTriage.execute("/non/existent/path.ex")
      assert results == []
    end
  end
  
  describe "Mode B: Nominal Index" do
    test "returns empty results for non-registered names" do
      {:ok, :nominal, results} = CMS.QueryTriage.execute("nonexistent_file.ex")
      assert results == []
    end
  end
  
  describe "Mode C: Semantic Search" do
    test "handles embedding failures gracefully" do
      # This will fail if embedder is not configured
      case CMS.QueryTriage.execute("test semantic query") do
        {:ok, :semantic, _results} -> assert true
        {:error, :embedding_failed} -> assert true  # Expected if no embedder
        _ -> flunk("Unexpected result")
      end
    end
  end
end

defmodule CMS.ActivationTest do
  use ExUnit.Case
  require Logger
  alias CMS.{NodeActor, NodeSupervisor, Node, NodeHead, NodeBody, DataHead, DataTail, NodeAntenna, Edge}

  setup do
    # Start minimal infrastructure for activation tests
    ensure_started({Phoenix.PubSub, [name: CMS.PubSub, adapter: Phoenix.PubSub.PG2]})
    ensure_started({Registry, [keys: :unique, name: CMS.NodeRegistry]})
    ensure_started(CMS.ActivationEngine)
    
    # We need LogAppender and EpochManager for the firing logs
    # but since we want to avoid Mnesia hassle, we'll just mock/stub or skip them if possible.
    # Actually, NodeActor calls them. Let's start a stub LogAppender if possible, 
    # or just start the real one but handle its dependencies.
    
    # Start all Hebbian shards
    Enum.each(0..31, fn i ->
      ensure_started({CMS.RegionalHebbianBuffer, i})
    end)
    
    ensure_started(CMS.NodeSupervisor)
    
    :ok
  end

  defp ensure_started(module_or_spec) do
    case start_supervised(module_or_spec) do
      {:ok, _} -> :ok
      {:error, {:already_started, _}} -> :ok
      {:error, _} = err -> err
    end
  end

  test "Node Firing updates last_fired" do
    uuid = "node-1"
    node = create_dummy_node(uuid)
    {:ok, pid} = NodeSupervisor.start_child(node)
    
    {:ok, node_info} = GenServer.call(pid, :get_state_snapshot)
    initial_last_fired = node_info.last_fired
    
    GenServer.cast(pid, :fire)
    
    # Wait for processing
    Process.sleep(500)
    
    {:ok, state} = GenServer.call(pid, :get_state_snapshot)
    Logger.info("[TRACE] Node #{uuid} last_fired after: #{inspect(state.last_fired)}")
    assert DateTime.compare(state.last_fired, initial_last_fired) == :gt
  end

  test "Signal Propagation between connected nodes" do
    uuid_a = "node-a"
    uuid_b = "node-b"
    
    # Node A links to Node B
    edge = Edge.new(uuid_b, :semantic, 0.9)
    node_a = create_dummy_node(uuid_a)
    # Give Node A max gain to ensure propagation triggers Node B
    node_a = %{node_a | antenna: %{node_a.antenna | gain: 1.0}}
    node_a = %{node_a | tail: CMS.NodeTail.add_edge(node_a.tail, edge)}
    
    node_b = create_dummy_node(uuid_b)
    
    {:ok, _pid_a} = NodeSupervisor.start_child(node_a)
    {:ok, _pid_b} = NodeSupervisor.start_child(node_b)
    
    # Fire Node A
    NodeActor.fire(uuid_a)
    
    # Wait for propagation
    Process.sleep(200)
    
    # Verify Node B received activation
    # We can check Node B's state for last_activation_times
    # But wait, NodeActor.get_state_snapshot returns state.node, not the actor state.
    # Let's add a way to get the full actor state or check behavior.
    # In NodeActor, strength > 0.7 triggers firing. 0.9 * 0.8 * 1.0 (gain * salience) = 0.72.
    # This should trigger Node B to fire too.
    
    {:ok, state_b} = GenServer.call(via_tuple(uuid_b), :get_state_snapshot)
    assert DateTime.compare(state_b.last_fired, node_b.last_fired) == :gt

    # Verify Hebbian learning (Cells that fire together, wire together)
    # Node A activated Node B, within a short window, link A -> B is strengthened.
    # We check the ETS buffer directly.
    # NodeActor hardcodes region 0 currently, so it goes to shard 0.
    
    # Wait for the async GenServer.cast to reach the buffer
    Process.sleep(200)
    
    key = {uuid_a, uuid_b}
    case :ets.lookup(:hebbian_buffer_0, key) do
      [{^key, delta}] ->
         assert delta > 0
         IO.puts("[TEST_TRACE] Hebbian Delta for #{uuid_a} -> #{uuid_b}: #{delta}")
      [] ->
         flunk("Hebbian update for #{inspect(key)} not found in :hebbian_buffer_0")
    end
  end

  defp create_dummy_node(uuid) do
    embedding = Nx.broadcast(Nx.tensor(0.1), {384})
    head = NodeHead.new(uuid, embedding, "test-model")
    data_head = DataHead.new("Summary")
    ledger = DataTail.new("/path/to/file", "hash", "text/plain", 1.0) # Max salience
    body = NodeBody.new(data_head, "Content", ledger)
    antenna = NodeAntenna.new("/path/to/file") # Gain 0.8 for source
    
    # Use an older timestamp to ensure comparison > works
    past = DateTime.utc_now() |> DateTime.add(-10, :second)
    
    Node.new(head, body, antenna, past, past)
  end

  defp via_tuple(id), do: {:via, Registry, {CMS.NodeRegistry, id}}
end

defmodule CMS.CognitiveFeaturesTest do
  use ExUnit.Case, async: false
  require Logger
  alias CMS.{NodeActor, NodeSupervisor, Node, NodeHead, NodeBody, DataHead, DataTail, NodeAntenna, Edge, IngestionEngine, MetaNode}

  defmodule MockBridge do
    use Plug.Router
    plug :match
    plug :dispatch

    post "/api/v1/embed" do
      conn
      |> Plug.Conn.put_resp_content_type("application/json")
      |> Plug.Conn.send_resp(200, Jason.encode!(%{vector: Enum.map(1..384, fn _ -> 0.1 end)}))
    end
  end

  setup do
    # Start Mock Bridge
    {:ok, _} = Plug.Cowboy.http(MockBridge, [], port: 5001)
    Application.put_env(:acn_cms, :embedder_bridge_url, "http://localhost:5001/api/v1/embed")

    # Start infrastructure
    ensure_started({Phoenix.PubSub, [name: CMS.PubSub, adapter: Phoenix.PubSub.PG2]})
    ensure_started({Registry, [keys: :unique, name: CMS.NodeRegistry]})
    ensure_started(CMS.ActivationEngine)
    Enum.each(0..31, fn i -> ensure_started({CMS.RegionalHebbianBuffer, i}) end)
    ensure_started(CMS.NodeSupervisor)
    ensure_started(CMS.IngestionEngine)
    
    on_exit(fn ->
      Plug.Cowboy.shutdown(MockBridge.HTTP)
    end)
    
    :ok
  end

  defp ensure_started(module_or_spec) do
    case start_supervised(module_or_spec) do
      {:ok, _} -> :ok
      {:error, {:already_started, _}} -> :ok
      {:error, _} = err -> err
    end
  end

  test "Dialectical Conflict Resolution spawns MetaNode" do
    # 1. Ingest initial fact
    fact1 = "The sky is blue because of Rayleigh scattering."
    req1 = %{
      fact_text: fact1,
      agent_id: "system",
      description_payloads: [%CMS.DataBodyPayload.Text{type: :text, content: "Initial description"}],
      provenance: %{"trust_score" => 0.5},
      acls: %{read: ["public"], write: ["system"]}
    }
    {:ok, node1_id} = IngestionEngine.ingest(req1)
    
    # 2. Ingest conflicting fact (high similarity, different content/provenance)
    fact2 = "The sky is blue because of Rayleigh scattering, but also sometimes green."
    req2 = %{
      fact_text: fact2,
      agent_id: "system",
      description_payloads: [%CMS.DataBodyPayload.Text{type: :text, content: "Conflicting description"}],
      provenance: %{"trust_score" => 0.4}, # Lower trust -> should trigger Dialectical Merge
      acls: %{read: ["public"], write: ["system"]}
    }
    
    # Use a small wait to ensure node1 is registered
    Process.sleep(100)
    
    {:ok, :ingested_with_conflict_resolution, meta_id} = IngestionEngine.ingest(req2)
    
    assert meta_id != nil
    
    # 3. Verify edges
    {:ok, node1} = GenServer.call(via_tuple(node1_id), :get_state_snapshot)
    
    # Should have a contradicts edge to the new node
    assert Enum.any?(node1.tail.semantic ++ node1.tail.syntactic, fn e -> e.type == :contradicts end)
  end

  test "Semantic Proprioception (Drift) corrects region" do
    # 1. Start a node
    node = create_dummy_node("drift-node")
    {:ok, pid} = NodeSupervisor.start_child(node)
    
    # 2. Start DriftManager
    start_supervised(CMS.NodeDriftManager)
    
    # 3. Simulate drift (we don't actually change the embedding, 
    # but we can observe the manager checking it).
    # Since we can't easily force a wrong region from outside without mocking,
    # let's just verify it calls get_drift_info.
    
    # Wait for check_drift cycle (or trigger it if possible)
    # DriftManager runs every 30 mins by default. Let's make it faster for test?
    # Actually, we can just send it a message.
    send(CMS.NodeDriftManager, :check_drift)
    
    Process.sleep(100)
    # If no crash, it means the interface get_drift_info is working.
  end

  defp create_dummy_node(uuid) do
    embedding = Nx.broadcast(Nx.tensor(0.1), {384})
    head = NodeHead.new(uuid, embedding, "test-model")
    data_head = DataHead.new("Summary")
    ledger = DataTail.new("/path/to/file", "hash", "text/plain", 1.0)
    body = NodeBody.new(data_head, "Content", ledger)
    antenna = NodeAntenna.new("/path/to/file")
    past = DateTime.utc_now() |> DateTime.add(-10, :second)
    Node.new(head, body, antenna, past, past)
  end

  defp via_tuple(id), do: {:via, Registry, {CMS.NodeRegistry, id}}
end

defmodule CMS.ChronoForensicsTest do
  use ExUnit.Case
  require Logger
  
  describe "Stack Traversal" do
    test "get_node_history returns error for non-existent node" do
      result = CMS.TemporalQueryEngine.get_node_history("nonexistent", depth: 5)
      # Non-existent nodes should return error
      assert result == {:error, :node_not_found}
    end
  end
  
  describe "Differential Diagnosis" do
    test "find_recent_mutations returns list of mutated nodes" do
      {:ok, mutations} = CMS.TemporalQueryEngine.find_recent_mutations(60)
      assert is_list(mutations)
      # Each mutation should have required fields
      if length(mutations) > 0 do
        mutation = List.first(mutations)
        assert Map.has_key?(mutation, :node_id)
        assert Map.has_key?(mutation, :path)
        assert Map.has_key?(mutation, :mutation_cause)
      end
    end
    
    test "find_recent_mutations with large time range" do
      {:ok, mutations} = CMS.TemporalQueryEngine.find_recent_mutations(10000)
      assert is_list(mutations)
    end
  end
  
  describe "Directory State Reconstruction" do
    test "reconstruct_directory_state handles non-existent directory" do
      past_time = DateTime.add(DateTime.utc_now(), -3600, :second)
      result = CMS.TemporalQueryEngine.reconstruct_directory_state("/nonexistent/path", past_time)
      
      case result do
        {:ok, _state} -> assert true
        {:error, _} -> assert true  # Expected for non-existent
      end
    end
  end
end

File: cdms_phase1_test.exs

defmodule CMS.Phase1Test do
  use ExUnit.Case
  require Logger
  alias CMS.NodeSupervisor

  @tag :phase1
  test "Phase 1: Full Ingestion Simulation" do
    # 1. Setup Dummy Directory
    tmp_dir = Path.join(System.tmp_dir!(), "cdms_test_project_#{System.unique_integer()}")
    File.mkdir_p!(tmp_dir)
    File.write!(Path.join(tmp_dir, "mix.exs"), "defmodule Test do end") # Config (High Gain)
    File.write!(Path.join(tmp_dir, "lib.ex"), "defmodule Lib do end") # Source (High Gain)
    File.write!(Path.join(tmp_dir, "readme.txt"), "Documentation") # Doc (Medium Gain)
    File.write!(Path.join(tmp_dir, "temp.log"), "Log file") # Log (Low Gain)

    # 2. Launch Genesis
    # We use start_directory_agent directly to avoid "app.start" issues in test environment
    {:ok, _pid} = NodeSupervisor.start_directory_agent(tmp_dir, nil)

    # 3. Wait for Ingestion (Active Wait)
    # 4 files + 1 dir = 5 nodes.
    wait_for_nodes(5, 10_000)
    
    # 4. Verification
    all_pids = NodeSupervisor.get_all_active_node_pids()
    assert length(all_pids) >= 5
    
    # Check specific node attributes
    nodes = Enum.map(all_pids, fn pid -> 
       {:ok, node} = GenServer.call(pid, :get_state_snapshot)
       node
    end)
    
    # Verify Antenna Gains
    config_node = Enum.find(nodes, fn n -> String.contains?(n.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink, "mix.exs") end)
    assert config_node.antenna.gain == 0.8
    
    log_node = Enum.find(nodes, fn n -> String.contains?(n.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink, "temp.log") end)
    assert log_node.antenna.gain == 0.1
    
    # Verify Compression (Mode A)
    # lib.ex should be Mode A (Deep)
    lib_node = Enum.find(nodes, fn n -> String.contains?(n.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink, "lib.ex") end)
    assert lib_node.body.mode == :deep
    assert is_binary(lib_node.body.data_body) # Compressed binary
    
    # Decompress to verify
    assert CMS.NodeBody.decompress(lib_node.body) == "defmodule Lib do end"

    # 5. LiveWire Mutation Test
    # Mutate lib.ex
    Logger.info("Mutating lib.ex...")
    File.write!(Path.join(tmp_dir, "lib.ex"), "defmodule LibModified do end")
    
    # LiveWire is polling every 5s. We wait.
    Process.sleep(6000) 
    
    # Fetch node again
    {:ok, mutated_node} = NodeSupervisor.get_node(lib_node.id)
    
    # Verify ChronoStack
    # Stack in Ledger
    stack = mutated_node.body.data_ledger.chrono_stack
    refute Enum.empty?(stack.snapshots)
    
    latest_snapshot = List.first(stack.snapshots)
    assert latest_snapshot.cause == :livewire_mutation
    
    # Verify Antenna Boost
    # 0.8 initial + 0.2 boost = 1.0
    assert mutated_node.antenna.gain >= 0.99 

    # 6. Spatial Manifold & Descriptor Verification
    # Root node should have Z=0 and an incremented width descriptor (e.g. m4 since 4 items added)
    {:ok, root_node} = NodeSupervisor.get_node(UUID.uuid5(:oid, tmp_dir))
    assert root_node.head.spatial_coords == {0.0, 0.0, 0}
    assert String.starts_with?(root_node.head.width_descriptor, "m")
    assert String.to_integer(String.replace(root_node.head.width_descriptor, "m", "")) > 0

    # Child node should have Z=1 and hierarchical descriptor (e.g. mA0 or similar)
    assert lib_node.head.spatial_coords |> elem(2) == 1
    assert String.length(lib_node.head.width_descriptor) >= 3 # e.g. "mA0" or "mD0"

    # 7. Self-Healing: Deletion Test
    Logger.info("Deleting readme.txt...")
    readme_uuid = Enum.find(nodes, fn n -> String.contains?(n.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink, "readme.txt") end).id
    File.rm!(Path.join(tmp_dir, "readme.txt"))
    
    # Wait for LiveWire to detect deletion
    Process.sleep(6000)
    
    # Verify Node is scoured
    assert NodeSupervisor.get_node(readme_uuid) == {:error, :not_found}
    assert :mnesia.dirty_read(:nodes_table, readme_uuid) == []
  end

  defp wait_for_nodes(target_count, timeout) when timeout <= 0 do
    flunk("Timeout waiting for #{target_count} nodes.")
  end
  defp wait_for_nodes(target_count, timeout) do
    count = length(NodeSupervisor.get_all_active_node_pids())
    if count >= target_count do
      :ok
    else
      Process.sleep(500)
      wait_for_nodes(target_count, timeout - 500)
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: cdms_mixer_test.exs
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.SystemMixerTest do
  use ExUnit.Case
  require Logger
  alias CMS.NodeSupervisor
  alias CMS.Services.SystemMixer

  setup do
    # 1. Stop the application first so we can safely wipe data
    Application.stop(:acn_cms)

    # 2. Ensure Mnesia and VectorRouter are clean for the test
    data_dir = Application.get_env(:acn_cms, :data_root)
    File.rm(Path.join(data_dir, "running.pid"))
    File.rm_rf!("priv/data/mnesia_test")
    File.rm_rf!("priv/data/hnsw_indices")
    
    # 3. Start fresh
    Application.start(:acn_cms)
    
    tmp_dir = "/tmp/cdms_mixer_test_#{:rand.uniform(1000000)}"
    File.mkdir_p!(tmp_dir)
    
    on_exit fn ->
      File.rm_rf!(tmp_dir)
    end
    
    {:ok, tmp_dir: tmp_dir}
  end

  test "Two-Pass Genesis establishes syntactic and semantic bonds", %{tmp_dir: tmp_dir} do
    # 1. Create two files with semantic overlap and syntactic dependency
    # File A: The Dependency
    file_a = Path.join(tmp_dir, "auth_logic.ex")
    File.write!(file_a, """
    defmodule AuthLogic do
      @doc "This module handles login and authentication."
      def login(user), do: :ok
    end
    """)

    # File B: The Dependent (Semantic & Syntactic overlap)
    file_b = Path.join(tmp_dir, "user_controller.ex")
    File.write!(file_b, """
    defmodule UserController do
      alias AuthLogic
      @doc "Handles user requests and calls authentication login."
      def handle(conn, params) do
        AuthLogic.login(params["user"])
      end
    end
    """)

    # File C: Python Dependency (e.g. numpy.py)
    file_c = Path.join(tmp_dir, "numpy.py")
    File.write!(file_c, "# NumPy stub")
    
    # File D: Python Dependent
    file_d = Path.join(tmp_dir, "science_script.py")
    File.write!(file_d, "import numpy\n# Using numpy here")

    # 2. Run Pass One (Awakening) - Manually triggering Genesis logic
    Logger.info("Starting Pass One...")
    {:ok, _pid} = NodeSupervisor.start_directory_agent(tmp_dir, nil, {0.0, 0.0, 0}, "m0", [edge_generation: false, notify_pid: self()])
    
    receive do
      {:wavefront_complete, _} -> :ok
    after
      10000 -> flunk("Pass One timed out")
    end

    # Verify nodes exist but have NO syntactic/semantic edges yet
    # We use UUID5 like the agent does
    uuid_a = UUID.uuid5(:oid, file_a)
    uuid_b = UUID.uuid5(:oid, file_b)
    uuid_c = UUID.uuid5(:oid, file_c)
    uuid_d = UUID.uuid5(:oid, file_d)
    
    {:ok, _node_a} = NodeSupervisor.get_node(uuid_a)
    {:ok, node_b} = NodeSupervisor.get_node(uuid_b)
    
    assert Enum.empty?(node_b.tail.syntactic)
    assert Enum.empty?(node_b.tail.semantic)

    # 3. Run Pass Two (Binding)
    Logger.info("Starting Pass Two...")
    SystemMixer.bind_all()

    # 4. Final Verification
    # Reload nodes
    {:ok, node_b_after} = NodeSupervisor.get_node(uuid_b)
    {:ok, node_d_after} = NodeSupervisor.get_node(uuid_d)
    
    # Syntactic check: Should have an :imports edge to Node A
    # User controller aliases AuthLogic, and we registered "AuthLogic" to uuid_a
    assert Enum.any?(node_b_after.tail.syntactic, fn e -> e.target_node_id == uuid_a and e.type == :imports end)
    
    # Python Syntactic check (Via Stem Registration)
    assert Enum.any?(node_d_after.tail.syntactic, fn e -> e.target_node_id == uuid_c and e.type == :imports end)
    
    # Semantic check: Should have a :relates_to edge to Node A due to overlapping docstrings
    assert Enum.any?(node_b_after.tail.semantic, fn e -> e.target_node_id == uuid_a end)
  end
end
